 266:     private void renderChips(LinearLayout chips, String[] names, int active) {
 267:         for (int i = 0; i < chips.getChildCount() && i < names.length; i++) {
 268:             TextView tv = (TextView) chips.getChildAt(i);
 269:             boolean on = (i + 1) == active;
 270:             tv.setTextColor(on ? C_BG : C_FG3);
 271:             tv.setTypeface(on ? Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD : Typeface.DEFAULT);
 272:             GradientDrawable g = new GradientDrawable();
 273:             g.setCornerRadius(dp(7));
 274:             g.setColor(on ? C_TEAL : C_BG2);
 275:             tv.setBackground(g);
 276:         }
 277:     }
 278: 
 279:     // ── 下一题 ────────────────────────────────────────────
 280:     private void next() {
 281:         if (queue.isEmpty()) return;
 282:         if (idx >= queue.size()) {
 283:             renderSummary();
 284:             return;
 285:         }
 286:         answered = null;
 287:         picked = -1;
 288:         verdict = -1;
 289:         feedback = "";
 290: 
 291:         // 最多试 12 次：某个词可能五种题型都出不了（没例句又不可拼写）
 292:         cur = null;
 293:         for (int attempt = 0; attempt < 12 && cur == null; attempt++) {
 294:             EngTrain.W w = queue.get(idx % queue.size());
 295:             int mode = modes[rnd.nextInt(modes.length)];
 296:             cur = EngTrain.make(mode, w, queue, all, rnd);
 297:             if (cur == null) idx++;
 298:             if (idx >= queue.size()) break;
 299:         }
 300:         if (cur == null) {
 301:             // 这一轮里剩下的词都出不了题：直接结算，别让人卡住
 302:             renderSummary();
 303:             return;
 304:         }
 305:         renderQuestion();
 306:     }
 307: 
 308:     private void renderQuestion() {
 309:         cardBody.removeAllViews();
 310:         tvProgress.setText((idx + 1) + " / " + queue.size()
 311:                 + "　✓" + right + " ✗" + wrong);
 312: 
 313:         // 题干卡
 314:         LinearLayout card = new LinearLayout(act);
 315:         card.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
 316:         GradientDrawable g = new GradientDrawable();
 317:         g.setCornerRadius(dp(12));
 318:         g.setColor(C_BG2);
 319:         card.setBackground(g);
 320:         card.setPadding(dp(14), dp(14), dp(14), dp(16));
 321:         LinearLayout.LayoutParams lp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 322:                 ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT);
 323:         lp.bottomMargin = dp(10);
 324:         card.setLayoutParams(lp);
 325: 
 326:         TextView modeTag = new TextView(act);
 327:         modeTag.setText(EngTrain.modeName(cur.mode));
 328:         modeTag.setTextSize(11);
 329:         modeTag.setTextColor(C_TEAL);
 330:         modeTag.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 331:         card.addView(modeTag);
 332: 
 333:         TextView sub2 = new TextView(act);
 334:         sub2.setText(EngTrain.modeHint(cur.mode));
 335:         sub2.setTextSize(11);
 336:         sub2.setTextColor(C_FG3);
 337:         sub2.setPadding(0, dp(2), 0, dp(10));
 338:         card.addView(sub2);
 339: 
 340:         TextView prompt = new TextView(act);
 341:         prompt.setText(cur.prompt);
 342:         prompt.setTextSize(cur.mode == EngTrain.M_CLOZE ? 16f : 22f);
 343:         prompt.setTextColor(C_FG);
 344:         prompt.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 345:         prompt.setLineSpacing(dp(5), 1f);
 346:         card.addView(prompt);
 347: 
 348:         if (cur.sub != null && !cur.sub.isEmpty()) {
 349:             TextView s = new TextView(act);
 350:             s.setText(cur.sub);
 351:             // 词性题：英文是主体，中文释义只做小字提示。
 352:             // 原来 prompt 里 "apple  苹果" 整串用 22pt，中文和英文一样大，
 353:             // 视觉上中文成了主角 —— 用户直接指出来了。
 354:             s.setTextSize(cur.mode == EngTrain.M_POS ? 13f : 12.5f);
 355:             s.setTextColor(C_FG3);
 356:             s.setPadding(0, dp(6), 0, 0);
 357:             card.addView(s);
 358:         }
 359:         cardBody.addView(card);
 360: 
 361:         if (cur.isChoice()) renderChoices();
 362:         else renderInput();
 363: 
 364:         if (answered != null) renderFeedback();
 365:     }
 366: 
 367:     // ── 选择题 ────────────────────────────────────────────
 368:     private void renderChoices() {
 369:         for (int i = 0; i < cur.options.length; i++) {
 370:             final int k = i;
 371:             TextView b = new TextView(act);
 372:             String opt = cur.options[i];
 373:             // 词性题：光给 "art." 等于在考缩写，补上中文才是在考词性
 374:             if (cur.mode == EngTrain.M_POS) {
 375:                 String nm = EngTrain.posName(opt);
 376:                 if (!nm.isEmpty()) opt = opt + "　" + nm;
 377:             }
 378:             b.setText(((char) ('A' + i)) + "． " + opt);
 379:             b.setTextSize(15);
 380:             b.setTextColor(C_FG);
 381:             b.setPadding(dp(14), dp(13), dp(14), dp(13));
 382:             b.setLineSpacing(dp(3), 1f);
 383: 
 384:             GradientDrawable bg = new GradientDrawable();
 385:             bg.setCornerRadius(dp(10));
 386:             // 已作答：正确答案标绿，选错的标红
 387:             if (answered != null) {
 388:                 if (i == cur.answer) {
 389:                     bg.setColor(0xFF1E3A34);
 390:                     b.setTextColor(C_TEAL);
 391:                 } else if (i == picked) {
 392:                     bg.setColor(0xFF3A2020);
 393:                     b.setTextColor(C_RED);
 394:                 } else {
 395:                     bg.setColor(C_BG2);
 396:                     b.setTextColor(C_FG3);
 397:                 }
 398:             } else {
 399:                 bg.setColor(C_BG2);
 400:             }
 401:             b.setBackground(bg);
 402: 
 403:             LinearLayout.LayoutParams lp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 404:                     ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT);
 405:             lp.bottomMargin = dp(8);
 406:             b.setLayoutParams(lp);
 407: 
 408:             if (answered == null) {
 409:                 b.setOnClickListener(v -> {
 410:                     picked = k;
 411:                     verdict = EngTrain.judgeChoice(cur, k);
 412:                     answered = cur;
 413:                     if (verdict == EngTrain.J_RIGHT) right++;
 414:                     else {
 415:                         wrong++;
 416:                         recordWrong(cur);
 417:                     }
 418:                     feedback = "";
 419:                     renderQuestion();
 420:                 });
 421:             }
 422:             cardBody.addView(b);
 423:         }
 424:     }
 425: 
 426:     // ── 拼写 / 填空 ───────────────────────────────────────
 427:     private void renderInput() {
 428:         EditText et = new EditText(act);
 429:         et.setHint(cur.mode == EngTrain.M_SPELL ? "敲出这个单词" : "填这一空");
 430:         et.setHintTextColor(0xFF4E5565);
 431:         et.setTextColor(C_FG);
 432:         et.setTextSize(17);
 433:         et.setBackgroundColor(C_BG2);
 434:         et.setPadding(dp(14), dp(12), dp(14), dp(12));
 435:         et.setSingleLine(true);
 436:         // ── 关掉输入法的「自作聪明」 ────────────────────────
 437:         // 默认 TYPE_CLASS_TEXT 在两个地方会替学生改答案：
 438:         //   ① TYPE_TEXT_FLAG_CAP_SENTENCES 会自动把首字母大写；
 439:         //   ② 输入法的自动纠错/联想会悄悄换掉拼错的词。
 440:         // 拼写题要的是「学生自己写的那个词」，被输入法改过就不再是他的答案了。
 441:         // 顺带关掉全角输入：中文输入法开着时敲出来是全角字母（U+FF41 起），
 442:         // 屏幕上几乎看不出差别却必然判定不等 —— 归一化那边已经能兜住，
 443:         // 这里再从源头减少一种可能。
 444:         // 不用 VISIBLE_PASSWORD —— 有些输入法会因此把已输入的字母暂时遮成圆点。
 445:         // NO_SUGGESTIONS 关联想；CAP_CHARACTERS 保证不会被自动改成首字母大写。
 446:         et.setInputType(InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT
 447:                 | InputType.TYPE_TEXT_FLAG_NO_SUGGESTIONS
 448:                 | InputType.TYPE_TEXT_FLAG_CAP_CHARACTERS);
 449:         if (answered != null) {
 450:             // 回显玩家自己敲的东西，不是标准答案。
 451:             // 答对了就显示他写的（本来就等于答案）；答错了显示他错在哪，
 452:             // 再配合下面的「答案：xxx」对照，才知道差在哪个字母。
 453:             et.setText(typedText);
 454:             et.setEnabled(false);
 455:             et.setTextColor(verdict == EngTrain.J_RIGHT ? C_TEAL : C_RED);
 456:         }
 457:         cardBody.addView(et);
 458: 
 459:         if (answered == null) {
 460:             TextView go = new TextView(act);
 461:             go.setText("确认");
 462:             go.setTextSize(14);
 463:             go.setTextColor(C_BG);
 464:             go.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 465:             go.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
 466:             go.setPadding(0, dp(11), 0, dp(11));
 467:             GradientDrawable gg = new GradientDrawable();
 468:             gg.setCornerRadius(dp(10));
 469:             gg.setColor(C_TEAL);
 470:             go.setBackground(gg);
 471:             LinearLayout.LayoutParams lp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 472:                     ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT);
 473:             lp.topMargin = dp(10);
 474:             go.setLayoutParams(lp);
 475:             go.setOnClickListener(v -> {
 476:                 String in = et.getText().toString();
 477:                 if (EngTrain.norm(in).isEmpty()) return;
 478:                 typedText = in;                 // 原样留底，用于回显
 479:                 verdict = EngTrain.judgeText(cur, in);
 480:                 answered = cur;
 481:                 if (verdict == EngTrain.J_RIGHT) right++;
 482:                 else {
 483:                     wrong++;
 484:                     recordWrong(cur);
 485:                 }
 486:                 feedback = verdict == EngTrain.J_NEAR
 487:                         ? EngTrain.nearHint(cur.answerText, in) : "";
 488:                 renderQuestion();
 489:             });
 490:             cardBody.addView(go);
 491:         }
 492:     }
 493: 
 494:     // ── 反馈 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
 495:     private void renderFeedback() {
 496:         LinearLayout c = new LinearLayout(act);
 497:         c.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
 498:         GradientDrawable g = new GradientDrawable();
 499:         g.setCornerRadius(dp(12));
 500:         g.setColor(C_BG2);
 501:         c.setBackground(g);
 502:         c.setPadding(dp(14), dp(12), dp(14), dp(14));
 503:         LinearLayout.LayoutParams lp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 504:                 ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT);
 505:         lp.topMargin = dp(10);
 506:         lp.bottomMargin = dp(10);
 507:         c.setLayoutParams(lp);
 508: 
 509:         TextView head = new TextView(act);
 510:         if (verdict == EngTrain.J_RIGHT) {
 511:             head.setText("✓ 正确");
 512:             head.setTextColor(C_TEAL);
 513:         } else if (verdict == EngTrain.J_NEAR) {
 514:             head.setText("✗ 差一点。" + feedback);
 515:             head.setTextColor(C_AMBER);
 516:         } else {
 517:             head.setText("✗ 不对");
 518:             head.setTextColor(C_RED);
 519:         }
 520:         head.setTextSize(15);
 521:         head.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 522:         c.addView(head);
 523: 
 524:         TextView ans = new TextView(act);
 525:         String ansText = cur.answerText;
 526:         // 词性题的答案也补中文名，和选项口径一致
 527:         if (cur.mode == EngTrain.M_POS) {
 528:             String nm = EngTrain.posName(ansText);
 529:             if (!nm.isEmpty()) ansText = ansText + "（" + nm + "）";
 530:         }
 531:         ans.setText("答案：" + ansText
 532:                 + (cur.word != null && !cur.word.ph.isEmpty() ? "　/" + cur.word.ph + "/" : ""));
 533:         ans.setTextSize(14);
 534:         ans.setTextColor(C_FG);
 535:         ans.setPadding(0, dp(6), 0, 0);
 536:         c.addView(ans);
 537: 
 538:         if (cur.word != null && !cur.word.cn.isEmpty()) {
 539:             TextView cn = new TextView(act);
 540:             cn.setText("释义：" + cur.word.cn);
 541:             cn.setTextSize(13);
 542:             cn.setTextColor(C_FG2);
 543:             cn.setPadding(0, dp(3), 0, 0);
 544:             c.addView(cn);
 545:         }
 546:         // 完形填空 / 其它题型都补上原例句，把词放回语境里看一遍
 547:         if (cur.word != null && !cur.word.ex.isEmpty()) {
 548:             TextView ex = new TextView(act);
 549:             ex.setText("例句：" + cur.word.ex);
 550:             ex.setTextSize(12.5f);
 551:             ex.setTextColor(C_FG3);
 552:             ex.setPadding(0, dp(5), 0, 0);
 553:             ex.setLineSpacing(dp(3), 1f);
 554:             c.addView(ex);
 555:             if (!cur.word.exCn.isEmpty()) {
 556:                 TextView exCn = new TextView(act);
 557:                 exCn.setText(cur.word.exCn);
 558:                 exCn.setTextSize(12);
 559:                 exCn.setTextColor(C_FG3);
 560:                 c.addView(exCn);
 561:             }
 562:         }
 563:         cardBody.addView(c);
 564: 
 565:         TextView nxt = new TextView(act);
 566:         nxt.setText(idx + 1 >= queue.size() ? "看总结" : "下一题 ›");
 567:         nxt.setTextSize(14);
 568:         nxt.setTextColor(C_BG);
 569:         nxt.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 570:         nxt.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
 571:         nxt.setPadding(0, dp(12), 0, dp(12));
 572:         GradientDrawable gg = new GradientDrawable();
 573:         gg.setCornerRadius(dp(10));
 574:         gg.setColor(C_TEAL);
 575:         nxt.setBackground(gg);
 576:         nxt.setOnClickListener(v -> {
 577:             idx++;
 578:             next();
 579:         });
 580:         cardBody.addView(nxt);
 581:     }
 582: 
 583:     // ── 结算 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
 584:     private void renderSummary() {
 585:         cardBody.removeAllViews();
 586:         tvProgress.setText("已完成");
 587: 
 588:         LinearLayout c = new LinearLayout(act);
 589:         c.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
 590:         GradientDrawable g = new GradientDrawable();
 591:         g.setCornerRadius(dp(12));
 592:         g.setColor(C_BG2);
 593:         c.setBackground(g);
 594:         c.setPadding(dp(16), dp(16), dp(16), dp(16));
 595:         c.setLayoutParams(new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 596:                 ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT));
 597: 
 598:         // 落盘。以前 right/wrong 是两个内存变量，退出就归零，
 599:         // 用户连「这周做了多少题」都看不到 —— 排行榜要的数据以前不存在。
 600:         saveRound();
 601: 
 602:         TextView t = new TextView(act);
 603:         int total = right + wrong;
 604:         t.setText("这一轮做完了");
 605:         t.setTextSize(18);
 606:         t.setTextColor(C_FG);
 607:         t.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 608:         c.addView(t);
 609: 
 610:         TextView s = new TextView(act);
 611:         s.setText("答对 " + right + " 题，答错 " + wrong + " 题"
 612:                 + (total > 0 ? "\n正确率 " + (right * 100 / total) + "%" : ""));
 613:         s.setTextSize(14);
 614:         s.setTextColor(C_FG2);
 615:         s.setPadding(0, dp(8), 0, 0);
 616:         s.setLineSpacing(dp(5), 1f);
 617:         c.addView(s);
 618: 
 619:         if (total > 0 && right * 100 / Math.max(1, total) < 70) {
 620:             TextView tip = new TextView(act);
 621:             tip.setText("低于 70%。建议先回单词卡把这一批词过一遍，再回来做。");
 622:             tip.setTextSize(12.5f);
 623:             tip.setTextColor(C_AMBER);
 624:             tip.setPadding(0, dp(8), 0, 0);
 625:             tip.setLineSpacing(dp(3), 1f);
 626:             c.addView(tip);
 627:         }
 628:         cardBody.addView(c);
 629: 
 630:         TextView again = new TextView(act);
 631:         again.setText("再来一轮");
 632:         again.setTextSize(14);
 633:         again.setTextColor(C_BG);
 634:         again.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 635:         again.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
 636:         again.setPadding(0, dp(12), 0, dp(12));
 637:         GradientDrawable gg = new GradientDrawable();
 638:         gg.setCornerRadius(dp(10));
 639:         gg.setColor(C_TEAL);
 640:         again.setBackground(gg);
 641:         LinearLayout.LayoutParams lp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 642:                 ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT);
 643:         lp.topMargin = dp(12);
 644:         again.setLayoutParams(lp);
 645:         again.setOnClickListener(v -> {
 646:             idx = 0;
 647:             right = 0;
 648:             wrong = 0;
 649:             // 新一轮必须重置计时和指纹，否则第二轮会继承第一轮的用时
 650:             roundStart = System.currentTimeMillis();
 651:             roundRev = "";
 652:             next();
 653:         });
 654:         cardBody.addView(again);
 655:     }
 656: 
 657:     /**
 658:      * 一轮结束落盘。
 659:      *
 660:      * 之前 right / wrong 是纯内存变量，退出界面就归零，一行都没存过。
 661:      * 排行榜要的数据从这一刻起才有。
 662:      *
 663:      * 幂等：roundRev 由「起始时间+题量」算，同一轮写多次仍是同一条，
 664:      * Store 那边 rev 上有 UNIQUE 约束兜底。
 665:      * 题量为 0 的一轮不记 —— 那是打开看一眼就退出的，不该拉低正确率。
 666:      */
 667:     private void saveRound() {
 668:         if (store == null) return;
 669:         int total = right + wrong;
 670:         if (total <= 0) return;
 671:         if (roundRev.isEmpty()) {
 672:             roundRev = Long.toHexString(roundStart) + "-" + total + "-" + right;
 673:         }
 674:         try {
 675:             Store.EngRound r = new Store.EngRound();
 676:             r.day = StateLogic.todayIso();
 677:             r.mode = modes.length > 0 ? modes[0] : 0;   // 记录本轮起手题型
 678:             r.right = right;
 679:             r.wrong = wrong;
 680:             r.ms = Math.max(0, System.currentTimeMillis() - roundStart);
 681:             r.started = roundStart;
 682:             r.rev = roundRev;
 683:             store.addEngRound(r);
 684:         } catch (Exception ignored) {
 685:             // 存不上不能影响用户继续做题
 686:         }
 687:         uploadPending();
 688:     }
 689: 
 690:     /**
 691:      * 把没传上去的成绩补传给服务器。
 692:      *
 693:      * 为什么要「补传」而不是「当场传完就算了」：
 694:      *   同学做题的地方经常没网（教室、地铁），当场传必失败。
 695:      *   所以成绩先进本地 eng_round 表（up=0），
 696:      *   每次出成绩单的时候顺手把积压的都试一遍 ——
 697:      *   幂等键是 rev，重复传不会在榜上算两次。
 698:      *
 699:      * 全程后台线程，失败静默：上传失败不该弹窗打断做题的人。
 700:      */
 701:     private void uploadPending() {
 702:         if (store == null) return;
 703:         final Account acc = Account.load(act);
 704:         if (!acc.loggedIn()) return;
 705:         final java.util.List<Store.EngRound> pend = store.pendingEngRounds();
 706:         if (pend.isEmpty()) return;
 707: 
 708:         new Thread(() -> {
 709:             int sent = 0;
 710:             for (Store.EngRound r : pend) {
 711:                 Api.R res = Api.postRound(acc.token, r.right, r.wrong, r.ms, r.started, r.rev);
 712:                 // 只有服务器明确收下才算传过。
 713:                 // ignored=true（用时太短/超上限）也算「服务器已处理」，
 714:                 // 再传一次结果还是一样，不该无限重试。
 715:                 if (res.ok || (res.body != null && res.body.optBoolean("ignored"))) {
 716:                     store.markEngUploaded(r.rev);
 717:                     sent++;
 718:                 } else {
 719:                     break;   // 网络断了就别硬刷，下次再说
 720:                 }
 721:             }
 722:             final int n = sent;
 723:             if (n > 0) act.runOnUiThread(() ->
 724:                     android.widget.Toast.makeText(act, "已上传 " + n + " 轮成绩",
 725:                             android.widget.Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show());
 726:         }).start();
 727:     }
 728: 
 729:     // ── 错题入库 ──────────────────────────────────────────
 730:     /** 错题进错题库，key 用 "eng:<单词>"，和题库的 id 不会撞。 */
 731:     private void recordWrong(EngTrain.Item it) {
 732:         if (it == null || it.word == null) return;
 733:         if (store == null) return;
 734:         try {
 735:             store.addWrong("eng:" + it.word.w, "eng", it.word.w,
 736:                     EngTrain.modeName(it.mode) + "｜" + it.word.cn);
 737:         } catch (Exception ignored) {
 738:         }
 739:     }
 740: 
 741:     private void toast(String s) {
 742:         android.widget.Toast.makeText(act, s, android.widget.Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
 743:     }
 744: }
===== FILE: E:\小黑盒运营\cq-zhuanshengbens\mobile\src\com\zsb\plan\Expr.java =====
   1: package com.zsb.plan;
   2: 
   3: import java.util.ArrayList;
   4: import java.util.List;
   5: 
   6: /**
   7:  * 表达式引擎：把 "sin(x^2)" 这样的文本解析成语法树，然后
   8:  *   ① 数值求值（画图用）
   9:  *   ② 符号求导（求导结果用，不是数值近似）
  10:  *   ③ 规范化输出（求导结果要人能看懂）
  11:  *
  12:  * 为什么单独写一个：这些逻辑必须能在电脑上跑，才能被审计。
  13:  * 纯 Java，零 Android 依赖——verify.py 会拿它做数值对照审计。
  14:  *
  15:  * 输入语法（比手写数学宽松）：
  16:  *   数字 3、3.5、1E3        变量 x
  17:  *   常数 pi、e              运算 + - * / ^（^ 右结合，-x^2 = -(x^2)）
  18:  *   函数 sin cos tan cot sec csc asin acos atan
  19:  *        sinh cosh tanh ln lg log10 log2 exp sqrt abs sign floor ceil
  20:  *        pow(a,b) min(a,b) max(a,b)
  21:  *   隐式乘法：2x、3sin(x)、(x+1)(x-1)、x(x+1)、2pi 都按乘法算
  22:  *   全角括号、×、÷、−、· 会自动换成半角（手机输入法常打出这些）
  23:  *
  24:  * 约定：数学上无定义的地方一律返回 NaN（例如 ln(-1)、asin(2)），
  25:  * 除以 0 返回 ±Infinity。画图端据此断开曲线，不连线。
  26:  */
  27: public class Expr {
  28: 
  29:     public static final int T_NUM = 1;
  30:     public static final int T_VAR = 2;
  31:     public static final int T_ADD = 3;
  32:     public static final int T_SUB = 4;
  33:     public static final int T_MUL = 5;
  34:     public static final int T_DIV = 6;
  35:     public static final int T_POW = 7;
  36:     public static final int T_NEG = 8;
  37:     public static final int T_FUNC = 9;
  38: 
  39:     /** 解析/求值错误。文案直接给用户看，所以写中文。 */
  40:     public static class ExprError extends RuntimeException {
  41:         public ExprError(String m) { super(m); }
  42:     }
  43: 
  44:     public static class Node {
  45:         public final int t;
  46:         public double v;        // T_NUM
  47:         public String fn;       // T_FUNC
  48:         public Node a, b;
  49:         Node(int t) { this.t = t; }
  50:     }
  51: 
  52:     // ── 构造 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  53:     public static Node num(double v) { Node n = new Node(T_NUM); n.v = v; return n; }
  54:     public static Node var() { return new Node(T_VAR); }
  55:     public static Node bin(int t, Node a, Node b) { Node n = new Node(t); n.a = a; n.b = b; return n; }
  56:     public static Node neg(Node a) { Node n = new Node(T_NEG); n.a = a; return n; }
  57:     public static Node fn(String name, Node a) { Node n = new Node(T_FUNC); n.fn = name; n.a = a; return n; }
  58: 
  59:     // ── 入口：解析 ────────────────────────────────────────
  60:     public static Node parse(String src) {
  61:         if (src == null) throw new ExprError("表达式为空");
  62:         Parser p = new Parser(normalize(src));
  63:         Node n = p.parseSum();
  64:         p.skipSpace();
  65:         if (!p.eof()) throw new ExprError("第 " + (p.i + 1) + " 个字符看不懂：" + p.rest());
  66:         return n;
  67:     }
  68: 
  69:     /** 全角/数学符号统一成 ASCII，去掉空白。 */
  70:     public static String normalize(String s) {
  71:         StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder(s.length());
  72:         for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
  73:             char c = s.charAt(i);
  74:             switch (c) {
  75:                 case '（': b.append('('); break;
  76:                 case '）': b.append(')'); break;
  77:                 case '，': b.append(','); break;
  78:                 case '×': case '·': case '⋅': case '*': b.append('*'); break;
  79:                 case '÷': b.append('/'); break;
  80:                 case '−': case '–': case '—': b.append('-'); break;
  81:                 case '＋': b.append('+'); break;
  82:                 case '５': case '３': case '２': case '１': case '４':
  83:                 case '６': case '７': case '８': case '９': case '０':
  84:                     b.append((char) ('0' + (c - '０'))); break;
  85:                 case '²': b.append("^2"); break;
  86:                 case '³': b.append("^3"); break;
  87:                 case 'π': b.append("pi"); break;
  88:                 case '　': break;
  89:                 case ' ': case '\t': case '\n': case '\r': break;
  90:                 default: b.append(c);
  91:             }
  92:         }
  93:         return b.toString();
  94:     }
  95: 
  96:     // ── 递归下降解析 ──────────────────────────────────────
  97:     static class Parser {
  98:         final String s;
  99:         int i;
 100: 
 101:         Parser(String s) { this.s = s; }
 102: 
 103:         boolean eof() { return i >= s.length(); }
 104:         char peek() { return i < s.length() ? s.charAt(i) : '\0'; }
 105:         char peek2() { return i + 1 < s.length() ? s.charAt(i + 1) : '\0'; }
 106:         void skipSpace() { while (i < s.length() && s.charAt(i) == ' ') i++; }
 107:         String rest() { return s.substring(i, Math.min(s.length(), i + 8)); }
 108: 
 109:         Node parseSum() {
 110:             Node n = parseTerm();
 111:             while (true) {
 112:                 skipSpace();
 113:                 char c = peek();
 114:                 if (c == '+') { i++; n = bin(T_ADD, n, parseTerm()); }
 115:                 else if (c == '-') { i++; n = bin(T_SUB, n, parseTerm()); }
 116:                 else return n;
 117:             }
 118:         }
 119: 
 120:         Node parseTerm() {
 121:             Node n = parseUnary();
 122:             while (true) {
 123:                 skipSpace();
 124:                 char c = peek();
 125:                 if (c == '*') { i++; n = bin(T_MUL, n, parseUnary()); continue; }
 126:                 if (c == '/') { i++; n = bin(T_DIV, n, parseUnary()); continue; }
 127:                 // 隐式乘法：后面紧跟一个“原子的开头”就乘
 128:                 if (startsPrimary(c)) { n = bin(T_MUL, n, parseUnary()); continue; }
 129:                 return n;
 130:             }
 131:         }
 132: 
 133:         boolean startsPrimary(char c) {
 134:             return (c >= '0' && c <= '9') || c == '.' || c == '(' || isAlpha(c);
 135:         }
 136: 
 137:         static boolean isAlpha(char c) { return (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z'); }
 138: 
 139:         Node parseUnary() {
 140:             skipSpace();
 141:             if (peek() == '-') { i++; return neg(parseUnary()); }
 142:             if (peek() == '+') { i++; return parseUnary(); }
 143:             return parsePower();
 144:         }
 145: 
 146:         Node parsePower() {
 147:             Node base = parsePrimary();
 148:             skipSpace();
 149:             if (peek() == '^') {
 150:                 i++;
 151:                 // 右结合：x^2^3 = x^(2^3)；指数允许带负号
 152:                 Node exp = parseUnary();
 153:                 return bin(T_POW, base, exp);
 154:             }
 155:             return base;
 156:         }
 157: 
 158:         Node parsePrimary() {
 159:             skipSpace();
 160:             if (eof()) throw new ExprError("表达式不完整");
 161:             char c = peek();
 162:             if (c == '(') {
 163:                 i++;
 164:                 Node n = parseSum();
 165:                 skipSpace();
 166:                 if (peek() != ')') throw new ExprError("括号没有配对");
 167:                 i++;
 168:                 return n;
 169:             }
 170:             if ((c >= '0' && c <= '9') || c == '.') return parseNumber();
 171:             if (isAlpha(c)) return parseName();
 172:             throw new ExprError("第 " + (i + 1) + " 个字符看不懂：" + c);
 173:         }
 174: 
 175:         Node parseNumber() {
 176:             int st = i;
 177:             while (i < s.length() && ((s.charAt(i) >= '0' && s.charAt(i) <= '9') || s.charAt(i) == '.')) i++;
 178:             // 大写 E 表示科学计数：1E3 = 1000。小写 e 是自然常数，避免 2e5 歧义
 179:             if (i < s.length() && s.charAt(i) == 'E') {
 180:                 int save = i;
 181:                 i++;
 182:                 if (i < s.length() && (s.charAt(i) == '+' || s.charAt(i) == '-')) i++;
 183:                 if (i < s.length() && s.charAt(i) >= '0' && s.charAt(i) <= '9') {
 184:                     while (i < s.length() && s.charAt(i) >= '0' && s.charAt(i) <= '9') i++;
 185:                 } else {
 186:                     i = save;   // 不是合法指数，退回
 187:                 }
 188:             }
 189:             String t = s.substring(st, i);
 190:             try {
 191:                 return num(Double.parseDouble(t));
 192:             } catch (Exception e) {
 193:                 throw new ExprError("数字写错了：" + t);
 194:             }
 195:         }
 196: 
 197:         /**
 198:          * 名字识别用「最长已知前缀」，不是贪婪吃光整串。
 199:          * 否则 xsin(x) 会被当成一个叫 xsin 的函数；学生手写时这种连写极常见。
 200:          * 反例必须保留：exp 不能被拆成 e·xp。
 201:          */
 202:         Node parseName() {
 203:             int st = i;
 204:             while (i < s.length() && (isAlpha(s.charAt(i)) || (s.charAt(i) >= '0' && s.charAt(i) <= '9'))) i++;
 205:             String run = s.substring(st, i);
 206: 
 207:             String name = null;
 208:             for (int len = run.length(); len >= 1; len--) {
 209:                 String cand = run.substring(0, len);
 210:                 if (isKnownName(cand)) { name = cand; break; }
 211:             }
 212:             if (name == null) throw new ExprError("不认识的名字：" + run + "（变量只有 x）");
 213:             i = st + name.length();
 214: 
 215:             if ("pi".equals(name)) return num(Math.PI);
 216:             if ("e".equals(name)) return num(Math.E);
 217:             if ("x".equals(name)) return var();
 218: 
 219:             skipSpace();
 220:             if (peek() != '(') throw new ExprError(name + " 后面要跟括号，例如 " + name + "(x)");
 221:             i++;
 222:             Node a1 = parseSum();
 223:             Node a2 = null;
 224:             skipSpace();
 225:             if (peek() == ',') {
 226:                 i++;
 227:                 a2 = parseSum();
 228:                 skipSpace();
 229:             }
 230:             if (peek() != ')') throw new ExprError(name + " 的括号没有配对");
 231:             i++;
 232:             if (a2 != null) {
 233:                 if ("pow".equals(name)) return bin(T_POW, a1, a2);
 234:                 Node n = new Node(T_FUNC);
 235:                 n.fn = name;
 236:                 n.a = a1;
 237:                 n.b = a2;
 238:                 return n;
 239:             }
 240:             Node n = new Node(T_FUNC);
 241:             n.fn = name;
 242:             n.a = a1;
 243:             return n;
 244:         }
 245:     }
 246: 
 247:     static boolean isKnownName(String n) {
 248:         return "x".equals(n) || "pi".equals(n) || "e".equals(n) || isFunc(n);
 249:     }
 250: 
 251:     public static boolean isFunc(String name) {
 252:         return "sin".equals(name) || "cos".equals(name) || "tan".equals(name)
 253:                 || "cot".equals(name) || "sec".equals(name) || "csc".equals(name)
 254:                 || "asin".equals(name) || "acos".equals(name) || "atan".equals(name)
 255:                 || "sinh".equals(name) || "cosh".equals(name) || "tanh".equals(name)
 256:                 || "ln".equals(name) || "lg".equals(name) || "log10".equals(name)
 257:                 || "log2".equals(name) || "exp".equals(name) || "sqrt".equals(name)
 258:                 || "abs".equals(name) || "sign".equals(name) || "floor".equals(name)
 259:                 || "ceil".equals(name) || "pow".equals(name) || "min".equals(name)
 260:                 || "max".equals(name);
 261:     }
 262: 
 263:     // ── 求值 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
 264:     public static double eval(Node n, double x) {
 265:         if (n == null) return Double.NaN;
 266:         switch (n.t) {
 267:             case T_NUM: return n.v;
 268:             case T_VAR: return x;
 269:             case T_ADD: return eval(n.a, x) + eval(n.b, x);
 270:             case T_SUB: return eval(n.a, x) - eval(n.b, x);
 271:             case T_MUL: return eval(n.a, x) * eval(n.b, x);
 272:             case T_DIV: return eval(n.a, x) / eval(n.b, x);
 273:             case T_POW: return Math.pow(eval(n.a, x), eval(n.b, x));
 274:             case T_NEG: return -eval(n.a, x);
 275:             default: {
 276:                 double u = eval(n.a, x);
 277:                 double w = n.b == null ? Double.NaN : eval(n.b, x);
 278:                 return callFn(n.fn, u, w);
 279:             }
 280:         }
 281:     }
 282: 
 283:     static double callFn(String f, double u, double w) {
 284:         if ("sin".equals(f)) return Math.sin(u);
 285:         if ("cos".equals(f)) return Math.cos(u);
 286:         if ("tan".equals(f)) return Math.tan(u);
 287:         if ("cot".equals(f)) return 1.0 / Math.tan(u);
 288:         if ("sec".equals(f)) return 1.0 / Math.cos(u);
 289:         if ("csc".equals(f)) return 1.0 / Math.sin(u);
 290:         if ("asin".equals(f)) return (u > 1 || u < -1) ? Double.NaN : Math.asin(u);
 291:         if ("acos".equals(f)) return (u > 1 || u < -1) ? Double.NaN : Math.acos(u);
 292:         if ("atan".equals(f)) return Math.atan(u);
 293:         if ("sinh".equals(f)) return Math.sinh(u);
 294:         if ("cosh".equals(f)) return Math.cosh(u);
 295:         if ("tanh".equals(f)) return Math.tanh(u);
 296:         if ("ln".equals(f)) return u > 0 ? Math.log(u) : Double.NaN;
 297:         if ("lg".equals(f) || "log10".equals(f)) return u > 0 ? Math.log10(u) : Double.NaN;
 298:         if ("log2".equals(f)) return u > 0 ? Math.log(u) / Math.log(2) : Double.NaN;
 299:         if ("exp".equals(f)) return Math.exp(u);
 300:         if ("sqrt".equals(f)) return u >= 0 ? Math.sqrt(u) : Double.NaN;
 301:         if ("abs".equals(f)) return Math.abs(u);
 302:         if ("sign".equals(f)) return u > 0 ? 1 : (u < 0 ? -1 : 0);
 303:         if ("floor".equals(f)) return Math.floor(u);
 304:         if ("ceil".equals(f)) return Math.ceil(u);
 305:         if ("min".equals(f)) return Math.min(u, w);
 306:         if ("max".equals(f)) return Math.max(u, w);
 307:         return Double.NaN;
 308:     }
 309: 
 310:     // ── 符号求导 ──────────────────────────────────────────
 311:     /**
 312:      * 对 x 求一阶导。返回的树已经化简（去 0/1、合并常数、常数提前）。
 313:      * 这是真符号求导，不是数值差分——所以它显示的是公式，不是近似值。
 314:      * 数值正确性由 verify.py 的 MathTest 用中心差分对照审计（24 组公式）。
 315:      */
 316:     public static Node diff(Node n) {
 317:         return simplify(d0(n));
 318:     }
 319: 
 320:     static Node d0(Node n) {
 321:         if (n == null) return num(0);
 322:         switch (n.t) {
 323:             case T_NUM: return num(0);
 324:             case T_VAR: return num(1);
 325:             case T_ADD: return bin(T_ADD, d0(n.a), d0(n.b));
 326:             case T_SUB: return bin(T_SUB, d0(n.a), d0(n.b));
 327:             case T_NEG: return neg(d0(n.a));
 328:             case T_MUL:
 329:                 return bin(T_ADD,
 330:                         bin(T_MUL, d0(n.a), n.b),
 331:                         bin(T_MUL, n.a, d0(n.b)));
 332:             case T_DIV: {
 333:                 // (u/v)' = (u'v - uv') / v^2
 334:                 Node nume = bin(T_SUB, bin(T_MUL, d0(n.a), n.b), bin(T_MUL, n.a, d0(n.b)));
 335:                 return bin(T_DIV, nume, bin(T_POW, n.b, num(2)));
 336:             }
 337:             case T_POW: {
 338:                 boolean baseConst = isConst(n.a);
 339:                 boolean expConst = isConst(n.b);
 340:                 if (expConst) {
 341:                     // (u^c)' = c·u^(c-1)·u'
 342:                     Node coef = n.b;
 343:                     Node p = bin(T_POW, n.a, bin(T_SUB, n.b, num(1)));
 344:                     return bin(T_MUL, bin(T_MUL, coef, p), d0(n.a));
 345:                 }
 346:                 if (baseConst) {
 347:                     // (a^v)' = a^v·ln a·v'
 348:                     return bin(T_MUL,
 349:                             bin(T_MUL, n, fn("ln", n.a)),
 350:                             d0(n.b));
 351:                 }
 352:                 // 一般幂指函数：u^v = e^(v ln u)
 353:                 // (u^v)' = u^v·(v'·ln u + v·u'/u)
 354:                 Node inner = bin(T_ADD,
 355:                         bin(T_MUL, d0(n.b), fn("ln", n.a)),
 356:                         bin(T_DIV, bin(T_MUL, n.b, d0(n.a)), n.a));
 357:                 return bin(T_MUL, n, inner);
 358:             }
 359:             default: {
 360:                 String f = n.fn;
 361:                 Node u = n.a;
 362:                 Node du = d0(u);
 363:                 if ("sin".equals(f)) return bin(T_MUL, fn("cos", u), du);
 364:                 if ("cos".equals(f)) return bin(T_MUL, neg(fn("sin", u)), du);
 365:                 if ("tan".equals(f))
 366:                     return bin(T_DIV, du, bin(T_POW, fn("cos", u), num(2)));
 367:                 if ("cot".equals(f))
 368:                     return neg(bin(T_DIV, du, bin(T_POW, fn("sin", u), num(2))));
 369:                 if ("sec".equals(f)) return bin(T_MUL, bin(T_MUL, fn("sec", u), fn("tan", u)), du);
 370:                 if ("csc".equals(f)) return neg(bin(T_MUL, bin(T_MUL, fn("csc", u), fn("cot", u)), du));
 371:                 if ("asin".equals(f))
 372:                     return bin(T_DIV, du, fn("sqrt", bin(T_SUB, num(1), bin(T_POW, u, num(2)))));
 373:                 if ("acos".equals(f))
 374:                     return neg(bin(T_DIV, du, fn("sqrt", bin(T_SUB, num(1), bin(T_POW, u, num(2))))));
 375:                 if ("atan".equals(f))
 376:                     return bin(T_DIV, du, bin(T_ADD, num(1), bin(T_POW, u, num(2))));
 377:                 if ("sinh".equals(f)) return bin(T_MUL, fn("cosh", u), du);
 378:                 if ("cosh".equals(f)) return bin(T_MUL, fn("sinh", u), du);
 379:                 if ("tanh".equals(f))
 380:                     return bin(T_DIV, du, bin(T_POW, fn("cosh", u), num(2)));
 381:                 if ("ln".equals(f)) return bin(T_DIV, du, u);
 382:                 if ("lg".equals(f) || "log10".equals(f))
 383:                     return bin(T_DIV, du, bin(T_MUL, u, fn("ln", num(10))));
 384:                 if ("log2".equals(f))
 385:                     return bin(T_DIV, du, bin(T_MUL, u, fn("ln", num(2))));
 386:                 if ("exp".equals(f)) return bin(T_MUL, fn("exp", u), du);
 387:                 if ("sqrt".equals(f))
 388:                     return bin(T_DIV, du, bin(T_MUL, num(2), fn("sqrt", u)));
 389:                 if ("abs".equals(f)) return bin(T_MUL, fn("sign", u), du);
 390:                 if ("sign".equals(f) || "floor".equals(f) || "ceil".equals(f)) return num(0);
 391:                 if ("min".equals(f) || "max".equals(f)) return num(Double.NaN);
 392:                 throw new ExprError("这个函数不支持求导：" + f);
 393:             }
 394:         }
 395:     }
 396: 
 397:     /** 是否与 x 无关（子树里没有 T_VAR）。 */
 398:     public static boolean isConst(Node n) {
 399:         if (n == null) return true;
 400:         if (n.t == T_VAR) return false;
 401:         return isConst(n.a) && isConst(n.b);
 402:     }
 403: 
 404:     // ── 化简 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
 405:     public static Node simplify(Node n) {
 406:         if (n == null) return num(0);
 407:         switch (n.t) {
 408:             case T_NUM:
 409:             case T_VAR:
 410:                 return n;
 411:             case T_NEG: {
 412:                 Node a = simplify(n.a);
 413:                 if (a.t == T_NUM) return num(-a.v);
 414:                 if (a.t == T_NEG) return a.a;
 415:                 return neg(a);
 416:             }
 417:             case T_ADD: {
 418:                 Node a = simplify(n.a), b = simplify(n.b);
 419:                 if (isZero(a)) return b;
 420:                 if (isZero(b)) return a;
 421:                 if (a.t == T_NUM && b.t == T_NUM) return num(a.v + b.v);
 422:                 return bin(T_ADD, a, b);
 423:             }
 424:             case T_SUB: {
 425:                 Node a = simplify(n.a), b = simplify(n.b);
 426:                 if (isZero(b)) return a;
 427:                 if (isZero(a)) return neg(b);
 428:                 if (a.t == T_NUM && b.t == T_NUM) return num(a.v - b.v);
 429:                 if (same(a, b)) return num(0);
 430:                 return bin(T_SUB, a, b);
 431:             }
 432:             case T_MUL: return simplifyMul(n);
 433:             case T_DIV: {
 434:                 Node a = simplify(n.a), b = simplify(n.b);
 435:                 if (isZero(a)) return num(0);
 436:                 if (isOne(b)) return a;
 437:                 if (same(a, b)) return num(1);
 438:                 if (a.t == T_NUM && b.t == T_NUM && b.v != 0) {
 439:                     double q = a.v / b.v;
 440:                     if (Math.rint(q) == q && Math.abs(q) < 1e9) return num(q);
 441:                 }
 442:                 // 负号提到分式外：(-a)/b = -(a/b)
 443:                 if (a.t == T_NEG) return neg(bin(T_DIV, a.a, b));
 444:                 return bin(T_DIV, a, b);
 445:             }
 446:             case T_POW: {
 447:                 Node a = simplify(n.a), b = simplify(n.b);
 448:                 if (isZero(b)) return num(1);
 449:                 if (isOne(b)) return a;
 450:                 if (isZero(a)) return num(0);
 451:                 if (isOne(a)) return num(1);
 452:                 if (a.t == T_NUM && b.t == T_NUM && Math.rint(b.v) == b.v
 453:                         && Math.abs(b.v) <= 12 && b.v >= 0) {
 454:                     return num(Math.pow(a.v, b.v));
 455:                 }
 456:                 return bin(T_POW, a, b);
 457:             }
 458:             default: {
 459:                 Node a = simplify(n.a);
 460:                 Node b = n.b == null ? null : simplify(n.b);
 461:                 if (a.t == T_NUM && (b == null || b.t == T_NUM) && !Double.isNaN(a.v)) {
 462:                     double r = callFn(n.fn, a.v, b == null ? Double.NaN : b.v);
 463:                     if (!Double.isNaN(r)) return num(r);
 464:                 }
 465:                 Node out = new Node(T_FUNC);
 466:                 out.fn = n.fn;
 467:                 out.a = a;
 468:                 out.b = b;
 469:                 return out;
 470:             }
 471:         }
 472:     }
 473: 
 474:     static Node simplifyMul(Node n) {
 475:         // 把乘法摊平成因子表，合并常数、去掉 1、把常数排到最前
 476:         List<Node> fs = new ArrayList<Node>();
 477:         flattenMul(simplify(n.a), fs);
 478:         flattenMul(simplify(n.b), fs);
 479:         double c = 1;
 480:         List<Node> keep = new ArrayList<Node>();
 481:         for (int k = 0; k < fs.size(); k++) {
 482:             Node f = fs.get(k);
 483:             if (f.t == T_NUM) { c *= f.v; continue; }
 484:             if (isOne(f)) continue;
 485:             keep.add(f);
 486:         }
 487:         if (c == 0) return num(0);
 488:         if (keep.isEmpty()) return num(c);
 489:         Node out;
 490:         if (c == 1) {
 491:             out = keep.get(0);
 492:         } else if (c == -1) {
 493:             out = neg(keep.get(0));
 494:         } else {
 495:             out = bin(T_MUL, num(c), keep.get(0));
 496:         }
 497:         for (int k = 1; k < keep.size(); k++) out = bin(T_MUL, out, keep.get(k));
 498:         return out;
 499:     }
 500: 
 501:     static void flattenMul(Node n, List<Node> out) {
 502:         if (n != null && n.t == T_MUL) { flattenMul(n.a, out); flattenMul(n.b, out); }
 503:         else if (n != null) out.add(n);
 504:     }
 505: 
 506:     static boolean isZero(Node n) { return n != null && n.t == T_NUM && n.v == 0; }
 507:     static boolean isOne(Node n) { return n != null && n.t == T_NUM && n.v == 1; }
 508: 
 509:     static boolean same(Node a, Node b) {
 510:         if (a == null || b == null) return a == b;
 511:         if (a.t != b.t) return false;
 512:         if (a.t == T_NUM) return a.v == b.v;
 513:         if (a.t == T_VAR) return true;
 514:         if (a.t == T_FUNC) {
 515:             if (!a.fn.equals(b.fn)) return false;
 516:         }
 517:         return same(a.a, b.a) && same(a.b, b.b);
 518:     }
 519: 
 520:     // ── 输出成可读文本 ────────────────────────────────────
 521:     /**
 522:      * 输出刻意保持“能再喂回输入框”的语法：乘号用 ·，幂用 ^，
 523:      * 这样用户看到 d/dx 结果可以直接复制去画图。
 524:      */
 525:     public static String toText(Node n) {
 526:         return text(n, 0);
 527:     }
 528: 
 529:     static final String[] SUP = {"⁰", "¹", "²", "³", "⁴", "⁵", "⁶", "⁷", "⁸", "⁹"};
 530: 
 531:     static String sup(long v) {
 532:         if (v < 0 || v > 9) return null;
 533:         return SUP[(int) v];
 534:     }
 535: 
 536:     static String text(Node n, int prec) {
 537:         if (n == null) return "0";
 538:         switch (n.t) {
 539:             case T_NUM: return fmtNum(n.v);
 540:             case T_VAR: return "x";
 541:             case T_NEG: {
 542:                 String s = "-" + text(n.a, 3);
 543:                 return prec > 2 ? "(" + s + ")" : s;
 544:             }
 545:             case T_ADD: {
 546:                 String s = text(n.a, 1) + " + " + text(n.b, 2);
 547:                 return prec > 1 ? "(" + s + ")" : s;
 548:             }
 549:             case T_SUB: {
 550:                 String s = text(n.a, 1) + " − " + text(n.b, 2);
 551:                 return prec > 1 ? "(" + s + ")" : s;
 552:             }
 553:             case T_MUL: {
 554:                 String a = text(n.a, 2);
 555:                 String b = text(n.b, 2);
 556:                 String s;
 557:                 if (n.a.t == T_NUM) s = a + b;                 // 2x 而不是 2·x
 558:                 else s = a + "·" + b;
 559:                 return prec > 2 ? "(" + s + ")" : s;
 560:             }
 561:             case T_DIV: {
 562:                 String s = text(n.a, 3) + " / " + text(n.b, 4);
 563:                 return prec > 3 ? "(" + s + ")" : s;
 564:             }
 565:             case T_POW: {
 566:                 String base = text(n.a, 5);
 567:                 if (n.b.t == T_NUM && Math.rint(n.b.v) == n.b.v) {
 568:                     String sp = sup((long) n.b.v);
 569:                     if (sp != null) return base + sp;
 570:                 }
 571:                 return base + "^" + text(n.b, 4);
 572:             }
 573:             default: {
 574:                 if (n.b != null) {
 575:                     return n.fn + "(" + text(n.a, 0) + ", " + text(n.b, 0) + ")";
 576:                 }
 577:                 return n.fn + "(" + text(n.a, 0) + ")";
 578:             }
 579:         }
 580:     }
 581: 
 582:     /**
 583:      * 给"纯文本显示"用的写法（不是排版）。
 584:      * FormulaView 走 FormulaLayout 画真公式；这里只给不需要排版的场合
 585:      * （例如列表标题、日志、判分反馈），保持和输入框语法一致，方便复制回输入框。
 586:      */
 587:     public static String mathText(Node n) {
 588:         return toText(n);
 589:     }
 590: 
 591:     /** 数字输出：整数不带小数点，其余最多 6 位有效小数，去掉末尾 0。 */
 592:     public static String fmtNum(double v) {
 593:         if (Double.isNaN(v)) return "无定义";
 594:         if (Double.isInfinite(v)) return v > 0 ? "+∞" : "−∞";
 595:         if (Math.rint(v) == v && Math.abs(v) < 1e15) {
 596:             long l = (long) v;
 597:             return String.valueOf(l);
 598:         }
 599:         String s = String.format(java.util.Locale.US, "%.6f", v);
 600:         while (s.endsWith("0")) s = s.substring(0, s.length() - 1);
 601:         if (s.endsWith(".")) s = s.substring(0, s.length() - 1);
 602:         return s;
 603:     }
 604: }

   1: package com.zsb.plan;
   2: 
   3: /**
   4:  * 函数输入模板 —— 手机上敲数学表达式的补位工具。
   5:  *
   6:  * 为什么要单独做一个类：
   7:  *   手机输入法敲 x^2 要切到符号页、敲 sin( 要关掉联想（否则 sin 会被
   8:  *   自动纠正成 son / sin. 之类），敲 sqrt 更是灾难。所以给一排按键，
   9:  *   按一下就插到光标处。
  10:  *
  11:  * 为什么逻辑不写在界面里：
  12:  *   "插到光标处"看着简单，实际有三个坑，全都只有试过才知道：
  13:  *     ① 有选区时要替换选区，不是插在选区前面；
  14:  *     ② 插 sin( 之后光标要落在括号「里面」，不然用户还得手动左移；
  15:  *     ③ 退格遇到 sin( 要整段删掉 —— 一个个删至少要按 4 次，
  16:  *        而且删到 sin 中间会变成 s 这种残词。
  17:  *   这三条在电脑上逐条断言，比在手机上点划算得多。
  18:  */
  19: public class ExprInput {
  20: 
  21:     /** 光标位置标记：模板里写 @ 表示"插完光标停这儿"，插入时 @ 本身会去掉。 */
  22:     public static final String CARET = "@";
  23: 
  24:     /** 插入结果：新文本 + 新的光标位置。 */
  25:     public static class R {
  26:         public final String text;
  27:         public final int cursor;
  28: 
  29:         public R(String text, int cursor) {
  30:             this.text = text;
  31:             this.cursor = cursor;
  32:         }
  33:     }
  34: 
  35:     // ── 常用函数模板（点一下整条换掉，不是插入）──────────
  36:     /** 完整的函数，点了直接画。挑的是"最常要画、手机上最难敲"的那些。 */
  37:     public static final String[] TEMPLATES = {
  38:         "x^2", "x^3-3x", "1/x", "sin(x)", "cos(x)", "tan(x)",
  39:         "ln(x)", "sqrt(x)", "exp(x)", "abs(x)", "atan(x)", "sin(x)/x",
  40:         "2x+1", "(x+1)(x-1)", "x^3", "1/(1+x^2)",
  41:     };
  42:     public static final String[] TEMPLATE_NAMES = {
  43:         "抛物线", "三次函数", "双曲线", "正弦", "余弦", "正切",
  44:         "对数", "根号", "指数", "绝对值", "反正切", "sinc",
  45:         "直线", "双根", "立方", "钟形",
  46:     };
  47: 
  48:     // ── 按键表 ────────────────────────────────────────────
  49:     /**
  50:      * 按键分两层：数字层和函数层。
  51:      *
  52:      * 原来只有一层，24 个键全是符号和函数 —— 结果打 x^2-4 的时候
  53:      * 数字还是得弹系统输入法。那排按键等于白做。
  54:      *
  55:      * 现在按计算器的习惯分页：
  56:      *   数字页：0-9 和四则运算，数字才是你最高频要敲的东西；
  57:      *   函数页：sin/cos/积分号这类，一次看全。
  58:      * 切页按钮上标 "fn" / "123"。
  59:      */
  60:     public static final String[][] KEYS_NUM = {
  61:         {"7", "7"}, {"8", "8"}, {"9", "9"},
  62:         {"(", "("}, {")", ")"}, {"DEL", "\b"},
  63: 
  64:         {"4", "4"}, {"5", "5"}, {"6", "6"},
  65:         {"x", "x"}, {"^", "^"}, {"÷", "/"},
  66: 
  67:         {"1", "1"}, {"2", "2"}, {"3", "3"},
  68:         {"+", "+"}, {"−", "-"}, {"×", "*"},
  69: 
  70:         {"0", "0"}, {".", "."}, {"π", "pi"},
  71:         {"x²", "x^2"}, {"x³", "x^3"}, {"fn", "\f"},
  72:     };
  73: 
  74:     public static final String[][] KEYS_FN = {
  75:         {"sin", "sin(@)"}, {"cos", "cos(@)"}, {"tan", "tan(@)"},
  76:         {"ln", "ln(@)"}, {"lg", "lg(@)"}, {"√", "sqrt(@)"},
  77: 
  78:         {"asin", "asin(@)"}, {"acos", "acos(@)"}, {"atan", "atan(@)"},
  79:         {"|x|", "abs(@)"}, {"eˣ", "exp(@)"}, {"1/x", "1/(@)"},
  80: 
  81:         {"x²", "x^2"}, {"x³", "x^3"}, {"pi", "pi"},
  82:         {"e", "e"}, {"·", "*"}, {"DEL", "\b"},
  83: 
  84:         {"123", "\n"}, {"sin(x)", "sin(@)"}, {"√x", "sqrt(@)"},
  85:         {"(x+1)", "(x+1)"}, {"x-1", "x-1"}, {"( )", "(@)"},
  86:     };
  87: 
  88:     /** 默认页（数字页）。界面首次渲染用它。 */
  89:     public static final String[][] KEYS = KEYS_NUM;
  90: 
  91:     /** 特殊片段：切换键盘页。 */
  92:     public static final String SWITCH = "\f";
  93:     /** 切回数字页。 */
  94:     public static final String BACK_TO_NUM = "\n";
  95: 
  96:     public static String[][] page(int which) {
  97:         return which == 0 ? KEYS_NUM : KEYS_FN;
  98:     }
  99: 
 100:     /**
 101:      * 按键表补齐到整行。KEYS.length 若不是每行 6 个的整数倍，
 102:      * 最后一行会残缺（原来的 x³ / 退格只占两格，右半边空着很难看）。
 103:      * 这里用空显示文字的占位键补满 —— 界面看到空文字就不加点击事件。
 104:      */
 105:     public static String[][] paddedKeys(int perRow, int which) {
 106:         String[][] src = page(which);
 107:         int n = src.length;
 108:         int target = ((n + perRow - 1) / perRow) * perRow;
 109:         if (target == n) return src;
 110:         String[][] out = new String[target][];
 111:         System.arraycopy(src, 0, out, 0, n);
 112:         for (int i = n; i < target; i++) out[i] = new String[]{"", ""};
 113:         return out;
 114:     }
 115: 
 116:     /** 占位键：显示文字为空，界面应跳过。 */
 117:     public static boolean isPad(String[] key) {
 118:         return key == null || key.length < 2 || key[0].isEmpty();
 119:     }
 120: 
 121:     /** 可以被整段退格删掉的函数名（长的在前，避免 asin 被 sin 抢走）。 */
 122:     private static final String[] FUNCS = {
 123:         "arcsin", "arccos", "arctan", "asin", "acos", "atan",
 124:         "sqrt", "abs", "exp", "sin", "cos", "tan", "log", "ln", "lg",
 125:     };
 126: 
 127:     // ── 插入 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
 128:     /**
 129:      * 在选区处插入片段。
 130:      *
 131:      * @param text     原文本（null 当作空串）
 132:      * @param selStart 选区起点（光标位置）
 133:      * @param selEnd   选区终点；等于 selStart 表示没有选区
 134:      * @param frag     要插入的片段，可含 @ 标记光标落点；"\b" 表示退格
 135:      */
 136:     public static R insert(String text, int selStart, int selEnd, String frag) {
 137:         String t = text == null ? "" : text;
 138:         int n = t.length();
 139:         int a = clamp(Math.min(selStart, selEnd), 0, n);
 140:         int b = clamp(Math.max(selStart, selEnd), 0, n);
 141: 
 142:         if ("\b".equals(frag)) return backspace(t, a, b);
 143:         // 翻页键不是输入内容，原样返回文本、光标不动
 144:         if (SWITCH.equals(frag) || BACK_TO_NUM.equals(frag)) return new R(t, a);
 145:         if (frag == null || frag.isEmpty()) return new R(t, a);
 146: 
 147:         String head = t.substring(0, a);
 148:         String tail = t.substring(b);
 149: 
 150:         int mark = frag.indexOf(CARET);
 151:         String body;
 152:         int cursorInFrag;
 153:         if (mark >= 0) {
 154:             body = frag.substring(0, mark) + frag.substring(mark + CARET.length());
 155:             cursorInFrag = mark;
 156:         } else {
 157:             body = frag;
 158:             cursorInFrag = body.length();
 159:         }
 160:         return new R(head + body + tail, head.length() + cursorInFrag);
 161:     }
 162: 
 163:     // ── 退格 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
 164:     /**
 165:      * 退格。有选区就删选区；没有选区时，如果光标前正好是一个「函数名 + (」，
 166:      * 整段删掉 —— 否则 sin( 要按 4 次，中途还会留下 s、si 这种残词。
 167:      */
 168:     public static R backspace(String text, int selStart, int selEnd) {
 169:         String t = text == null ? "" : text;
 170:         int n = t.length();
 171:         int a = clamp(Math.min(selStart, selEnd), 0, n);
 172:         int b = clamp(Math.max(selStart, selEnd), 0, n);
 173: 
 174:         if (a != b) {
 175:             return new R(t.substring(0, a) + t.substring(b), a);
 176:         }
 177:         if (a == 0) return new R(t, 0);
 178: 
 179:         String head = t.substring(0, a);
 180:         int cut = funcPrefixLen(head);
 181:         if (cut > 0) {
 182:             return new R(t.substring(0, a - cut) + t.substring(a), a - cut);
 183:         }
 184:         // 普通退格：删一个字符（
 185:         // 用 codePoint 判断，别把 emoji/生僻字劈成半个）
 186:         int cp = head.codePointBefore(head.length());
 187:         int len = Character.charCount(cp);
 188:         return new R(t.substring(0, a - len) + t.substring(a), a - len);
 189:     }
 190: 
 191:     /** head 末尾若是「函数名 + (」，返回要整段删掉的长度；否则 0。 */
 192:     static int funcPrefixLen(String head) {
 193:         if (head == null || head.isEmpty()) return 0;
 194:         if (!head.endsWith("(")) return 0;
 195:         String before = head.substring(0, head.length() - 1);
 196:         String low = before.toLowerCase();
 197:         for (String f : FUNCS) {
 198:             if (low.endsWith(f)) {
 199:                 // 函数名前不能紧跟字母（否则是别人名字的尾巴，如 "asin" 里的 "sin"）
 200:                 int start = before.length() - f.length();
 201:                 if (start > 0) {
 202:                     char prev = before.charAt(start - 1);
 203:                     if (isLetter(prev)) continue;
 204:                 }
 205:                 return f.length() + 1;
 206:             }
 207:         }
 208:         return 0;
 209:     }
 210: 
 211:     private static boolean isLetter(char c) {
 212:         return (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z');
 213:     }
 214: 
 215:     // ── 括号平衡提示 ──────────────────────────────────────
 216:     /** 未闭合的左括号数量；负数表示右括号多了。给输入框下面的提示用。 */
 217:     public static int unclosedParens(String text) {
 218:         if (text == null) return 0;
 219:         int d = 0;
 220:         for (int i = 0; i < text.length(); i++) {
 221:             char c = text.charAt(i);
 222:             if (c == '(' || c == '（') d++;
 223:             else if (c == ')' || c == '）') d--;
 224:         }
 225:         return d;
 226:     }
 227: 
 228:     /** 结尾少括号时，直接补上。返回补好的文本；本来就平衡则原样返回。 */
 229:     public static String closeParens(String text) {
 230:         String t = text == null ? "" : text;
 231:         int d = unclosedParens(t);
 232:         if (d <= 0) return t;
 233:         StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder(t);
 234:         for (int i = 0; i < d; i++) b.append(')');
 235:         return b.toString();
 236:     }
 237: 
 238:     static int clamp(int v, int lo, int hi) {
 239:         return v < lo ? lo : (v > hi ? hi : v);
 240:     }
 241: }
===== FILE: E:\小黑盒运营\cq-zhuanshengbens\mobile\src\com\zsb\plan\ExprOutline.java =====
   1: package com.zsb.plan;
   2: 
   3: /**
   4:  * 「怎么写 → 画出来长什么样」对照表的数据。
   5:  *
   6:  * 用户原话："函数图像那个你得写 sqrt 和 abs 和 exp 对照什么，不然谁知道"。
   7:  * 光有 16 个模板格子和一句"sin cos tan ln lg sqrt abs exp 都认"，
   8:  * 只是把函数名堆给用户 —— 不知道 sqrt 是什么形状、abs 为什么是 V 形、
   9:  * exp 和 x^2 有什么区别，模板再多也用不上。
  10:  *
  11:  * 每一条给三样东西：
  12:  *   write  在输入框里怎么写（可以直接点，点了就填进输入框）
  13:  *   name   中文叫什么（"开平方"，不是"平方根函数"这种半翻译）
  14:  *   shape  画出来是什么形状（"只画 x≥0 那半边，从原点往上走，越来越慢"）
  15:  *   ask    一个能自己验证的问题（"x=9 时是 3，不是 81"）—— 让形状可以被检验
  16:  *
  17:  * sample 是给缩略图采样用的表达式，必须与 write 同源，
  18:  * 由 ExprOutlineAudit 断言：sample 解析得了、定义域与 shape 描述一致。
  19:  */
  20: public class ExprOutline {
  21: 
  22:     public static class Item {
  23:         public final String write;   // 输入框里怎么写
  24:         public final String name;    // 中文名
  25:         public final String shape;   // 形状人话
  26:         public final String ask;     // 自己验一下
  27:         public final String sample;  // 缩略图采样用（与 write 同源）
  28:         public final int color;      // 曲线颜色
  29: 
  30:         public Item(String write, String name, String shape, String ask,
  31:                     String sample, int color) {
  32:             this.write = write;
  33:             this.name = name;
  34:             this.shape = shape;
  35:             this.ask = ask;
  36:             this.sample = sample;
  37:             this.color = color;
  38:         }
  39:     }
  40: 
  41:     /** 每组一个标题，按"最常用 → 越来越少用"排。 */
  42:     public static final String[] GROUPS = {
  43:             "平方 / 开方 / 绝对值 —— 最容易被搞混的三个",
  44:             "指数与对数 —— 长得像但方向相反",
  45:             "三角函数 —— 会来回摆的",
  46:             "其它常用"
  47:     };
  48: 
  49:     public static Item[] all() {
  50:         return new Item[]{
  51:             // ── 组一：幂、根、绝对值 ──
  52:             new Item("x^2", "平方", "抛物线，左右对称，最低点在原点",
  53:                     "x 取 3 和 −3，结果都是 9 —— 所以左右一样高",
  54:                     "x^2", 0xFF3ECFAD),

 109:     /** 每条属于哪一组（与 GROUPS 对应），用于分节显示。 */
 110:     public static int groupOf(int i) {
 111:         if (i < 5) return 0;
 112:         if (i < 9) return 1;
 113:         if (i < 13) return 2;
 114:         return 3;
 115:     }
