 211:     /**
 212:      * 系统返回键的接管。
 213:      *
 214:      * 原来没有任何处理 —— 按一下就把 MainActivity 关掉回桌面，
 215:      * 而战斗状态（血量/手牌/回合/题目）只存在 GBattleView 实例里，
 216:      * Activity 一死就全没了。重进时地图以为那一格已经打过，
 217:      * 于是「上一个怪就自动过关了」。
 218:      *
 219:      * 现在的顺序：
 220:      *   1. 在词汇塔且正在战斗 → 什么都不做（战后有「继续」按钮，不用返回键逃）
 221:      *   2. 在词汇塔（非战斗）→ 回今日页，不退出应用
 222:      *   3. 其它 tab → 回今日页
 223:      *   4. 已经在今日页 → 才真正退出（moveTaskToBack 保留进程）
 224:      */
 225:     @Override
 226:     public void onBackPressed() {
 227:         if (tab == 3) {
 228:             if (gameView != null && gameView.inBattle()) {
 229:                 Toast.makeText(this, "战斗中不能退出 —— 打完这一场再用「继续」推进",
 230:                         Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
 231:                 return;
 232:             }
 233:             tab = 0;
 234:             render();
 235:             return;
 236:         }
 237:         if (tab != 0) {
 238:             tab = 0;
 239:             render();
 240:             return;
 241:         }
 242:         // 回到后台而不是 finish()：进程留着，下次打开还在原处
 243:         moveTaskToBack(true);
 244:     }
 245: 
 246:     // ── 渲染分发 ──────────────────────────────────────────
 247:     private void render() {
 248:         tvDays.setText(String.valueOf(store.daysLeft(plan)));
 249:         tvLeftLabel.setText("已确认 " + store.completed.size() + " / " + plan.days.size() + " 天");
 250: 
 251:         for (int i = 0; i < navBtn.length; i++) {
 252:             navBtn[i].setTextColor(i == tab ? C_FG : C_FG3);
 253:             navBtn[i].setTypeface(i == tab ? Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD : Typeface.DEFAULT);
 254:         }
 255: 
 256:         content.removeAllViews();
 257:         // 游戏页要贴满屏幕，其它页保留统一的左右留白。
 258:         // 在这里集中设置，避免各 render 方法各设一套、切 tab 后互相残留。
 259:         if (tab == 3) content.setPadding(0, 0, 0, 0);
 260:         else content.setPadding(dp(12), dp(10), dp(12), dp(10));
 261: 
 262:         switch (tab) {
 263:             case 0: renderToday(); break;
 264:             case 1: renderHard(); break;
 265:             case 2: renderPlanList(); break;
 266:             case 3: renderGame(); break;
 267:             default: renderStats(); break;
 268:         }
 269:     }
 270: 
 271:     // ══ 今日 ══════════════════════════════════════════════
 272:     private void renderToday() {
 273:         if (plan.days.isEmpty()) { content.addView(hint("计划数据为空")); return; }
 274:         idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(idx, plan.days.size() - 1));
 275:         final Plan.Day d = plan.days.get(idx);
 276: 
 277:         content.addView(dayHeader(d));
 278: 
 279:         // ── 每日置顶必背卡 ────────────────────────────────
 280:         //
 281:         // 用户原话："让你默写这个东西，不用默写毕竟手机上怎么默写，
 282:         // 把答案写出来直接提醒用户答案是这个记住就行了，做成每日置顶，
 283:         // 显眼每次打开看一遍更好"。
 284:         //
 285:         // 所以这里不是"练习"，是"答案卡"：一打开就先看到今天该背的
 286:         // 结论，默写框已删除（原来 reviewRow 里的 EditText 去掉了）。
 287:         addRecallTop();
 288: 
 289:         // 任务
 290:         if (!d.tasks.isEmpty()) {
 291:             LinearLayout card = card("今日任务", null);
 292:             for (Plan.Task t : d.tasks) card.addView(taskRow(d.date, t));
 293:             content.addView(card);
 294:         }
 295: 
 296:         // 检索
 297:         //
 298:         // 原来这里还有个独立的「检索」tab，渲染的是同一批 d.reviews ——
 299:         // 明明是同一份数据换个位置重复显示，用户自然会问"怎么还是以前的东西"。
 300:         // 检索本来就属于当天：逾期会累积到当天，不单独成页。已删。
 301:         //
 302:         // 但"还有多少项没做、会不会累积"这件事要让人看得见，
 303:         // 所以把逾期统计补在这一张卡上，而不是靠翻页去数。
 304:         if (!d.reviews.isEmpty()) {
 305:             int done = 0;
 306:             for (Plan.Review r : d.reviews) {
 307:                 if (store.completed.contains("R-" + r.id)) done++;
 308:             }
 309:             int left = d.reviews.size() - done;
 310:             String sub = left == 0
 311:                     ? "今天要回忆的都看过了"
 312:                     : "还剩 " + left + " 项没看 · 点开直接看答案，不用默写";
 313:             LinearLayout card = card("今日回忆 · " + d.reviews.size() + " 章", sub);
 314:             for (Plan.Review r : d.reviews) card.addView(reviewRow(d.date, r));
 315:             content.addView(card);
 316:         }
 317: 
 318:         // 词汇 —— 英语这一科在 App 里就是这块
 319:         // 用户原话："英语只需要单词就行了"
 320:         // 原来这里只把 json 片段当文字打印出来（{"from":20,"to":40}），
 321:         // 既不能背也不能点。现在做成真入口。
 322:         if (d.vocabFrom >= 0 && d.vocabTo > d.vocabFrom) {
 323:             final int vf = d.vocabFrom, vt = d.vocabTo;
 324:             int nNew = vt - vf;
 325: 
 326:             LinearLayout vcard = card("英语 · 背单词", null);
 327: 
 328:             TextView big = new TextView(this);
 329:             big.setText("今天 " + nNew + " 个新词");
 330:             big.setTextColor(C_FG);
 331:             big.setTextSize(17);
 332:             big.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 333:             vcard.addView(big);
 334: 
 335:             TextView sub = new TextView(this);
 336:             sub.setText("第 " + (vf + 1) + " – " + vt + " 个"
 337:                     + (d.vocabReviewJson != null ? " · 另有旧词要复习" : ""));
 338:             sub.setTextColor(C_FG3);
 339:             sub.setTextSize(12);
 340:             sub.setPadding(0, dp(3), 0, 0);
 341:             vcard.addView(sub);
 342: 
 343:             // 主按钮：背今天的词
 344:             TextView go = new TextView(this);
 345:             go.setText("开始背这 " + nNew + " 个 →");
 346:             go.setTextColor(C_TEAL);
 347:             go.setTextSize(13.5f);
 348:             go.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 349:             go.setPadding(0, dp(10), 0, dp(2));
 350:             go.setOnClickListener(v -> wordView.open(vf, vt, "今天的单词"));
 351:             vcard.addView(go);
 352: 
 353:             // 练一练：翻卡只测"见过没"，测不出版面真实能力。
 354:             // 五种题型分别查读、写、语境、词性 —— 这是"丰富教法"的落点。
 355:             TextView drill = new TextView(this);
 356:             drill.setText("✎ 练一练这 " + nNew + " 个词（五种题型）→");
 357:             drill.setTextColor(C_PURPLE);
 358:             drill.setTextSize(13.5f);
 359:             drill.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 360:             drill.setPadding(0, dp(9), 0, dp(2));
 361:             drill.setOnClickListener(v -> engView.open(vf, vt, "练今天这批词"));
 362:             vcard.addView(drill);
 363: 
 364:             // 复习按钮：只在有旧词时出现
 365:             final String rvJson = d.vocabReviewJson;
 366:             if (rvJson != null) {
 367:                 TextView rev = new TextView(this);
 368:                 rev.setText("复习旧词 →");
 369:                 rev.setTextColor(C_AMBER);
 370:                 rev.setTextSize(13.5f);
 371:                 rev.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 372:                 rev.setPadding(0, dp(6), 0, dp(2));
 373:                 rev.setOnClickListener(v -> {
 374:                     try {
 375:                         wordView.openReview(new org.json.JSONArray(rvJson), "复习旧词");
 376:                     } catch (Exception e) {
 377:                         android.widget.Toast.makeText(MainActivity.this,
 378:                                 "复习数据读取失败", android.widget.Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
 379:                     }
 380:                 });
 381:                 vcard.addView(rev);
 382: 
 383:                 TextView drillOld = new TextView(this);
 384:                 drillOld.setText("✎ 练这批旧词 →");
 385:                 drillOld.setTextColor(C_PURPLE);
 386:                 drillOld.setTextSize(13.5f);
 387:                 drillOld.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 388:                 drillOld.setPadding(0, dp(6), 0, dp(2));
 389:                 drillOld.setOnClickListener(v -> {
 390:                     try {
 391:                         org.json.JSONArray arr = new org.json.JSONArray(rvJson);
 392:                         List<String> ws = new ArrayList<String>();
 393:                         for (int i = 0; i < arr.length(); i++) {
 394:                             org.json.JSONObject o = arr.optJSONObject(i);
 395:                             if (o != null) {
 396:                                 String w = o.optString("w", "");
 397:                                 if (!w.isEmpty()) ws.add(w);
 398:                             }
 399:                         }
 400:                         if (ws.isEmpty()) {
 401:                             engView.open();
 402:                         } else {
 403:                             engView.openList(ws, "练旧词");
 404:                         }
 405:                     } catch (Exception e) {
 406:                         engView.open();
 407:                     }
 408:                 });
 409:                 vcard.addView(drillOld);
 410:             }
 411: 
 412:             vcard.addView(checkRow(d.date, "V-" + d.date, "今天的单词过完了"));
 413:             content.addView(vcard);
 414:         }
 415: 
 416:         // 推进闸门
 417:         content.addView(commitBlock(d));
 418:     }
 419: 
 420:     private View dayHeader(Plan.Day d) {
 421:         LinearLayout box = new LinearLayout(this);
 422:         box.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
 423:         box.setPadding(0, 0, 0, dp(10));
 424: 
 425:         boolean isToday = Store.todayIso().equals(d.date);
 426: 
 427:         TextView title = new TextView(this);
 428:         title.setText(d.date + "  周" + d.weekday + (isToday ? "   · 今天" : ""));
 429:         title.setTextColor(isToday ? C_TEAL : C_FG);
 430:         title.setTextSize(19);
 431:         title.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 432:         box.addView(title);
 433: 
 434:         TextView sub = new TextView(this);
 435:         String s = "第 " + (idx + 1) + " / " + plan.days.size() + " 学习日";
 436:         if (!TextUtils.isEmpty(d.phase)) s += "   ·   " + d.phase;
 437:         int examLeft = store.realDaysToExam(plan);
 438:         if (examLeft > 0) s += "   ·   距考 " + examLeft + " 天";
 439:         sub.setText(s);
 440:         sub.setTextColor(C_FG3);
 441:         sub.setTextSize(12);
 442:         sub.setPadding(0, dp(4), 0, 0);
 443:         box.addView(sub);
 444: 
 445:         // 前后翻页
 446:         LinearLayout nav = new LinearLayout(this);
 447:         nav.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
 448:         nav.setPadding(0, dp(9), 0, 0);
 449: 
 450:         nav.addView(smallBtn("‹ 上一天", () -> {
 451:             if (idx > 0) { idx--; stopTimer(); render(); }
 452:         }, idx > 0));
 453: 
 454:         nav.addView(smallBtn("下一天 ›", () -> {
 455:             if (idx < plan.days.size() - 1) { idx++; stopTimer(); render(); }
 456:         }, idx < plan.days.size() - 1));
 457: 
 458:         if (idx != store.todayIndex(plan)) {
 459:             nav.addView(smallBtn("回到今天", () -> {
 460:                 idx = store.todayIndex(plan); stopTimer(); render();
 461:             }, true));
 462:         }
 463:         box.addView(nav);
 464:         return box;
 465:     }
 466: 
 467:     // ── 任务 → 教材页 ─────────────────────────────────────
 468:     // 每科一本教材，页码由 plan.json 的 pdfStart 给出
 469:     // （生成时已按各书的印刷页→PDF 页偏移换算好）。
 470:     // 练习册与数学科已整块移除，这里不再有 pract 分支。
 471:     static class BookRef {
 472:         String id, title, shortName;
 473:         int page;
 474:     }
 475: 
 476:     /** 学科代码 → 中文名。题库按钮和页面标题都用它。 */
 477:     static String subjectName(String s) {
 478:         if ("math".equals(s)) return "数学";
 479:         if ("comp".equals(s)) return "计算机";
 480:         if ("eng".equals(s)) return "英语";
 481:         return s == null ? "" : s;
 482:     }
 483: 
 484:     private BookRef bookRefOf(Plan.Task t) {
 485:         if (t == null) return null;
 486: 
 487:         String subj = t.subject == null ? "" : t.subject;
 488:         String bookId, title, shortName;
 489:         if ("comp".equals(subj)) {
 490:             bookId = "comp_book"; title = "计算机基础教材"; shortName = "计算机教材";
 491:         } else if ("eng".equals(subj)) {
 492:             bookId = "eng_book"; title = "大学英语基础教材"; shortName = "英语教材";
 493:         } else {
 494:             return null;
 495:         }
 496: 
 497:         // 练习册整块去掉了：用户说排版乱、不要了。
 498:         // 原来这里按 kind=="pract" 换成 *_pract 书并 +2 页偏移，
 499:         // 连带 plan.json 里 77 条 pract 任务一起清了。
 500:         int page = 0;
 501:         if (t.pdfStart != null) page = t.pdfStart;
 502:         else if (t.startPage > 0) page = t.startPage + 1;
 503: 
 504:         if (page <= 0) return null;
 505:         BookRef r = new BookRef();
 506:         r.id = bookId;
 507:         r.title = title;
 508:         r.shortName = shortName;
 509:         r.page = page;
 510:         return r;
 511:     }
 512: 
 513:     private View taskRow(final String date, final Plan.Task t) {
 514:         LinearLayout row = new LinearLayout(this);
 515:         row.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
 516:         row.setPadding(0, dp(10), 0, dp(10));
 517:         row.setGravity(Gravity.TOP);
 518: 
 519:         final String tid = Plan.taskId(t);
 520:         final boolean done = store.isDone(date, tid);
 521: 
 522:         // 勾选框
 523:         final TextView chk = new TextView(this);
 524:         chk.setText(done ? "✓" : "");
 525:         chk.setTextSize(13);
 526:         chk.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
 527:         chk.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
 528:         GradientDrawable g = new GradientDrawable();
 529:         g.setCornerRadius(dp(6));
 530:         g.setStroke(dp(2), done ? C_PURPLE : C_FG3);
 531:         g.setColor(done ? C_PURPLE : Color.TRANSPARENT);
 532:         chk.setBackground(g);
 533:         LinearLayout.LayoutParams clp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(dp(24), dp(24));
 534:         clp.topMargin = dp(1);
 535:         chk.setLayoutParams(clp);
 536:         chk.setOnClickListener(v -> {
 537:             boolean nv = store.toggleDone(date, tid);
 538:             chk.setText(nv ? "✓" : "");
 539:             GradientDrawable g2 = new GradientDrawable();
 540:             g2.setCornerRadius(dp(6));
 541:             g2.setStroke(dp(2), nv ? C_PURPLE : C_FG3);
 542:             g2.setColor(nv ? C_PURPLE : Color.TRANSPARENT);
 543:             chk.setBackground(g2);
 544:             renderHeaderOnly();
 545:             // 勾选状态变化可能影响推进按钮，重画整页
 546:             render();
 547:         });
 548:         row.addView(chk);
 549: 
 550:         // 文字区
 551:         LinearLayout col = new LinearLayout(this);
 552:         col.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
 553:         LinearLayout.LayoutParams colp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 554:                 0, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f);
 555:         colp.leftMargin = dp(11);
 556:         col.setLayoutParams(colp);
 557: 
 558:         Plan.Subject sj = plan.subject(t.subject);
 559:         Plan.Chapter ch = plan.chapter(t.chapterId);
 560: 
 561:         TextView name = new TextView(this);
 562:         String line1;
 563:         if ("learn".equals(t.kind) && ch != null) {
 564:             line1 = sj.name + " · " + ch.name;
 565:         } else {
 566:             line1 = TextUtils.isEmpty(t.note) ? sj.name : t.note;
 567:         }
 568:         name.setText(line1);
 569:         name.setTextColor(done ? C_FG3 : C_FG);
 570:         name.setTextSize(14.5f);
 571:         if (!done) name.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 572:         col.addView(name);
 573: 
 574:         // 页码 + 时长
 575:         List<String> meta = new ArrayList<>();
 576:         if (t.startPage > 0 && t.endPage >= t.startPage) {
 577:             meta.add("p" + t.startPage + "-" + t.endPage);
 578:         }
 579:         if (t.min > 0) meta.add(t.min + " 分钟");
 580:         long el = store.timingOf(date, tid);
 581:         if (el > 1000) meta.add("已计时 " + fmtDur(el));
 582: 
 583:         TextView m = new TextView(this);
 584:         m.setText(TextUtils.join("   ", meta));
 585:         m.setTextColor(C_FG3);
 586:         m.setTextSize(11.5f);
 587:         m.setPadding(0, dp(3), 0, 0);
 588:         col.addView(m);
 589:         if (!TextUtils.isEmpty(t.note) && "learn".equals(t.kind)) {
 590:             TextView note = new TextView(this);
 591:             note.setText(t.note);
 592:             note.setTextColor(0xFF5C6373);
 593:             note.setTextSize(11);
 594:             note.setPadding(0, dp(2), 0, 0);
 595:             col.addView(note);
 596:         }
 597: 
 598:         // 打开教材 —— 点这行文字直接跳对应页，不用去翻 PDF
 599:         final BookRef ref = bookRefOf(t);
 600:         if (ref != null) {
 601:             TextView open = new TextView(this);
 602:             open.setText("📖 打开" + ref.shortName + " p" + ref.page);
 603:             open.setTextColor(C_TEAL);
 604:             open.setTextSize(12.5f);
 605:             open.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 606:             open.setPadding(0, dp(7), 0, dp(1));
 607:             open.setOnClickListener(v -> book.open(ref.id, ref.title, ref.page));
 608:             col.addView(open);
 609:         }
 610: 
 611:         // 做题 —— 任务本身带题库时才有这个按钮
 612:         // 用户原话："根本没有题目"：光写"练习册 p5-6"没法在手机上做
 613:         if (!TextUtils.isEmpty(t.bankSubject)) {
 614:             TextView quiz = new TextView(this);
 615:             int n = t.bankLimit > 0 ? t.bankLimit : 10;
 616: 
 617:             // 数学分支已删：数学任务整块移出今日任务，
 618:             // 函数图像/求导公式改在「工具」页里直达（不会丢入口）。
 619:             String label;
 620:             if (t.bankYear > 0) {
 621:                 label = "✎ 做 " + t.bankYear + " 年真题";
 622:             } else {
 623:                 label = "✎ 做题（" + n + " 道）";
 624:             }
 625:             quiz.setText(label);
 626:             quiz.setTextColor(C_AMBER);
 627:             quiz.setTextSize(12.5f);
 628:             quiz.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 629:             quiz.setPadding(0, dp(6), 0, dp(1));
 630:             quiz.setOnClickListener(v -> {
 631:                 if (t.bankYear > 0) {
 632:                     quizView.openYear(t.bankSubject, t.bankYear,
 633:                             t.bankYear + " 年真题");
 634:                 } else {
 635:                     quizView.openSubject(t.bankSubject, n, false,
 636:                             subjectName(t.bankSubject) + " 练习");
 637:                 }
 638:             });
 639:             col.addView(quiz);
 640:         }
 641: 
 642:         row.addView(col);
 643: 
 644:         // 计时按钮
 645:         final TextView timer = new TextView(this);
 646:         final String tkey = Store.key(date, tid);
 647:         boolean running = tkey.equals(timingKey);
 648:         timer.setText(running ? "■" : "▶");
 649:         timer.setTextSize(13);
 650:         timer.setTextColor(running ? C_PINK : C_FG3);
 651:         timer.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
 652:         GradientDrawable bg = new GradientDrawable();
 653:         bg.setCornerRadius(dp(8));
 654:         bg.setColor(C_BG3);
 655:         timer.setBackground(bg);
 656:         LinearLayout.LayoutParams tlp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(dp(34), dp(30));
 657:         tlp.leftMargin = dp(6);
 658:         timer.setLayoutParams(tlp);
 659:         timer.setOnClickListener(v -> {
 660:             if (tkey.equals(timingKey)) stopTimer();
 661:             else startTimer(tkey, m);
 662:             render();
 663:         });
 664:         row.addView(timer);
 665: 
 666:         return row;
 667:     }
 668: 
 669:     /**
 670:      * 今日置顶必背卡。
 671:      *
 672:      * 取今天所有到期复习章节的答案，一章一组，直接摊在最上面。
 673:      * 用户要求"每次打开看一遍"，所以不折叠、不需要点开。
 674:      */
 675:     private void addRecallTop() {
 676:         if (idx < 0 || idx >= plan.days.size()) return;
 677:         Plan.Day d = plan.days.get(idx);
 678: 
 679:         // 候选章：① 今天到期要复习的（优先，这是记忆曲线的正事）
 680:         //         ② 今天新学的（保证每天都有卡，不然头几天打开是空的）
 681:         // 都按出现顺序去重；没有答案的章直接跳过（宁缺勿编）。
 682:         java.util.LinkedHashSet<String> seen = new java.util.LinkedHashSet<>();
 683:         for (Plan.Review r : d.reviews) {
 684:             if (plan.recall.containsKey(r.chapterId)) seen.add(r.chapterId);
 685:         }
 686:         int fromReview = seen.size();
 687:         if (seen.isEmpty()) {
 688:             for (Plan.Task t : d.tasks) {
 689:                 if (plan.recall.containsKey(t.chapterId)) seen.add(t.chapterId);
 690:             }
 691:         }
 692:         // ③ 兜底：越靠后越是整天刷真题/错题，没有"新学的章"。
 693:         // 但"每日置顶"不能因为今天是综合练习就空着——那就退回到
 694:         // 最近学过的那几章，让用户每天都有东西扫一眼。
 695:         if (seen.isEmpty()) {
 696:             for (int i = idx; i >= 0 && seen.isEmpty(); i--) {
 697:                 for (Plan.Task t : plan.days.get(i).tasks) {
 698:                     if (plan.recall.containsKey(t.chapterId)) seen.add(t.chapterId);
 699:                 }
 700:             }
 701:         }
 702:         if (seen.isEmpty()) return;
 703: 
 704:         String sub;
 705:         if (fromReview > 0) {
 706:             sub = "今天到期的 " + fromReview + " 章 · 答案直接摊开，不用默写";
 707:         } else if (hasLearnedOn(d)) {
 708:             sub = "今天新学的 " + seen.size() + " 章 · 先背住这些结论";
 709:         } else {
 710:             sub = "最近学过的 " + seen.size() + " 章 · 综合练习日再看一遍";
 711:         }
 712: 
 713:         LinearLayout card = card("今日必背 · " + seen.size() + " 章", sub);
 714: 
 715:         int rank = 0;
 716:         for (String cid : seen) {
 717:             Plan.Chapter ch = plan.chapter(cid);
 718:             String name = ch != null ? ch.name : cid;
 719: 
 720:             TextView h = new TextView(this);
 721:             h.setText((++rank) + ".  " + name);
 722:             h.setTextColor(C_AMBER);
 723:             h.setTextSize(14.5f);
 724:             h.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 725:             h.setPadding(0, dp(rank == 1 ? 2 : 12), 0, dp(2));
 726:             card.addView(h);
 727: 
 728:             addRecallPoints(card, cid);
 729:         }
 730:         content.addView(card);
 731:     }
 732: 
 733:     /** 今天有没有可以直接出卡的「新学」章节。 */
 734:     private boolean hasLearnedOn(Plan.Day d) {
 735:         for (Plan.Task t : d.tasks) {
 736:             if (plan.recall.containsKey(t.chapterId)) return true;
 737:         }
 738:         return false;
 739:     }
 740: 
 741:     /** 把一章的答案点逐条摊开。没有卡就静默跳过。 */
 742:     private void addRecallPoints(LinearLayout parent, String chapterId) {
 743:         Plan.Recall rc = plan.recall.get(chapterId);
 744:         if (rc == null) return;
 745: 
 746:         for (String p : rc.points) {
 747:             TextView tv = new TextView(this);
 748:             tv.setText("· " + p);
 749:             tv.setTextColor(C_FG2);
 750:             tv.setTextSize(13.5f);
 751:             tv.setLineSpacing(dp(3), 1f);
 752:             tv.setPadding(dp(2), dp(2), 0, dp(2));
 753:             parent.addView(tv);
 754:         }
 755: 
 756:         if (!rc.exam.isEmpty()) {
 757:             TextView k = new TextView(this);
 758:             k.setText("考点： " + rc.exam);
 759:             k.setTextColor(C_TEAL);
 760:             k.setTextSize(13f);
 761:             k.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 762:             k.setLineSpacing(dp(3), 1f);
 763:             k.setPadding(dp(2), dp(4), 0, dp(3));
 764:             parent.addView(k);
 765:         }
 766:     }
 767: 
 768:     private View reviewRow(final String date, final Plan.Review r) {
 769:         LinearLayout box = new LinearLayout(this);
 770:         box.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
 771:         box.setPadding(0, dp(9), 0, dp(9));
 772: 
 773:         final String rid = "R-" + r.id;
 774:         final boolean done = store.isDone(date, rid);
 775: 
 776:         LinearLayout row = new LinearLayout(this);
 777:         row.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
 778:         row.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER_VERTICAL);
 779: 
 780:         final TextView chk = new TextView(this);
 781:         chk.setText(done ? "✓" : "");
 782:         chk.setTextSize(13);
 783:         chk.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
 784:         chk.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
 785:         GradientDrawable g = new GradientDrawable();
 786:         g.setCornerRadius(dp(6));
 787:         g.setStroke(dp(2), done ? C_PURPLE : C_FG3);
 788:         g.setColor(done ? C_PURPLE : Color.TRANSPARENT);
 789:         chk.setBackground(g);
 790:         chk.setLayoutParams(new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(dp(24), dp(24)));
 791:         chk.setOnClickListener(v -> store.toggleDone(date, rid));
 792:         row.addView(chk);
 793: 
 794:         Plan.Subject sj = plan.subject(r.subject);
 795:         Plan.Chapter ch = plan.chapter(r.chapterId);
 796: 
 797:         TextView tv = new TextView(this);
 798:         tv.setText(r.kind + "  " + sj.name + (ch != null ? " · " + ch.name : "")
 799:                 + (r.min > 0 ? "  (" + r.min + " 分钟)" : ""));
 800:         tv.setTextColor(done ? C_FG3 : C_FG);
 801:         tv.setTextSize(13.5f);
 802:         tv.setPadding(dp(11), 0, 0, 0);
 803:         LinearLayout.LayoutParams lp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 804:                 0, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f);
 805:         tv.setLayoutParams(lp);
 806:         row.addView(tv);
 807: 
 808:         box.addView(row);
 809: 
 810:         // 默写框已删除。
 811:         // 手机上打字慢、还看不全公式，默写只会变成"卡住不动"。
 812:         // 改成直接把答案摊开（见下面的 recallPoints），看一眼记一遍。
 813:         addRecallPoints(box, r.chapterId);
 814: 
 815:         // 三档自评
 816:         LinearLayout grades = new LinearLayout(this);
 817:         grades.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
 818:         LinearLayout.LayoutParams glp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 819:                 ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT);
 820:         glp.topMargin = dp(6);
 821:         glp.leftMargin = dp(35);
 822:         grades.setLayoutParams(glp);
 823: 
 824:         // 三档自评保留，但不再带默写内容 —— 没有输入框了，
 825:         // 原来那两句 store.setNote(..., et.getText()) 已删。
 826:         grades.addView(gradeBtn("记不住", () -> {
 827:             store.bumpHard(r.chapterId.isEmpty() ? r.id : r.chapterId);
 828:             store.setDone(date, rid, false);
 829:             toast("已进错题库，明天再看一遍");
 830:             render();
 831:         }));
 832:         grades.addView(gradeBtn("有点印象", () -> {
 833:             store.setDone(date, rid, true);
 834:             toast("再多扫两遍就记住了");
 835:             render();
 836:         }));
 837:         grades.addView(gradeBtn("记住了", () -> {
 838:             store.setDone(date, rid, true);
 839:             toast("过关");
 840:             render();
 841:         }));
 842:         box.addView(grades);
 843: 
 844:         View sep = new View(this);
 845:         sep.setBackgroundColor(C_LINE);
 846:         LinearLayout.LayoutParams slp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 847:                 ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, dp(1));
 848:         slp.topMargin = dp(9);
 849:         sep.setLayoutParams(slp);
 850:         box.addView(sep);
 851: 
 852:         return box;
 853:     }
 854: 
 855:     private View commitBlock(final Plan.Day d) {
 856:         LinearLayout box = new LinearLayout(this);
 857:         box.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
 858:         box.setPadding(0, dp(6), 0, 0);
 859: 
 860:         List<String> ids = Plan.dayTaskIds(d);
 861:         int doneN = 0;
 862:         for (String id : ids) if (store.isDone(d.date, id)) doneN++;
 863:         final boolean all = !ids.isEmpty() && doneN == ids.size();
 864:         final boolean passed = store.completed.contains(d.date);
 865: 
 866:         TextView label = new TextView(this);
 867:         label.setText("完成度 " + doneN + " / " + ids.size()
 868:                 + (all ? "   全部完成" : "   还有 " + (ids.size() - doneN) + " 项"));
 869:         label.setTextColor(all ? C_GREEN : C_FG3);
 870:         label.setTextSize(12.5f);
 871:         label.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
 872:         label.setPadding(0, dp(6), 0, dp(8));
 873:         box.addView(label);
 874: 
 875:         Button btn = new Button(this);
 876:         if (passed) {
 877:             btn.setText("已确认完成 · 点此撤销");
 878:             btn.setTextColor(C_FG2);
 879:             btn.setBackgroundColor(C_BG3);
 880:             btn.setOnClickListener(v -> new AlertDialog.Builder(this)
 881:                     .setMessage("把 " + d.date + " 退回未完成？剩余天数会增加 1。")
 882:                     .setNegativeButton("取消", null)
 883:                     .setPositiveButton("撤销", (x, y) -> {
 884:                         store.uncommitDay(d.date);
 885:                         idx = store.todayIndex(plan);
 886:                         render();
 887:                         toast("已撤销");
 888:                     }).show());
 889:         } else {
 890:             btn.setText(all ? "今日完成 · 推进到下一天" : "把上面全部勾完才能推进");
 891:             btn.setTextColor(all ? Color.WHITE : C_FG3);
 892:             btn.setBackgroundColor(all ? C_PURPLE : C_BG3);
 893:             btn.setEnabled(all);
 894:             btn.setOnClickListener(v -> new AlertDialog.Builder(this)
 895:                     .setMessage("确认 " + d.date + " 已完成？\n\n这会推进计划进度，剩余天数减少 1。")
 896:                     .setNegativeButton("再想想", null)
 897:                     .setPositiveButton("确认完成", (x, y) -> {
 898:                         store.commitDay(d.date);
 899:                         idx = store.todayIndex(plan);
 900:                         render();
 901:                         toast("已推进 · 剩余 " + store.daysLeft(plan) + " 天");
 902:                     }).show());
 903:         }
 904:         LinearLayout.LayoutParams lp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 905:                 ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, dp(48));
 906:         lp.topMargin = dp(2);
 907:         btn.setLayoutParams(lp);
 908:         btn.setTextSize(14);
 909:         box.addView(btn);
 910: 
 911:         return box;
 912:     }

 914:     // ══ 错题库 ════════════════════════════════════════════
 915:     private void renderHardMap() { renderHard(); }
 916: 
 917:     private void renderHard() {
 918:         if (store.hard.isEmpty()) {
 919:             content.addView(hint("错题库是空的。\n\n检索时选「写不出」，那一节就会进来。"
 920:                     + "\n英语训练答错的单词也会自动进来。"));
 921:             content.addView(wideBtn("✎ 练英语单词（五种题型）", () -> engView.open()));
 922:             return;
 923:         }
 924:         LinearLayout card = card("错题库 · " + store.hard.size() + " 项",
 925:                 "等级越高越该回头看。清掉后就从列表移除。");
 926:         for (final String id : new ArrayList<>(store.hard.keySet())) {
 927:             Plan.Chapter ch = plan.chapter(id);
 928:             Integer lv = store.hard.get(id);
 929:             LinearLayout row = new LinearLayout(this);
 930:             row.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
 931:             row.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER_VERTICAL);
 932:             row.setPadding(0, dp(9), 0, dp(9));
 933: 
 934:             TextView tv = new TextView(this);
 935:             // 英语错题的 key 是 "eng:单词"，不是章节 id，plan.chapter() 找不到。
 936:             // 不特判的话这里会把 "eng:apple" 原样打出来，看着像脏数据。
 937:             boolean isEngWord = id.startsWith("eng:");
 938:             String nm;
 939:             if (isEngWord) {
 940:                 nm = "英语 · 单词 " + id.substring(4);
 941:             } else if (ch != null) {
 942:                 nm = plan.subject(ch.subject).name + " · " + ch.name;
 943:             } else {
 944:                 nm = id;
 945:             }
 946:             tv.setText(nm + "    等级 " + (lv == null ? 1 : lv) + " / 3");
 947:             tv.setTextColor(C_FG);
 948:             tv.setTextSize(13.5f);
 949:             tv.setLayoutParams(new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 950:                     0, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f));
 951:             if (isEngWord) {
 952:                 final String word = id.substring(4);
 953:                 tv.setOnClickListener(v -> {
 954:                     List<String> one = new ArrayList<String>();
 955:                     one.add(word);
 956:                     engView.openList(one, "重练 " + word);
 957:                 });
 958:             }
 959:             row.addView(tv);
 960: 
 961:             row.addView(smallBtn("降级", () -> {
 962:                 Integer c = store.hard.get(id);
 963:                 if (c == null || c <= 1) { store.clearHard(id); toast("移出错题库"); }
 964:                 else { store.hard.put(id, c - 1); store.save(); toast("降一级"); }
 965:                 render();
 966:             }, true));
 967: 
 968:             card.addView(row);
 969:         }
 970:         content.addView(card);
 971:     }
 972: 
 973:     // ══ 全表 ══════════════════════════════════════════════
 974:     private void renderPlanList() {
 975:         LinearLayout card = card("全部 " + plan.days.size() + " 个学习日", "点任意一天查看并补做");
 976:         final int cur = store.todayIndex(plan);
 977:         for (int i = 0; i < plan.days.size(); i++) {
 978:             final Plan.Day d = plan.days.get(i);
 979:             final int pos = i;
 980:             boolean done = store.completed.contains(d.date);
 981: 
 982:             LinearLayout row = new LinearLayout(this);
 983:             row.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
 984:             row.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER_VERTICAL);
 985:             row.setPadding(0, dp(11), 0, dp(11));
 986: 
 987:             View bar = new View(this);
 988:             GradientDrawable bg = new GradientDrawable();
 989:             bg.setCornerRadius(dp(2));
 990:             bg.setColor(done ? C_GREEN : (i == cur ? C_PURPLE : C_BG3));
 991:             bar.setBackground(bg);
 992:             LinearLayout.LayoutParams blp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(dp(3), dp(30));
 993:             blp.rightMargin = dp(11);
 994:             bar.setLayoutParams(blp);
 995:             row.addView(bar);
 996: 
 997:             LinearLayout col = new LinearLayout(this);
 998:             col.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
 999:             col.setLayoutParams(new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
1000:                     0, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f));
1001: 
1002:             TextView a = new TextView(this);
1003:             a.setText(d.date + "  周" + d.weekday);
1004:             a.setTextColor(i == cur ? C_FG : C_FG2);
1005:             a.setTextSize(13.5f);
1006:             if (i == cur) a.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
1007:             col.addView(a);
1008: 
1009:             TextView bx = new TextView(this);
1010:             String st = "第 " + (i + 1) + " 天";
1011:             if (!TextUtils.isEmpty(d.phase)) st += " · " + d.phase;
1012:             st += " · " + d.tasks.size() + " 任务";
1013:             if (!d.reviews.isEmpty()) st += " · " + d.reviews.size() + " 检索";
1014:             if (done) st += "   ✓ 已确认";
1015:             else if (i == cur) st += "   ← 当前";
1016:             bx.setText(st);
1017:             bx.setTextColor(done ? C_GREEN : C_FG3);
1018:             bx.setTextSize(11.5f);
1019:             bx.setPadding(0, dp(2), 0, 0);
1020:             col.addView(bx);
1021: 
1022:             row.addView(col);
1023: 
1024:             row.setOnClickListener(v -> { idx = pos; tab = 0; stopTimer(); render(); });
1025:             card.addView(row);
1026: 
1027:             if (i < plan.days.size() - 1) {
1028:                 View sep = new View(this);
1029:                 sep.setBackgroundColor(C_LINE);
1030:                 card.addView(sep, new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
1031:                         ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, dp(1)));
1032:             }
1033:         }
1034:         content.addView(card);
1035:     }

1037:     // ══ 词汇塔 ══════════════════════════════════════════
1038:     private void renderGame() {
1039:         ViewGroup parent = (ViewGroup) gameView.getParent();
1040:         if (parent != null) parent.removeView(gameView);
1041:         gameView.setLayoutParams(new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
1042:                 ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
1043:                 ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT));
1044:         content.addView(gameView);
1045:         gameView.show();
1046:     }

1048:     // ══ 数据 ══════════════════════════════════════════════
1049:     private void renderStats() {
1050:         int total = plan.days.size();
1051:         int conf = store.completed.size();
1052:         int taskDone = store.done.size();
1053: 
1054:         LinearLayout c1 = card("总览", null);
1055:         c1.addView(statRow("学习日总数", total + " 天"));
1056:         c1.addView(statRow("已确认完成", conf + " 天"));
1057:         c1.addView(statRow("剩余", store.daysLeft(plan) + " 天"));
1058:         c1.addView(statRow("已勾选条目", taskDone + " 项"));
1059:         c1.addView(statRow("错题库", store.hard.size() + " 项"));
1060:         c1.addView(statRow("考试日", plan.meta.examDate));
1061:         content.addView(c1);
1062: 
1063:         // 常驻工具入口：数学和英语的能力不该只在某一天的任务里出现。
1064:         // 数学题库已经删了（用户不要），换成能画图、能查公式表的工具。
1065:         LinearLayout tools = card("学习工具", "随时可用，不用等到某一天的任务");
1066:         tools.addView(wideBtn("📈 函数图像（可叠导数曲线、看切线）", () -> mathView.open(0)));
1067:         tools.addView(wideBtn("📋 求导公式对照表（40 条，点一条就画图）", () -> mathView.open(1)));
1068:         tools.addView(wideBtn("✍ 求导练习（写答案，程序判等价）", () -> mathView.open(2)));
1069:         tools.addView(wideBtn("🔤 英语五练（英译中/中译英/拼写/填空/词性）", () -> engView.open()));
1070:         tools.addView(wideBtn("🔄 线性变换（2×2 矩阵怎么拉扯平面、特征方向）",
1071:                 () -> linalgView.open()));
1072:         content.addView(tools);
1073: 
1074:         // 账号卡：登录后进度才上云。
1075:         // 放在「数据管理」前面，因为它决定了下面那些手动导出还要不要用。
1076:         LinearLayout cAcc = card("账号", "登录后进度自动存到云端，换手机不会丢");
1077:         final TextView btnAcc = wideBtn("", null);
1078:         final Runnable refreshAcc = () -> {
1079:             Account acc = Account.load(this);
1080:             btnAcc.setText(acc.loggedIn() ? "已登录：" + acc.username : "登录 / 注册");
1081:         };
1082:         refreshAcc.run();
1083:         btnAcc.setOnClickListener(v -> {
1084:             Account acc = Account.load(this);
1085:             if (acc.loggedIn()) {
1086:                 new AlertDialog.Builder(this)
1087:                         .setTitle("账号")
1088:                         .setMessage("当前用户：" + acc.username
1089:                                 + "\n\n退出登录不会清掉这台手机上的进度，"
1090:                                 + "只是停止上传。重新登录同一个用户名就能接着同步。")
1091:                         .setNegativeButton("关闭", null)
1092:                         .setPositiveButton("退出登录", (x, y) -> {
1093:                             Account.clear(this);
1094:                             refreshAcc.run();
1095:                             toast("已退出登录");
1096:                         })
1097:                         .show();
1098:             } else {
1099:                 LoginView lv = new LoginView(this);
1100:                 lv.setOnLoggedIn(a -> {
1101:                     refreshAcc.run();
1102:                     toast("欢迎，" + a.username);
1103:                 });
1104:                 lv.show();
1105:             }
1106:         });
1107:         cAcc.addView(btnAcc);
1108:         cAcc.addView(wideBtn("排行榜", () -> {
1109:             Account acc = Account.load(this);
1110:             if (!acc.loggedIn()) { toast("先登录才能看排行榜"); return; }
1111:             boardView.show();
1112:         }));
1113:         content.addView(cAcc);
1114: 
1115:         LinearLayout c2 = card("数据管理", "全部数据只存在这台手机上，不上传任何服务器");
1116:         c2.addView(wideBtn("生成进度码", () -> {
1117:             final String code = ProgressCode.encode(store.exportSnapshot());
1118:             final String json = store.exportJson();
1119:             if (code == null) { toast("生成失败"); return; }
1120:             new AlertDialog.Builder(this)
1121:                     .setTitle("进度码")
1122:                     .setMessage("这是一串可以带走的进度。换手机或重装后，"
1123:                             + "在新手机上点「导入进度码」粘进去，进度全部回来。\n\n"
1124:                             + "长度 " + code.length() + " 字符，含校验位，抄错会直接报错。")
1125:                     .setPositiveButton("复制进度码", (x, y) -> {
1126:                         ClipboardManager cm = (ClipboardManager) getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE);
1127:                         cm.setPrimaryClip(ClipData.newPlainText("zsb-progress", code));
1128:                         toast("进度码已复制");
1129:                     })
1130:                     .setNeutralButton("复制完整备份", (x, y) -> {
1131:                         ClipboardManager cm = (ClipboardManager) getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE);
1132:                         cm.setPrimaryClip(ClipData.newPlainText("zsb-backup", json));
1133:                         toast("完整 JSON 已复制（" + json.length() + " 字符）");
1134:                     })
1135:                     .setNegativeButton("关闭", null)
1136:                     .show();
1137:         }));
1138: 
1139:         c2.addView(wideBtn("导入进度码", () -> {
1140:             final EditText et = new EditText(this);
1141:             et.setHint("粘贴进度码（ZSB1-…）或完整备份 JSON");
1142:             et.setHintTextColor(C_FG3);
1143:             et.setTextColor(C_FG);
1144:             et.setTextSize(13);
1145:             et.setMinLines(4);
1146:             et.setInputType(InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT | InputType.TYPE_TEXT_FLAG_MULTI_LINE);
1147:             new AlertDialog.Builder(this)
1148:                     .setTitle("导入进度")
1149:                     .setMessage("会覆盖当前全部进度。空白和换行不影响，小写也行。")
1150:                     .setView(et)
1151:                     .setNegativeButton("取消", null)
1152:                     .setPositiveButton("导入", (x, y) -> {
1153:                         String in = et.getText().toString().trim();
1154:                         if (in.isEmpty()) { toast("没有内容"); return; }
1155:                         // 先当进度码解，不是码就按原始 JSON 处理
1156:                         String json = ProgressCode.decode(in);
1157:                         if (json == null) json = in;
1158:                         try {
1159:                             int n = store.importJson(json);
1160:                             idx = store.todayIndex(plan);
1161:                             render();
1162:                             toast("已导入 " + n + " 条勾选");
1163:                         } catch (Exception e) {
1164:                             toast("导入失败：内容无效（进度码可能抄错）");
1165:                         }
1166:                     }).show();
1167:         }));
1168: 
1169:         // 检查更新：按钮文案直接带版本号，省得用户不知道自己装的是哪一版。
1170:         // 首次渲染显示本机版本，异步查到线上版本后追加「最新 vX.Y.Z」或
1171:         // 「可升级 vX.Y.Z」。点一下重新查并弹窗（check(false)）。
1172:         final Button btnUpd = wideBtn("检查更新 · 当前 v" + updater.currentVersion(),
1173:                 () -> updater.check(false));
1174:         c2.addView(btnUpd);
1175:         updater.queryLatest(true, (tag, code) -> {
1176:             String local = updater.currentVersion();
1177:             if (tag == null) {
1178:                 btnUpd.setText("检查更新 · 当前 v" + local + "（线上查询失败，点击重试）");
1179:             } else if (code > Update.parseVer(local)) {
1180:                 btnUpd.setText("检查更新 · 当前 v" + local + " → 可升级 v" + tag);
1181:             } else {
1182:                 btnUpd.setText("检查更新 · 当前 v" + local + "（已是最新）");
1183:             }
1184:         });
1185:         c2.addView(wideBtn("清空全部进度", () -> new AlertDialog.Builder(this)
1186:                 .setMessage("清空全部勾选、默写和计时？无法撤销。")
1187:                 .setNegativeButton("取消", null)
1188:                 .setPositiveButton("清空", (x, y) -> {
1189:                     store.resetAll();
1190:                     idx = store.todayIndex(plan);
1191:                     render();
1192:                     toast("已清空");
1193:                 }).show()));
1194:         content.addView(c2);
1195: 
1196:         LinearLayout c3 = card("这套计划的依据", null);
1197:         TextView tv = new TextView(this);
1198:         tv.setText("· 检索阶梯 R1 +4天 / R2 +18天 / R3 +53天 / R4 +133天，"
1199:                 + "按 Cepeda 2008 的最优间隔比例（考试延迟的 8.5%–17%）换算。\n"
1200:                 + "· 第二轮第三轮都是做题而不是重读，依据 Dunlosky 2013 对 10 种学习法的评级。\n"
1201:                 + "· 日期跟真实日历走：今天是哪天就显示哪天的任务。没学完的会一直留着，"
1202:                 + "勾选完成后这部分天数才从「剩余」里减掉。\n"
1203:                 + "· 每页耗时是估算值，跑一周后要用真实数据重算整张表。");
1204:         tv.setTextColor(C_FG3);
1205:         tv.setTextSize(12.5f);
1206:         tv.setLineSpacing(dp(3), 1f);
1207:         c3.addView(tv);
1208:         content.addView(c3);
1209:     }

1211:     // ── 小组件 ────────────────────────────────────────────
1212:     private LinearLayout card(String title, String sub) {
1213:         LinearLayout box = new LinearLayout(this);
1214:         box.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
1215:         box.setBackground(roundRect(C_BG2, dp(14), C_LINE));
1216:         box.setPadding(dp(14), dp(13), dp(14), dp(13));
1217:         LinearLayout.LayoutParams lp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
1218:                 ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT);
1219:         lp.bottomMargin = dp(11);
1220:         box.setLayoutParams(lp);
1221: 
1222:         if (title != null) {
1223:             TextView t = new TextView(this);
1224:             t.setText(title);
1225:             t.setTextColor(C_FG);
1226:             t.setTextSize(15.5f);
1227:             t.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
1228:             box.addView(t);
1229:         }
1230:         if (sub != null) {
1231:             TextView s = new TextView(this);
1232:             s.setText(sub);
1233:             s.setTextColor(C_FG3);
1234:             s.setTextSize(12);
1235:             s.setPadding(0, dp(3), 0, dp(9));
1236:             box.addView(s);
1237:         }
1238:         return box;
1239:     }
1240: 
1241:     private GradientDrawable roundRect(int fill, float radius, int stroke) {
1242:         GradientDrawable g = new GradientDrawable();
1243:         g.setColor(fill);
1244:         g.setCornerRadius(radius);
1245:         if (stroke != 0) g.setStroke(dp(1), stroke);
1246:         return g;
1247:     }

1255:         tv.setPadding(dp(10), dp(40), dp(10), dp(40));
1256:         return tv;
1257:     }

1259:     private View checkRow(String date, String id, String label) {
1260:         LinearLayout row = new LinearLayout(this);
1261:         row.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
1262:         row.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER_VERTICAL);
1263:         row.setPadding(0, dp(9), 0, 0);
1264: 
1265:         final boolean done = store.isDone(date, id);
1266:         final TextView chk = new TextView(this);
1267:         chk.setText(done ? "✓" : "");
1268:         chk.setTextSize(13);
1269:         chk.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
1270:         chk.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
1271:         GradientDrawable g = new GradientDrawable();
1272:         g.setCornerRadius(dp(6));
1273:         g.setStroke(dp(2), done ? C_PURPLE : C_FG3);
1274:         g.setColor(done ? C_PURPLE : Color.TRANSPARENT);
1275:         chk.setBackground(g);
1276:         chk.setLayoutParams(new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(dp(24), dp(24)));
1277:         chk.setOnClickListener(v -> { store.toggleDone(date, id); render(); });
1278:         row.addView(chk);
1279: 
1280:         TextView tv = new TextView(this);
1281:         tv.setText(label);
1282:         tv.setTextColor(C_FG2);
1283:         tv.setTextSize(13.5f);
1284:         tv.setPadding(dp(11), 0, 0, 0);
1285:         row.addView(tv);
1286:         return row;
1287:     }

1342:     private View statRow(String k, String v) {
1343:         LinearLayout row = new LinearLayout(this);
1344:         row.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
1345:         row.setPadding(0, dp(7), 0, dp(7));
1346: 
1347:         TextView a = new TextView(this);
1348:         a.setText(k);
1349:         a.setTextColor(C_FG3);
1350:         a.setTextSize(13);
1351:         a.setLayoutParams(new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
1352:                 0, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f));
1353:         row.addView(a);
1354: 
1355:         TextView b = new TextView(this);
1356:         b.setText(v);
1357:         b.setTextColor(C_FG);
1358:         b.setTextSize(13.5f);
1359:         b.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
1360:         row.addView(b);
1361:         return row;
1362:     }
1363: 
1364:     // ── 计时 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
1365:     /** 计时中那个任务的文字标签，每秒刷新它显示已计时长 */
1366:     private TextView timingLabel;
1367: 
1368:     private void startTimer(String key, TextView label) {
1369:         stopTimer();
1370:         timingKey = key;
1371:         timingStart = System.currentTimeMillis();
1372:         timingLabel = label;
1373:         tickRun = new Runnable() {
1374:             @Override public void run() {
1375:                 if (timingKey == null) return;
1376:                 // 每秒只刷新那一行文字，不重画整页（重画会丢焦点、闪屏）
1377:                 if (timingLabel != null) {
1378:                     long el = System.currentTimeMillis() - timingStart
1379:                             + store.timingOf(dateOfKey(timingKey), idOfKey(timingKey));
1380:                     timingLabel.setText("已计时 " + fmtDur(el));
1381:                 }
1382:                 tickHandler.postDelayed(this, 1000);
1383:             }
1384:         };
1385:         tickHandler.postDelayed(tickRun, 1000);
1386:     }
1387: 
1388:     private static String dateOfKey(String k) {
1389:         int i = k.indexOf('|');
1390:         return i < 0 ? "" : k.substring(0, i);
1391:     }
1392: 
1393:     private static String idOfKey(String k) {
1394:         int i = k.indexOf('|');
1395:         return i < 0 ? "" : k.substring(i + 1);
1396:     }
1397: 
1398:     private void stopTimer() {
1399:         if (timingKey != null) {
1400:             long el = System.currentTimeMillis() - timingStart;
1401:             if (el > 1000) {
1402:                 store.addTiming(dateOfKey(timingKey), idOfKey(timingKey), el);
1403:             }
1404:         }
1405:         timingKey = null;
1406:         timingStart = 0;
1407:         timingLabel = null;
1408:         if (tickRun != null) tickHandler.removeCallbacks(tickRun);
1409:         tickRun = null;
1410:     }
1411: 
1412:     @Override
1413:     protected void onPause() {
1414:         super.onPause();
1415:         // 切后台先把计时结掉，避免手机锁屏后时间白跑。
1416:         // 这里不调 render()——Activity 已经不可见了，重建视图纯属浪费，
1417:         // 而且会把输入框里的未提交文本冲掉。回到前台时 onResume 会重画。
1418:         stopTimer();
1419:         store.flush();
1420:     }
1421: 
1422:     @Override
1423:     protected void onResume() {
1424:         super.onResume();
1425:         // 从后台回来：刷新一次，把后台期间不该累计的计时清掉并重画
1426:         if (plan != null && store != null) render();
1427:     }
1428: 
1429:     private void renderHeaderOnly() {
1430:         tvDays.setText(String.valueOf(store.daysLeft(plan)));
1431:         tvLeftLabel.setText("已确认 " + store.completed.size() + " / " + plan.days.size() + " 天");
1432:     }
1433: 
1434:     // ── 工具 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
1435:     private String readAsset(String name) {
1436:         try {
1437:             BufferedReader r = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(
1438:                     getAssets().open(name), "UTF-8"));
1439:             StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1440:             String line;
1441:             while ((line = r.readLine()) != null) sb.append(line).append('\n');
1442:             r.close();
1443:             return sb.toString();
1444:         } catch (Exception e) {
1445:             throw new RuntimeException("读不到 assets/" + name + "：" + e.getMessage(), e);
1446:         }
1447:     }
1448: 
1449:     private int dp(float v) {
1450:         return Math.round(TypedValue.applyDimension(
1451:                 TypedValue.COMPLEX_UNIT_DIP, v, getResources().getDisplayMetrics()));
1452:     }
1453: 
1454:     private String fmtDur(long ms) {
1455:         if (ms < 1000) return "";
1456:         long s = Math.round(ms / 1000.0);
1457:         long m = s / 60;
1458:         if (m < 1) return s + " 秒";
1459:         if (m < 60) return m + " 分";
1460:         return (m / 60) + " 小时 " + (m % 60) + " 分";
1461:     }
1462: 
1463:     private void toast(String s) {
1464:         Toast.makeText(this, s, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
1465:     }
1466: }
