 210:     // ══ 输入辅助：按键板 + 模板 ═══════════════════════════
 211:     /**
 212:      * 符号按键板。
 213:      *
 214:      * 手机上敲 x^2 要切三次符号页，敲 sqrt 会被联想改成别的词。
 215:      * 这排按键按一下就插到光标处，插完光标停在括号里。
 216:      * 插入逻辑全在 ExprInput（电脑上 52 项断言全过），这里只负责画。
 217:      */
 218:     private LinearLayout buildKeypad() {
 219:         LinearLayout wrap = new LinearLayout(act);
 220:         wrap.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
 221:         wrap.setPadding(0, dp(2), 0, dp(6));
 222: 
 223:         final int perRow = 6;
 224:         keyPage = 0;
 225:         keyWrap = wrap;
 226:         buildKeysInto(wrap, perRow);
 227:         return wrap;
 228:     }
 229: 
 230:     /** 按当前页重建按键。切页时清空重画。 */
 231:     private void buildKeysInto(LinearLayout wrap, int perRow) {
 232:         wrap.removeAllViews();
 233:         // 补齐到整行：否则最后一行只剩两个键、右半边空着
 234:         final String[][] keys = ExprInput.paddedKeys(perRow, keyPage);
 235:         LinearLayout row = null;
 236:         for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
 237:             if (i % perRow == 0) {
 238:                 row = new LinearLayout(act);
 239:                 row.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
 240:                 LinearLayout.LayoutParams rlp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 241:                         ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
 242:                         ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT);
 243:                 if (i > 0) rlp.topMargin = dp(5);
 244:                 row.setLayoutParams(rlp);
 245:                 wrap.addView(row);
 246:             }
 247:             final String[] key = keys[i];
 248:             if (ExprInput.isPad(key)) {
 249:                 // 占位键：透明占位，保持列对齐
 250:                 View pad = new View(act);
 251:                 LinearLayout.LayoutParams plp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 252:                         0, dp(36), 1f);
 253:                 plp.leftMargin = dp(2);
 254:                 plp.rightMargin = dp(2);
 255:                 pad.setLayoutParams(plp);
 256:                 row.addView(pad);
 257:                 continue;
 258:             }
 259:             boolean isBack = "\b".equals(key[1]);
 260:             TextView b = new TextView(act);
 261:             b.setText(key[0]);
 262:             b.setTextSize(isBack ? 15f : 14f);
 263:             b.setTextColor(isBack ? C_RED : C_FG);
 264:             b.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 265:             b.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
 266:             b.setPadding(0, dp(9), 0, dp(9));
 267:             // 按下要变样，否则手指按下去没有任何反馈，
 268:             // 用户根本不知道点中没有 —— 实机反馈就是这个问题。
 269:             b.setBackground(pressable(dp(7)));
 270:             b.setClickable(true);
 271:             b.setFocusable(true);
 272: 
 273:             LinearLayout.LayoutParams lp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 274:                     0, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f);
 275:             lp.leftMargin = dp(2);
 276:             lp.rightMargin = dp(2);
 277:             b.setLayoutParams(lp);
 278:             b.setOnClickListener(v -> applyKey(key[1]));
 279:             row.addView(b);
 280:         }
 281:     }
 282: 
 283:     /** 常用函数模板：点一下整条替换输入框，直接画。 */
 284:     private LinearLayout buildTemplates() {
 285:         LinearLayout wrap = new LinearLayout(act);
 286:         wrap.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
 287:         wrap.setPadding(0, 0, 0, dp(6));
 288: 
 289:         TextView t = new TextView(act);
 290:         t.setText("常用图形（点一下直接画）");
 291:         t.setTextSize(11);
 292:         t.setTextColor(C_FG3);
 293:         t.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 294:         t.setPadding(0, dp(4), 0, dp(5));
 295:         wrap.addView(t);
 296: 
 297:         // 3 列而不是 4 列。
 298:         //
 299:         // 4 列时每格只有约 75dp 可用宽，"(x+1)(x-1)" 必须缩到 64% 才塞得下，
 300:         // "x³ − 3x" 直接被截断 —— FitAudit 量出来的（74.04/75.0 卡在临界）。
 301:         // 减号两侧的空格是课本排版，不能为了塞进格子去删；
 302:         // 该让格子变宽。3 列每格约 103dp，16 个模板排 6 行，仍然一屏多可见。
 303:         final int perRow = 3;
 304:         LinearLayout row = null;
 305:         for (int i = 0; i < ExprInput.TEMPLATES.length; i++) {
 306:             if (i % perRow == 0) {
 307:                 row = new LinearLayout(act);
 308:                 row.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
 309:                 LinearLayout.LayoutParams rlp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 310:                         ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
 311:                         ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT);
 312:                 if (i > 0) rlp.topMargin = dp(5);
 313:                 row.setLayoutParams(rlp);
 314:                 wrap.addView(row);
 315:             }
 316:             final String expr = ExprInput.TEMPLATES[i];
 317:             final String nm = i < ExprInput.TEMPLATE_NAMES.length
 318:                     ? ExprInput.TEMPLATE_NAMES[i] : "";
 319:             LinearLayout cell = new LinearLayout(act);
 320:             cell.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
 321:             cell.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
 322:             // 上下留够：分式要两行，正弦式只有一行。
 323:             // 按一行的高度给格子，sin(x)/x 会被压扁到看不清分数线。
 324:             cell.setPadding(dp(3), dp(8), dp(3), dp(8));
 325:             cell.setMinimumHeight(dp(46));
 326:             cell.setBackground(pressable(dp(7)));
 327:             cell.setClickable(true);
 328:             cell.setFocusable(true);
 329: 
 330:             // 上排显示已经排好版的公式，下排是图形种类 ——
 331:             // 只写 "x^2" 看不出是抛物线，只写"抛物线"又不知道点了会画什么。
 332:             FormulaView fv = new FormulaView(act);
 333:             fv.setTextSize(12.5f);
 334:             fv.setTextColor(C_FG);
 335:             try { fv.setExpr(expr); } catch (Exception e) { /* 模板都过审计了 */ }
 336:             cell.addView(fv);
 337: 
 338:             if (!nm.isEmpty()) {
 339:                 TextView nt = new TextView(act);
 340:                 nt.setText(nm);
 341:                 nt.setTextSize(9.5f);
 342:                 nt.setTextColor(C_FG3);
 343:                 nt.setPadding(0, dp(1), 0, 0);
 344:                 cell.addView(nt);
 345:             }
 346: 
 347:             // 同一行必须等高，但不能用"固定 62dp"来实现。
 348:             //
 349:             // 走过的两条弯路：
 350:             //   ① 一开始用 WRAP_CONTENT —— 分式格子（1/x、sin x/x、1/(1+x²)）
 351:             //      内容两行，单行格子只占一行，一行之内高矮不齐 = "一大一小"；
 352:             //   ② 改成固定 62dp —— 等高是等高了，但分式格子"公式两行 + 说明
 353:             //      一行"塞不进 62dp，底下的"双曲线""sinc"被裁掉半截（实机截图）。
 354:             //
 355:             // 正确做法：先按内容量出这一行最高需要多少，再把这一行所有格子
 356:             // 都设成那个高度。等高和"不裁字"同时成立。
 357:             cell.setMinimumHeight(dp(TEMPLATE_CELL_H_MIN));
 358:             LinearLayout.LayoutParams lp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 359:                     0, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f);
 360:             lp.leftMargin = dp(2);
 361:             lp.rightMargin = dp(2);
 362:             cell.setLayoutParams(lp);
 363:             cell.setOnClickListener(v -> {
 364:                 if (etExpr == null) return;
 365:                 etExpr.setText(expr);
 366:                 etExpr.setSelection(expr.length());
 367:                 exprText = expr;
 368:                 draw();
 369:             });
 370:             row.addView(cell);
 371:         }
 372:         equalizeRows(wrap);
 373:         return wrap;
 374:     }
 375: 
 376:     /** 单行公式也至少这么高，免得格子看起来是根空条。 */
 377:     private static final int TEMPLATE_CELL_H_MIN = 52;
 378: 
 379:     /**
 380:      * 把每一行的格子高度拉齐到该行最高那个。
 381:      *
 382:      * 只在布局完成后才知道真实高度，所以用 post —— view 已经在树里，
 383:      * 这里改 LayoutParams 会触发一次重新布局，用户看不到跳变（首帧即最终态）。
 384:      */
 385:     private void equalizeRows(LinearLayout wrap) {
 386:         wrap.post(() -> {
 387:             for (int r = 0; r < wrap.getChildCount(); r++) {
 388:                 View child = wrap.getChildAt(r);
 389:                 if (!(child instanceof LinearLayout)) continue;
 390:                 LinearLayout row = (LinearLayout) child;
 391:                 int max = 0;
 392:                 for (int i = 0; i < row.getChildCount(); i++) {
 393:                     max = Math.max(max, row.getChildAt(i).getHeight());
 394:                 }
 395:                 if (max <= 0) continue;
 396:                 for (int i = 0; i < row.getChildCount(); i++) {
 397:                     View c = row.getChildAt(i);
 398:                     ViewGroup.LayoutParams p = c.getLayoutParams();
 399:                     if (p != null && p.height != max) {
 400:                         p.height = max;
 401:                         c.setLayoutParams(p);
 402:                     }
 403:                 }
 404:             }
 405:         });
 406:     }
 407: 
 408:     /**
 409:      * 「怎么写 → 画出来什么样」对照表。
 410:      *
 411:      * 用户原话："函数图像那个你得写 sqrt 和 abs 和 exp 对照什么，不然谁知道"。
 412:      * 只给模板格子（"sqrt(x)"）等于只给了名字，不知道形状、不知道定义域、
 413:      * 不知道和 x^2 有什么不同 —— 模板再多也用不上。
 414:      *
 415:      * 每条给四样：真缩略曲线（PlotGeom 采样，和主图同一份代码）、
 416:      * 怎么写、中文名、一句形状人话、一句能自己验证的话。
 417:      * 整条可点，点了就填进输入框并直接画出来。
 418:      */
 419:     private LinearLayout buildOutline() {
 420:         LinearLayout wrap = new LinearLayout(act);
 421:         wrap.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
 422:         wrap.setPadding(0, dp(10), 0, dp(4));
 423: 
 424:         TextView t = new TextView(act);
 425:         t.setText("这些写法画出来是什么样");
 426:         t.setTextSize(11);
 427:         t.setTextColor(C_FG3);
 428:         t.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 429:         t.setPadding(0, 0, 0, dp(2));
 430:         wrap.addView(t);
 431: 
 432:         TextView sub = new TextView(act);
 433:         sub.setText("点任意一行直接画。曲线就是它真实的形状，不是示意图。");
 434:         sub.setTextSize(10.5f);
 435:         sub.setTextColor(C_FG3);
 436:         sub.setPadding(0, 0, 0, dp(7));
 437:         wrap.addView(sub);
 438: 
 439:         ExprOutline.Item[] items = ExprOutline.all();
 440:         int lastGroup = -1;
 441:         for (int i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
 442:             final ExprOutline.Item it = items[i];
 443: 
 444:             int g = ExprOutline.groupOf(i);
 445:             if (g != lastGroup) {
 446:                 lastGroup = g;
 447:                 TextView gh = new TextView(act);
 448:                 gh.setText(ExprOutline.GROUPS[g]);
 449:                 gh.setTextSize(11.5f);
 450:                 gh.setTextColor(C_TEAL);
 451:                 gh.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 452:                 gh.setPadding(0, dp(i == 0 ? 2 : 9), 0, dp(4));
 453:                 wrap.addView(gh);
 454:             }
 455: 
 456:             wrap.addView(outlineRow(it));
 457:         }
 458:         return wrap;
 459:     }
 460: 
 461:     /** 一条对照：左缩略图，右边写法 + 名字 + 形状 + 自验。整条可点。 */
 462:     private View outlineRow(final ExprOutline.Item it) {
 463:         LinearLayout row = new LinearLayout(act);
 464:         row.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
 465:         row.setPadding(dp(8), dp(8), dp(8), dp(8));
 466:         row.setBackground(pressable(dp(8)));
 467:         row.setClickable(true);
 468:         row.setFocusable(true);
 469: 
 470:         MiniPlot mp = new MiniPlot(act);
 471:         try {
 472:             mp.set(Expr.parse(it.sample), it.color);
 473:         } catch (Exception e) {
 474:             // 数据都过审计了；真出问题也不该整块白屏
 475:         }
 476:         LinearLayout.LayoutParams mlp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 477:                 (int) dp(78), (int) dp(56));
 478:         mlp.rightMargin = dp(10);
 479:         row.addView(mp, mlp);
 480: 
 481:         LinearLayout col = new LinearLayout(act);
 482:         col.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
 483: 
 484:         // 第一行：写法（等宽感，一眼看出该敲什么）
 485:         TextView w = new TextView(act);
 486:         w.setText(it.write);
 487:         w.setTextSize(14);
 488:         w.setTextColor(C_FG);
 489:         w.setTypeface(Typeface.MONOSPACE, Typeface.BOLD);
 490:         col.addView(w);
 491: 
 492:         // 第二行：中文名
 493:         TextView nm = new TextView(act);
 494:         nm.setText(it.name);
 495:         nm.setTextSize(11.5f);
 496:         nm.setTextColor(it.color);
 497:         nm.setPadding(0, dp(1), 0, 0);
 498:         col.addView(nm);
 499: 
 500:         // 第三行：形状（这才是用户真正缺的）
 501:         TextView sh = new TextView(act);
 502:         sh.setText(it.shape);
 503:         sh.setTextSize(11);
 504:         sh.setTextColor(C_FG2);
 505:         sh.setPadding(0, dp(4), 0, 0);
 506:         col.addView(sh);
 507: 
 508:         // 第四行：自己能验的一句话
 509:         TextView ask = new TextView(act);
 510:         ask.setText("试试：" + it.ask);
 511:         ask.setTextSize(10.5f);
 512:         ask.setTextColor(C_FG3);
 513:         ask.setPadding(0, dp(3), 0, 0);
 514:         col.addView(ask);
 515: 
 516:         row.addView(col, new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 517:                 0, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f));
 518: 
 519:         LinearLayout.LayoutParams rlp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 520:                 ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT);
 521:         rlp.topMargin = dp(4);
 522:         row.setLayoutParams(rlp);
 523: 
 524:         row.setOnClickListener(v -> {
 525:             if (etExpr == null) return;
 526:             etExpr.setText(it.write);
 527:             etExpr.setSelection(it.write.length());
 528:             exprText = it.write;
 529:             draw();
 530:         });
 531:         return row;
 532:     }
 533: 
 534: 
 535:     /** 按一个键：插到光标处（或替换选区），然后刷新。 */
 536:     private void applyKey(String frag) {
 537:         if (etExpr == null) return;
 538: 
 539:         // 翻页：不插入任何内容，只换一张键盘
 540:         if (ExprInput.SWITCH.equals(frag)) {
 541:             keyPage = 1;
 542:             buildKeysInto(keyWrap, 6);
 543:             return;
 544:         }
 545:         if (ExprInput.BACK_TO_NUM.equals(frag)) {
 546:             keyPage = 0;
 547:             buildKeysInto(keyWrap, 6);
 548:             return;
 549:         }
 550:         int a = etExpr.getSelectionStart();
 551:         int b = etExpr.getSelectionEnd();
 552:         // Android 的 getSelectionStart 在极端情况下会返回 -1
 553:         if (a < 0) a = etExpr.getText().length();
 554:         if (b < 0) b = a;
 555: 
 556:         ExprInput.R r = ExprInput.insert(etExpr.getText().toString(), a, b, frag);
 557:         etExpr.setText(r.text);
 558:         int pos = Math.max(0, Math.min(r.cursor, etExpr.getText().length()));
 559:         etExpr.setSelection(pos);
 560: 
 561:         // 括号没闭上就补一下，省得用户数括号
 562:         int un = ExprInput.unclosedParens(r.text);
 563:         if (un != 0) updateKeyHint(un);
 564:         else updateKeyHint(0);
 565:         if (!"\b".equals(frag)) {
 566:             // 插入后立刻重画，边写边看
 567:             exprText = etExpr.getText().toString();
 568:             draw();
 569:         }
 570:     }
 571: 
 572:     private TextView keyHint;
 573:     private int keyPage;              // 0=数字页 1=函数页
 574:     private LinearLayout keyWrap;     // 按键容器，切页时重画
 575: 
 576:     private void updateKeyHint(int unclosed) {
 577:         if (keyHint == null) return;
 578:         if (unclosed > 0) {
 579:             keyHint.setText("缺 " + unclosed + " 个右括号");
 580:             keyHint.setTextColor(C_AMBER);
 581:         } else if (unclosed < 0) {
 582:             keyHint.setText("右括号多了 " + (-unclosed) + " 个");
 583:             keyHint.setTextColor(C_RED);
 584:         } else {
 585:             keyHint.setText("");
 586:         }
 587:     }
 588: 
 589:     // ══ ① 函数图像 ═══════════════════════════════════════
 590:     private void renderPlot() {
 591:         LinearLayout c1 = card("函数表达式");
 592: 
 593:         EditText et = new EditText(act);
 594:         et.setText(exprText);
 595:         et.setTextColor(C_FG);
 596:         et.setTextSize(15);
 597:         et.setBackgroundColor(C_BG3);
 598:         et.setPadding(dp(10), dp(8), dp(10), dp(8));
 599:         et.setMinHeight(dp(42));
 600:         // setSingleLine(true) 会把 EditText 的高度按"单行无内边距"算死，
 601:         // 但这里上下各有 8dp 内边距 —— 结果输入框比内容矮，文字顶出
 602:         // 自己的框，压到下面那行提示（实机截图里"右括号多了 1 个"
 603:         // 被上一个框盖住半截）。
 604:         // 用 maxLines=1 + 显式最小高度，既保证只输一行，又保证框够高。
 605:         et.setMaxLines(1);
 606:         et.setInputType(InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT
 607:                 | InputType.TYPE_TEXT_FLAG_NO_SUGGESTIONS);
 608:         // 数学表达式不该被当作密码/邮箱去猜，给个纯 ASCII 的键盘
 609:         et.setPrivateImeOptions("nm");
 610:         etExpr = et;
 611:         c1.addView(et);
 612: 
 613:         TextView hint = new TextView(act);
 614:         hint.setText("可以写 x、pi、e；sin cos tan ln lg sqrt abs exp；2x、3sin(x)、(x+1)(x-1) 都认。"
 615:                 + "\n下面有按键，不用切输入法符号页。");
 616:         hint.setTextSize(11);
 617:         hint.setTextColor(C_FG3);
 618:         hint.setPadding(0, dp(6), 0, dp(8));
 619:         c1.addView(hint);
 620: 
 621:         keyHint = new TextView(act);
 622:         keyHint.setTextSize(11);
 623:         keyHint.setTextColor(C_AMBER);
 624:         keyHint.setPadding(0, dp(2), 0, 0);
 625:         c1.addView(keyHint);
 626:         updateKeyHint(ExprInput.unclosedParens(exprText));
 627: 
 628:         c1.addView(buildKeypad());
 629:         c1.addView(buildTemplates());
 630:         c1.addView(buildOutline());
 631: 
 632:         LinearLayout row1 = new LinearLayout(act);
 633:         row1.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
 634:         TextView go = btn("画出来", C_BG);
 635:         GradientDrawable gg = new GradientDrawable();
 636:         gg.setCornerRadius(dp(8));
 637:         gg.setColor(C_TEAL);
 638:         go.setBackground(gg);
 639:         go.setOnClickListener(v -> {
 640:             exprText = etExpr.getText().toString();
 641:             draw();
 642:         });
 643:         row1.addView(go, new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0,
 644:                 ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f));
 645: 
 646:         TextView reset = btn("复位", C_FG2);
 647:         reset.setOnClickListener(v -> {
 648:             if (plot != null) { plot.resetView(); draw(); }
 649:         });
 650:         LinearLayout.LayoutParams rlp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 651:                 0, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f);
 652:         rlp.leftMargin = dp(8);
 653:         reset.setLayoutParams(rlp);
 654:         row1.addView(reset);
 655:         c1.addView(row1);
 656: 
 657:         LinearLayout row2 = new LinearLayout(act);
 658:         row2.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
 659:         row2.setPadding(0, dp(8), 0, 0);
 660:         TextView zin = btn("放大 +", C_FG2);
 661:         zin.setOnClickListener(v -> { if (plot != null) plot.zoomBy(0.6); });
 662:         row2.addView(zin, new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0,
 663:                 ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f));
 664:         TextView zout = btn("缩小 −", C_FG2);
 665:         LinearLayout.LayoutParams zlp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 666:                 0, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f);
 667:         zlp.leftMargin = dp(8);
 668:         zout.setLayoutParams(zlp);
 669:         zout.setOnClickListener(v -> { if (plot != null) plot.zoomBy(1.6); });
 670:         row2.addView(zout);
 671:         c1.addView(row2);
 672:         body.addView(c1);
 673: 
 674:         // 画布
 675:         LinearLayout c2 = new LinearLayout(act);
 676:         c2.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
 677:         GradientDrawable g2 = new GradientDrawable();
 678:         g2.setCornerRadius(dp(12));
 679:         g2.setColor(0xFF101319);
 680:         c2.setBackground(g2);
 681:         c2.setClipToOutline(true);
 682:         LinearLayout.LayoutParams c2lp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 683:                 ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, dp(300));
 684:         c2lp.bottomMargin = dp(10);
 685:         c2.setLayoutParams(c2lp);
 686: 
 687:         plot = new PlotView(act);
 688:         plot.setLayoutParams(new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 689:                 ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
 690:                 ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT));
 691:         c2.addView(plot);
 692:         body.addView(c2);
 693: 
 694:         // 显示开关
 695:         LinearLayout c3 = card("显示");
 696:         LinearLayout switches = new LinearLayout(act);
 697:         switches.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
 698: 
 699:         final TextView swD = btn("导数曲线 f′", C_FG3);
 700:         swD.setOnClickListener(v -> {
 701:             if (plot == null) return;
 702:             boolean nv = !plot.isShowDeriv();
 703:             plot.setShowDeriv(nv);
 704:             swD.setTextColor(nv ? C_AMBER : C_FG3);
 705:             updateInfo();
 706:         });
 707:         switches.addView(swD, new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0,
 708:                 ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f));
 709: 
 710:         final TextView swT = btn("切线（点图选点）", C_FG3);
 711:         LinearLayout.LayoutParams slp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 712:                 0, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f);
 713:         slp.leftMargin = dp(8);
 714:         swT.setLayoutParams(slp);
 715:         swT.setOnClickListener(v -> {
 716:             if (plot == null) return;
 717:             boolean nv = !plot.isShowTangent();
 718:             plot.setShowTangent(nv);
 719:             swT.setTextColor(nv ? C_PURPLE : C_FG3);
 720:             updateInfo();
 721:         });
 722:         switches.addView(swT);
 723:         c3.addView(switches);
 724: 
 725:         tvPlotInfo = new TextView(act);
 726:         tvPlotInfo.setTextSize(12);
 727:         tvPlotInfo.setTextColor(C_FG2);
 728:         tvPlotInfo.setPadding(0, dp(8), 0, 0);
 729:         tvPlotInfo.setLineSpacing(dp(3), 1f);
 730:         c3.addView(tvPlotInfo);
 731:         body.addView(c3);
 732: 
 733:         // 快捷函数
 734:         LinearLayout c4 = card("常用图像（点一下直接画）");
 735:         HorizontalScrollView hs = new HorizontalScrollView(act);
 736:         hs.setHorizontalScrollBarEnabled(false);
 737:         LinearLayout chips = new LinearLayout(act);
 738:         chips.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
 739:         String[] quicks = {"x^2", "x^3", "1/x", "sqrt(x)", "ln(x)", "exp(x)",
 740:                 "sin(x)", "cos(x)", "tan(x)", "abs(x)", "x^2-4", "sin(x)/x",
 741:                 "x^x", "atan(x)"};
 742:         for (String q : quicks) {
 743:             TextView chip = btn(q, C_TEAL);
 744:             LinearLayout.LayoutParams clp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 745:                     ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT);
 746:             clp.rightMargin = dp(6);
 747:             chip.setLayoutParams(clp);
 748:             chip.setOnClickListener(v -> {
 749:                 exprText = q;
 750:                 if (etExpr != null) etExpr.setText(q);
 751:                 draw();
 752:             });
 753:             chips.addView(chip);
 754:         }
 755:         hs.addView(chips);
 756:         c4.addView(hs);
 757:         body.addView(c4);
 758: 
 759:         draw();
 760: 
 761:         for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) if (false) tabBtn[i].setText("");
 762:         if (swD != null && plot != null && plot.isShowDeriv()) swD.setTextColor(C_AMBER);
 763:         if (swT != null && plot != null && plot.isShowTangent()) swT.setTextColor(C_PURPLE);
 764: 
 765:         plot.setOnPick((x, y, slope) -> updateInfo());
 766:     }
 767: 
 768:     private void draw() {
 769:         if (plot == null) return;
 770:         String err = plot.setExpr(exprText);
 771:         if (err != null) {
 772:             if (tvPlotInfo != null) {
 773:                 tvPlotInfo.setTextColor(C_RED);
 774:                 tvPlotInfo.setText("画不出来：" + err);
 775:             }
 776:             return;
 777:         }
 778:         updateInfo();
 779:     }
 780: 
 781:     private void updateInfo() {
 782:         if (tvPlotInfo == null || plot == null) return;
 783:         Expr.Node f = plot.expr();
 784:         if (f == null) { tvPlotInfo.setText("还没输入函数"); return; }
 785: 
 786:         StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder();
 787:         b.append("f(x) = ").append(exprText);
 788:         Expr.Node d = plot.deriv();
 789:         if (d != null) b.append("\nf′(x) = ").append(Expr.toText(d));
 790: 
 791:         List<Double> rs = PlotGeom.roots(f, plot.window(), 600);
 792:         if (!rs.isEmpty()) {
 793:             b.append("\n零点：");
 794:             for (int i = 0; i < rs.size() && i < 5; i++) {
 795:                 if (i > 0) b.append("，");
 796:                 b.append("x=").append(Expr.fmtNum(rs.get(i)));
 797:             }
 798:             if (rs.size() > 5) b.append(" 等 ").append(rs.size()).append(" 个");
 799:         } else {
 800:             b.append("\n零点：本窗口内没有");
 801:         }
 802: 
 803:         Double tx = plot.tangentX();
 804:         if (tx != null && plot.isShowTangent()) {
 805:             double y = PlotGeom.safeEval(f, tx);
 806:             double k = PlotGeom.slope(f, tx);
 807:             b.append("\n点 x=").append(Expr.fmtNum(tx))
 808:                     .append("：f(x)=").append(Expr.fmtNum(y))
 809:                     .append("，f′=").append(Expr.fmtNum(k));
 810:             if (PlotGeom.isFinite(k) && Math.abs(k) > 1e-9) {
 811:                 b.append("，切线 y=").append(Expr.fmtNum(k)).append("x");
 812:                 double c = y - k * tx;
 813:                 if (c >= 0) b.append("+").append(Expr.fmtNum(c));
 814:                 else b.append("−").append(Expr.fmtNum(-c));
 815:             } else if (PlotGeom.isFinite(k)) {
 816:                 b.append("，切线水平 y=").append(Expr.fmtNum(y));
 817:             }
 818:         }
 819:         tvPlotInfo.setTextColor(C_FG2);
 820:         tvPlotInfo.setText(b.toString());
 821:     }
 822: 
 823:     // ══ ② 求导公式对照表 ═════════════════════════════════
 824:     private void renderTable() {
 825:         TextView intro = new TextView(act);
 826:         intro.setText("点任意一行 → 直接画它的图像，并叠出它的导数曲线。"
 827:                 + "\n表里每一条都由 MathTest 用「符号求导 + 数值差分」双重验证过，抄错会当场报错。");
 828:         intro.setTextSize(11.5f);
 829:         intro.setTextColor(C_FG3);
 830:         intro.setPadding(dp(4), 0, dp(4), dp(10));
 831:         intro.setLineSpacing(dp(3), 1f);
 832:         body.addView(intro);
 833: 
 834:         Map<String, List<DerivTable.Row>> groups = DerivTable.grouped();
 835:         for (Map.Entry<String, List<DerivTable.Row>> e : groups.entrySet()) {
 836:             LinearLayout c = card(e.getKey() + " · " + e.getValue().size() + " 条");
 837:             for (DerivTable.Row r : e.getValue()) c.addView(rowView(r));
 838:             body.addView(c);
 839:         }
 840:     }
 841: 
 842:     /**
 843:      * 带按下态的圆角背景。
 844:      *
 845:      * 原来每个可点元素都是 GradientDrawable 单色 —— 按下、松手、禁用
 846:      * 长得一模一样，手指按下去屏幕毫无变化。真机上用户会说"点了没反应"，
 847:      * 其实事件是收到的，只是没有任何视觉确认。
 848:      *
 849:      * 用 StateListDrawable 给 pressed 一个明显更亮的底色：
 850:      * 深色界面上 C_BG3 → C_BG4 的差别肉眼能看出来，又不会太跳。
 851:      */
 852:     private android.graphics.drawable.Drawable pressable(final int radius) {
 853:         GradientDrawable normal = new GradientDrawable();
 854:         normal.setCornerRadius(radius);
 855:         normal.setColor(C_BG3);
 856: 
 857:         GradientDrawable pressed = new GradientDrawable();
 858:         pressed.setCornerRadius(radius);
 859:         // 比常态亮一档：深色底上这个差值足够看清，又不至于像选中态
 860:         pressed.setColor(0xFF3C4250);
 861:         // 按下时描一圈青色细边，短按也能看到闪一下
 862:         pressed.setStroke(dp(1), C_TEAL);
 863: 
 864:         android.graphics.drawable.StateListDrawable sd =
 865:                 new android.graphics.drawable.StateListDrawable();
 866:         sd.addState(new int[]{android.R.attr.state_pressed}, pressed);
 867:         sd.addState(new int[]{android.R.attr.state_focused}, pressed);
 868:         sd.addState(new int[]{}, normal);
 869:         return sd;
 870:     }
 871: 
 872:     /**
 873:      * 排好版的公式控件。解析失败就退回纯文本 —— 表里的式子都过审计了，
 874:      * 但界面不该因为一条数据坏了整块白屏。
 875:      */
 876:     private View formula(String expr, int color, float sp) {
 877:         FormulaView fv = new FormulaView(act);
 878:         fv.setTextSize(sp);
 879:         fv.setTextColor(color);
 880:         int padTop = dp(3);
 881:         fv.setPadding(0, padTop, 0, 0);
 882:         try {
 883:             fv.setExpr(expr);
 884:         } catch (Exception e) {
 885:             TextView tv = new TextView(act);
 886:             tv.setText(expr);
 887:             tv.setTextSize(sp);
 888:             tv.setTextColor(color);
 889:             tv.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 890:             return tv;
 891:         }
 892:         return fv;
 893:     }
 894: 
 895:     /** 「↓ 求导」这类过程标注，统一字号与缩进。 */
 896:     private TextView hintLabel(String s) {
 897:         TextView tv = new TextView(act);
 898:         tv.setText(s);
 899:         tv.setTextSize(10.5f);
 900:         tv.setTextColor(C_FG3);
 901:         tv.setPadding(dp(6), dp(2), 0, dp(2));
 902:         return tv;
 903:     }
 904: 
 905:     private View rowView(final DerivTable.Row r) {
 906:         LinearLayout row = new LinearLayout(act);
 907:         row.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
 908:         row.setPadding(dp(8), dp(8), dp(8), dp(8));
 909:         GradientDrawable bg = new GradientDrawable();
 910:         bg.setCornerRadius(dp(8));
 911:         bg.setColor(C_BG3);
 912:         row.setBackground(bg);
 913:         LinearLayout.LayoutParams lp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 914:                 ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT);
 915:         lp.bottomMargin = dp(6);
 916:         row.setLayoutParams(lp);
 917: 
 918:         LinearLayout head = new LinearLayout(act);
 919:         head.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
 920: 
 921:         TextView name = new TextView(act);
 922:         name.setText(r.name);
 923:         name.setTextSize(12);
 924:         name.setTextColor(C_FG3);
 925:         head.addView(name, new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 926:                 0, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f));
 927: 
 928:         TextView go = new TextView(act);
 929:         go.setText("画图 ›");
 930:         go.setTextSize(11.5f);
 931:         go.setTextColor(C_TEAL);
 932:         go.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 933:         go.setPadding(dp(8), 0, dp(2), 0);
 934:         go.setOnClickListener(v -> {
 935:             exprText = r.expr;
 936:             tab = 0;
 937:             render();
 938:         });
 939:         head.addView(go);
 940:         row.addView(head);
 941: 
 942:         // 用 FormulaView 排真公式，不用 Expr.mathText。
 943:         //
 944:         // mathText 是"能粘回输入框的机器写法"，它会原样输出 sqrt(x)、
 945:         // exp(x)、log2(x) —— 用户看的是这个，当然觉得不是人话。
 946:         // FormulaView 走 FormulaLayout，sqrt 会画成根号、exp 画成 e 的
 947:         // 上标、分式画成真的分数线，这才是课本上的样子。
 948:         //
 949:         // 注意 r.expr / r.der 仍是机器可解析的字符串，MathTest 照旧拿它们
 950:         // 做符号求导与数值差分对照 —— 显示换了，审计一点没减弱。
 951:         // 注意这里传的是 r.expr / r.der（机器写法），不是 r.show。
 952:         //
 953:         // FormulaView 自己就会把 sqrt 排成根号、exp 排成 e 的上标 ——
 954:         // 它需要的是能被 Expr 解析的表达式。若传 r.show（"e^(x)"），
 955:         // 里面的 e 会被 Expr 当成欧拉数常量折成 2.718282，
 956:         // 界面上就会出现 "2.718282^x" —— 审计渲染图当场抓到的。
 957:         // r.show / r.showDer 只用于纯文本场合（列表标题、说明）。
 958:         row.addView(formula(r.expr, C_FG, 16f));
 959:         row.addView(hintLabel("↓ 求导"));
 960:         row.addView(formula(r.der, C_AMBER, 16f));
 961: 
 962:         if (!r.note.isEmpty()) {
 963:             TextView note = new TextView(act);
 964:             note.setText("· " + r.note);
 965:             note.setTextSize(11);
 966:             note.setTextColor(C_FG3);
 967:             note.setPadding(0, dp(4), 0, 0);
 968:             row.addView(note);
 969:         }
 970:         return row;
 971:     }
 972: 
 973:     // ══ ③ 求导练习 ═══════════════════════════════════════
 974:     private void renderPractice() {
 975:         if (quizF == null) nextQuiz();
 976: 
 977:         LinearLayout c1 = card("算它的导数");
 978:         TextView tvF = new TextView(act);
 979:         tvF.setText("f(x) =  " + Expr.mathText(quizF));
 980:         tvF.setTextSize(19);
 981:         tvF.setTextColor(C_FG);

 994:         et.setHintTextColor(0xFF4E5565);

1000:         // 带内边距时文字会顶出框外。
1001:         et.setMaxLines(1);

1003:         et.setInputType(InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT);
1004:         c1.addView(et);
1005:

1007:         row.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
1008:         row.setPadding(0, dp(8), 0, 0);
1009:

1011:         GradientDrawable gg = new GradientDrawable();
1012:         gg.setCornerRadius(dp(8));
1013:         gg.setColor(C_TEAL);
1014:         check.setBackground(gg);
1015:         check.setOnClickListener(v -> {
1016:             String in = et.getText().toString();
1017:             quizMsg = judgeDeriv(quizF, in);
1018:             if (quizMsg.startsWith("✓")) quizRight++;
1019:             quizAll++;
1020:             render();
1021:         });
1022:         row.addView(check, new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0,
1023:                 ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f));
1024: 
1025:         TextView skip = btn("换一题", C_FG2);
1026:         LinearLayout.LayoutParams slp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
1027:                 0, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f);
1028:         slp.leftMargin = dp(8);
1029:         skip.setLayoutParams(slp);
1030:         skip.setOnClickListener(v -> { nextQuiz(); quizMsg = ""; render(); });
1031:         row.addView(skip);
1032:         c1.addView(row);
1033:         body.addView(c1);
1034: 
1035:         if (!quizMsg.isEmpty()) {
1036:             LinearLayout c2 = card("结果");
1037:             TextView t = new TextView(act);
1038:             t.setText(quizMsg);
1039:             t.setTextSize(13);
1040:             t.setTextColor(quizMsg.startsWith("✓") ? C_TEAL : C_RED);
1041:             t.setLineSpacing(dp(4), 1f);
1042:             c2.addView(t);
1043:             body.addView(c2);
1044:         }
1045: 
1046:         LinearLayout c3 = card("本轮");
1047:         TextView t3 = new TextView(act);
1048:         t3.setText("做了 " + quizAll + " 题，对 " + quizRight + " 题"
1049:                 + (quizAll > 0 ? "（正确率 " + (quizRight * 100 / quizAll) + "%）" : ""));
1050:         t3.setTextSize(12.5f);
1051:         t3.setTextColor(C_FG2);
1052:         c3.addView(t3);
1053:         body.addView(c3);
1054:     }
1055: 
1056:     /** 随机挑一个"求导有意义且不难看"的函数。 */
1057:     private void nextQuiz() {
1058:         String[] pool = {
1059:                 "x^2", "x^3", "x^4", "sin(x)", "cos(x)", "tan(x)",
1060:                 "exp(x)", "ln(x)", "sqrt(x)", "1/x",
1061:                 "sin(2x)", "cos(3x)", "exp(2x)", "ln(1+x^2)", "sqrt(1+x^2)",
1062:                 "x·sin(x)", "x^2·exp(x)", "sin(x)/x", "x^2-4x", "atan(x)",
1063:                 "x^3-3x", "exp(sin(x))", "tan(x)/2", "2^x"
1064:         };
1065:         int i = (int) (Math.random() * pool.length);
1066:         if (pool[i].equals(quizExpr)) i = (i + 1) % pool.length;
1067:         quizExpr = pool[i];
1068:         try { quizF = Expr.parse(quizExpr); } catch (Exception e) { quizF = Expr.parse("x"); }
1069:     }
1070: 
1071:     /**
1072:      * 判分。不比对文本，比对"数值上是不是同一个函数"：
1073:      * 学生可能写成 (2)·x 或 x·2 或 2 x，字面不同但都对。
1074:      * 做法：在 5 个点上比较他的表达式与标准导数；全部接近才算对。
1075:      * 这样既接受等价写法，也不会把 x 和 2x 判成一样。
1076:      */
1077:     private String judgeDeriv(Expr.Node f, String input) {
1078:         if (input == null || input.trim().isEmpty()) return "还没写";
1079:         Expr.Node mine;
1080:         try {
1081:             mine = Expr.parse(input);
1082:         } catch (Expr.ExprError e) {
1083:             return "表达式看不懂：" + e.getMessage();
1084:         }
1085:         Expr.Node ref = Expr.diff(f);
1086:         String refText = Expr.toText(ref);
1087: 
1088:         double[] xs = {-2.3, -1.1, -0.4, 0.6, 1.4, 2.2};
1089:         int tested = 0;
1090:         for (double x : xs) {
1091:             double a = Expr.eval(ref, x);
1092:             double b = Expr.eval(mine, x);
1093:             if (!PlotGeom.isFinite(a) || !PlotGeom.isFinite(b)) continue;
1094:             if (Math.abs(a) > 1e6 || Math.abs(b) > 1e6) continue;
1095:             tested++;
1096:             double scale = Math.max(1, Math.max(Math.abs(a), Math.abs(b)));
1097:             if (Math.abs(a - b) > 1e-6 * scale) {
1098:                 return "✗ 不对。\n正确答案：" + refText
1099:                         + "\n（你在 x=" + Expr.fmtNum(x) + " 处得到 "
1100:                         + Expr.fmtNum(b) + "，正确是 " + Expr.fmtNum(a) + "）";
1101:             }
1102:         }
1103:         if (tested == 0) {
1104:             return "✗ 无法判定：你的表达式和标准答案没有公共定义域。\n正确答案：" + refText;
1105:         }
1106:         if (Expr.toText(mine).equals(refText)) {
1107:             return "✓ 正确，而且写法和标准答案一致：" + refText;
1108:         }
1109:         return "✓ 正确（写法不同但等价）。\n你写的：" + Expr.toText(mine)
1110:                 + "\n标准写法：" + refText;
1111:     }
1112: }
===== FILE: E:\小黑盒运营\cq-zhuanshengbens\mobile\src\com\zsb\plan\MiniPlot.java =====
   1: package com.zsb.plan;
   2: 
   3: import android.content.Context;
   4: import android.graphics.Canvas;
   5: import android.graphics.Paint;
   6: import android.graphics.Path;
   7: import android.view.View;
   8: 
   9: import java.util.List;
  10: 
  11: /**
  12:  * 对照表的缩略曲线。用 PlotGeom 真采样，不是手画的示意图 ——
  13:  * 用户看到的就是"这个式子在 App 里画出来的样子"。
  14:  *
  15:  * 只负责画一小格，不管点击（点击在卡片上）。
  16:  */
  17: public class MiniPlot extends View {
  18: 
  19:     private Expr.Node node;
  20:     private PlotGeom.View geom;
  21:     private final Paint line = new Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG);
  22:     private final Paint axis = new Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG);
  23:     private final Path path = new Path();
  24: 
  25:     public MiniPlot(Context c) {
  26:         super(c);
  27:         line.setStyle(Paint.Style.STROKE);
  28:         line.setStrokeWidth(dp(1.7f));
  29:         line.setStrokeCap(Paint.Cap.ROUND);
  30:         axis.setStyle(Paint.Style.STROKE);
  31:         axis.setStrokeWidth(dp(1f));
  32:         axis.setColor(0x33383F4C);
  33:     }
  34: 
  35:     public void set(Expr.Node n, int color) {
  36:         node = n;
  37:         line.setColor(color);
  38:         requestLayout();
  39:         invalidate();
  40:     }
  41: 
  42:     private float dp(float v) {
  43:         return v * getResources().getDisplayMetrics().density;
  44:     }
  45: 
  46:     @Override
  47:     protected void onMeasure(int wSpec, int hSpec) {
  48:         int w = MeasureSpec.getSize(wSpec);
  49:         int h = MeasureSpec.getSize(hSpec);
  50:         if (w <= 0) w = (int) dp(74);
  51:         if (h <= 0) h = (int) dp(52);
  52:         setMeasuredDimension(w, h);
  53:     }
  54: 
  55:     @Override
  56:     protected void onDraw(Canvas c) {
  57:         super.onDraw(c);
  58:         if (node == null) return;
  59:         int w = getWidth(), h = getHeight();
  60:         if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return;
  61: 
  62:         // 缩略图必须自己裁边界。
  63:         //
  64:         // View 默认不会裁同伴：exp(x)、x^2 这类曲线在小窗口里很快就
  65:         // 冲出顶部，不裁的话会画到隔壁卡片上 —— 审计渲染图里
  66:         // 抛物线和指数线直接压到了下一格的文字上。
  67:         c.save();
  68:         c.clipRect(0, 0, w, h);
  69: 
  70:         // 每格都重新建 View：范围固定，才能横向比较形状
  71:         geom = new PlotGeom.View(-6, 6, -4, 4, w, h);
  72: 
  73:         // 坐标轴
  74:         float ax = (float) geom.px(0), ay = (float) geom.py(0);
  75:         if (ay >= 0 && ay <= h) c.drawLine(0, ay, w, ay, axis);
  76:         if (ax >= 0 && ax <= w) c.drawLine(ax, 0, ax, h, axis);
  77: 
  78:         // 曲线
  79:         List<PlotGeom.Seg> segs = PlotGeom.sample(node, geom, 1);
  80:         for (PlotGeom.Seg s : segs) {
  81:             path.reset();
  82:             boolean started = false;
  83:             for (int i = 0; i < s.pts.size(); i++) {
  84:                 double[] p = s.pts.get(i);
  85:                 float x = (float) geom.px(p[0]);
  86:                 float y = (float) geom.py(p[1]);
  87:                 // 冲出画面太多的点直接跳过，否则长直线会画到格子外面
  88:                 if (Math.abs(y) > h * 6) { started = false; continue; }
  89:                 if (!started) { path.moveTo(x, y); started = true; }
  90:                 else path.lineTo(x, y);
  91:             }
  92:             c.drawPath(path, line);
  93:         }
  94:         c.restore();
  95:     }
  96: }

 121:         public List<Review> reviews = new ArrayList<>();
 122:     }

 128:      * 答案是这个记住就行了"。所以检索从"默写"改成了"直接给答案背诵"。
 129:      * 键是 chapterId，值是答案点（每行一条）+ 一个考点。
 130:      */
 131:     public Map<String, Recall> recall = new LinkedHashMap<>();

 138:     // ── 解析 ────────────────────────────────────────────
 139:     public static Plan parse(String raw) {
 140:         Plan p = new Plan();
 141:         try {
 142:             JSONObject root = new JSONObject(raw);
 143: 
 144:             JSONObject m = root.optJSONObject("meta");
 145:             if (m != null) {
 146:                 p.meta.examDate = m.optString("examDate", "");
 147:                 p.meta.startDate = m.optString("startDate", "");
 148:                 p.meta.daysToExam = m.optInt("daysToExam", 0);
 149:                 p.meta.vocabTotal = m.optInt("vocabTotal", 0);
 150:             }
 151: 
 152:             // recall：章 id → 必背卡。缺失不算错，界面会跳过。
 153:             JSONObject rc = root.optJSONObject("recall");
 154:             if (rc != null) {
 155:                 java.util.Iterator<String> it = rc.keys();
 156:                 while (it.hasNext()) {
 157:                     String cid = it.next();
 158:                     JSONObject o = rc.optJSONObject(cid);
 159:                     if (o == null) continue;
 160:                     Recall r = new Recall();
 161:                     JSONArray pts = o.optJSONArray("points");
 162:                     if (pts != null) {
 163:                         for (int i = 0; i < pts.length(); i++) {
 164:                             String s = pts.optString(i, "").trim();
 165:                             if (!s.isEmpty()) r.points.add(s);
 166:                         }
 167:                     }
 168:                     r.exam = o.optString("exam", "").trim();
 169:                     p.recall.put(cid, r);
 170:                 }
 171:             }
 172: 
 173:             JSONArray subs = root.optJSONArray("subjects");
 174:             if (subs != null) {
 175:                 for (int i = 0; i < subs.length(); i++) {
 176:                     JSONObject o = subs.getJSONObject(i);
 177:                     Subject s = new Subject();
 178:                     s.id = o.optString("id");
 179:                     s.name = o.optString("name");
 180:                     s.color = o.optString("color", "#888888");
 181: 
 182:                     if (!s.id.isEmpty()) p.subjects.put(s.id, s);
 183:                 }
 184:             }
 185: 
 186:             JSONArray chs = root.optJSONArray("chapters");
 187:             if (chs != null) {
 188:                 for (int i = 0; i < chs.length(); i++) {
 189:                     JSONObject o = chs.getJSONObject(i);
 190:                     Chapter c = new Chapter();
 191:                     c.id = o.optString("id");
 192:                     c.subject = o.optString("subject");
 193:                     c.name = o.optString("name");
 194:                     c.tier = o.optString("tier");
 195:                     c.startPage = o.optInt("startPage");
 196:                     c.endPage = o.optInt("endPage");
 197:                     c.estMin = o.optInt("estMin");
 198:                     c.pdfStart = o.isNull("pdfStart") ? null : o.optInt("pdfStart");
 199: 
 200:                     if (!c.id.isEmpty()) p.chapters.put(c.id, c);
 201:                 }
 202:             }
 203: 
 204:             JSONArray ds = root.optJSONArray("days");
 205:             if (ds != null) {
 206:                 for (int i = 0; i < ds.length(); i++) {
 207:                     JSONObject o = ds.getJSONObject(i);
 208:                     Day d = new Day();
 209:                     d.date = o.optString("date");
 210:                     d.weekday = o.optString("weekday");
 211:                     d.phase = o.optString("phase");
 212:                     d.round = o.optString("round");
 213:                     d.reviewHint = o.optString("reviewHint", "");
 214:                     d.daysToExam = o.optInt("daysToExam");
 215:                     d.vocab = o.isNull("vocab") ? null : String.valueOf(o.opt("vocab"));
 216:                     // 结构化词汇区间
 217:                     JSONObject vo = o.optJSONObject("vocab");
 218:                     if (vo != null) {
 219:                         d.vocabFrom = vo.optInt("from", -1);
 220:                         d.vocabTo = vo.optInt("to", -1);
 221:                     }
 222:                     JSONArray vr = o.optJSONArray("vocabReview");
 223:                     if (vr != null && vr.length() > 0) {
 224:                         d.vocabReviewJson = vr.toString();
 225:                     }
 226: 
 227:                     JSONArray ts = o.optJSONArray("tasks");
 228:                     if (ts != null) {
 229:                         // 同一天内去重：learn 用章节+起始页，other 用 note 全量哈希
 230:                         Set<String> usedIds = new HashSet<>();
 231:                         for (int j = 0; j < ts.length(); j++) {
 232:                             JSONObject t = ts.getJSONObject(j);
 233:                             Task k = new Task();
 234:                             k.subject = t.optString("subject");
 235:                             k.chapterId = t.optString("chapterId");
 236:                             k.kind = t.optString("kind", "learn");
 237:                             k.note = t.optString("note", "");
 238:                             k.startPage = t.optInt("startPage");
 239:                             k.endPage = t.optInt("endPage");
 240:                             k.min = t.optInt("min");
 241:                             k.pdfStart = t.isNull("pdfStart") ? null : t.optInt("pdfStart");
 242:                             // 题库落点（可为 null：不是做题类任务）
 243:                             JSONObject bk = t.optJSONObject("bank");
 244:                             if (bk != null) {
 245:                                 k.bankSubject = bk.optString("subject", k.subject);
 246:                                 k.bankLimit = bk.optInt("limit", 10);
 247:                                 k.bankYear = bk.optInt("year", 0);
 248:                             }
 249:                             k.uid = t.optString("uid", "");
 250:                             k.id = k.uid.isEmpty() ? assignId(k, usedIds, d.date) : k.uid;
 251:                             usedIds.add(k.id);
 252:                             d.tasks.add(k);
 253:                         }
 254:                     }
 255: 
 256:                     JSONArray rs = o.optJSONArray("reviews");
 257:                     if (rs != null) {
 258:                         for (int j = 0; j < rs.length(); j++) {
 259:                             d.reviews.add(parseReview(rs.getJSONObject(j)));
 260:                         }
 261:                     }
 262:                     p.days.add(d);
 263:                 }
 264:             }
 265:         } catch (Exception e) {
 266:             throw new RuntimeException("plan.json 解析失败：" + e.getMessage(), e);
 267:         }
 268:         return p;
 269:     }
 270: 
 271:     static Review parseReview(JSONObject o) {
 272:         Review r = new Review();
 273:         r.id = o.optString("id");
 274:         r.chapterId = o.optString("chapterId");
 275:         r.subject = o.optString("subject");
 276:         r.kind = o.optString("kind");
 277:         r.due = o.optString("due");
 278:         r.min = o.optInt("min");
 279:         return r;
 280:     }

 282:     // ── 派生逻辑 ─────────────────────────────────────────
 283:     public Day dayByDate(String date) {
 284:         for (Day d : days) if (d.date.equals(date)) return d;
 285:         return null;
 286:     }
 287: 
 288:     public Chapter chapter(String id) {
 289:         return chapters.get(id);
 290:     }
 291: 
 292:     /**
 293:      * 查科目。查不到返回 null。
 294:      *
 295:      * 原来查不到会临时造一个 name=id 的假科目 —— 数学科从 plan.json
 296:      * 删掉之后，这个假科目让"math 已移除"的断言永远通过不了，
 297:      * 界面上也会冒出空名字。数据里没有就是没有，别编。
 298:      */
 299:     public Subject subject(String id) {
 300:         return subjects.get(id);
 301:     }
 302: 
 303:     /**
 304:      * 分配任务 id。
 305:      *
 306:      * learn：L-科目-章节-起始页。跨天会重复，这是「三轮螺旋」故意的同章节回访——
 307:      *        存储键是 date|id，所以不会串。
 308:      * other：O-科目-<note 的稳定哈希>。原来只取 note 前 12 字，
 309:      *        而差异恰好出现在后半段（"练习册《连续与间断》p21" vs "p22-26"），
 310:      *        导致同一天两个不同任务撞成同一个 id → 勾一个另一个也变勾上。
 311:      *        改用全量哈希 + 重名计数兜底。
 312:      */
 313:     static String assignId(Task t, Set<String> usedInDay, String date) {
 314:         String base;
 315:         if ("learn".equals(t.kind) && t.startPage > 0) {
 316:             base = "L-" + t.subject + "-" + t.chapterId + "-" + t.startPage;
 317:         } else {
 318:             base = "O-" + t.subject + "-" + fnv(t.note + "|" + t.startPage + "-" + t.endPage);
 319:         }
 320:         // 万一还是撞（note 完全相同的两条），加序号，绝不静默合并
 321:         String id = base;
 322:         int n = 2;
 323:         while (usedInDay.contains(id)) {
 324:             id = base + "#" + n;
 325:             n++;
 326:         }
 327:         usedInDay.add(id);
 328:         return id;
 329:     }
 330: 
 331:     /** FNV-1a 32 位，输出 8 位十六进制。用来给长文本生成短而稳定的 id 片段。 */
 332:     static String fnv(String s) {
 333:         int h = 0x811c9dc5;
 334:         for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
 335:             h ^= s.charAt(i);
 336:             h *= 0x01000193;
 337:         }
 338:         return String.format("%08x", h);
 339:     }
 340: 
 341:     /** 取任务 id（已在 parse 时分配）。 */
 342:     public static String taskId(Task t) {
 343:         return t.id;
 344:     }
 345: 
 346:     /** 某天全部需要勾选的 id（用于判断“是否全勾完”才能推进）。 */
 347:     public static List<String> dayTaskIds(Day d) {
 348:         List<String> ids = new ArrayList<>();
 349:         for (Task t : d.tasks) ids.add(taskId(t));
 350:         if (d.vocab != null) ids.add("V-" + d.date);
 351:         for (Review r : d.reviews) ids.add("R-" + r.id);
 352:         return ids;
 353:     }
 354: }
===== FILE: E:\小黑盒运营\cq-zhuanshengbens\mobile\src\com\zsb\plan\PlotGeom.java =====
   1: package com.zsb.plan;
   2: 
   3: import java.util.ArrayList;
   4: import java.util.List;
   5: 
   6: /**
   7:  * 绘图几何 —— 纯数字，不碰 Android。
   8:  *
   9:  * 为什么要单独抽出来：
  10:  *   画函数图像最容易出的错不是"画不出来"，而是
  11:  *   ① y 轴范围没算好，曲线全被裁掉  ② 渐近线两侧被连成一条竖线
  12:  *   ③ 高倍缩放时采样点不够，光滑曲线变锯齿
  13:  *   这些都能在电脑上用数字检查，不必装模拟器。verify.py 的 MathTest 就在查这些。
  14:  *
  15:  * 坐标约定：
  16:  *   数学坐标 (x, y) → 画布像素 (px, py)，px 向右增，py 向下增
  17:  *   view 对象里保存的是数学窗口 [xMin,xMax] × [yMin,yMax]
  18:  */
  19: public class PlotGeom {
  20: 
  21:     /** 数学窗口 + 画布尺寸。 */
  22:     public static class View {
  23:         public double xMin = -6, xMax = 6, yMin = -4, yMax = 4;
  24:         public int w = 1080, h = 900;
  25: 
  26:         public View() {}
  27: 
  28:         public View(double xMin, double xMax, double yMin, double yMax, int w, int h) {
  29:             this.xMin = xMin; this.xMax = xMax;
  30:             this.yMin = yMin; this.yMax = yMax;
  31:             this.w = w; this.h = h;
  32:         }
  33: 
  34:         public double spanX() { return xMax - xMin; }
  35:         public double spanY() { return yMax - yMin; }
  36: 
  37:         public double px(double x) {
  38:             return (x - xMin) / (xMax - xMin) * w;
  39:         }
  40: 
  41:         public double py(double y) {
  42:             return (yMax - y) / (yMax - yMin) * h;
  43:         }
  44: 
  45:         public double toX(double pxv) { return xMin + pxv / w * (xMax - xMin); }
  46:         public double toY(double pyv) { return yMax - pyv / h * (yMax - yMin); }
  47: 
  48:         /** 等比：让 1 个数学单位在横竖两个方向长度相同（圆看起来才是圆的）。 */
  49:         public void makeSquare() {
  50:             double cx = (xMin + xMax) / 2, cy = (yMin + yMax) / 2;
  51:             double unitX = spanX() / w, unitY = spanY() / h;
  52:             double u = Math.max(unitX, unitY);
  53:             double hx = u * w / 2, hy = u * h / 2;
  54:             xMin = cx - hx; xMax = cx + hx;
  55:             yMin = cy - hy; yMax = cy + hy;
  56:         }
  57: 
  58:         /** 以 (cx,cy) 为中心按 factor 缩放；factor<1 放大。 */
  59:         public void zoom(double cx, double cy, double factor) {
  60:             xMin = cx + (xMin - cx) * factor;
  61:             xMax = cx + (xMax - cx) * factor;
  62:             yMin = cy + (yMin - cy) * factor;
  63:             yMax = cy + (yMax - cy) * factor;
  64:         }
  65: 
  66:         public void pan(double dxMath, double dyMath) {
  67:             xMin += dxMath; xMax += dxMath;
  68:             yMin += dyMath; yMax += dyMath;
  69:         }
  70: 
  71:         public View copy() { return new View(xMin, xMax, yMin, yMax, w, h); }
  72:     }
  73: 
  74:     /**
  75:      * 一段连续曲线。断点（无定义 / 跳到无穷 / 陡到不像话）会切成多段，
  76:      * 这样 1/x 在 x=0 两侧不会被一条竖线连起来。
  77:      */
  78:     public static class Seg {
  79:         public final List<double[]> pts = new ArrayList<double[]>();  // 每项 {x, y}
  80:     }
  81: 
  82:     /** 一行求值结果，null 意义是"这里没有点"。 */
  83:     public static double safeEval(Expr.Node n, double x) {
  84:         try { return Expr.eval(n, x); } catch (Exception e) { return Double.NaN; }
  85:     }
  86: 
  87:     /**
  88:      * 采样 + 断点切分。
  89:      *
  90:      * @param n     表达式
  91:      * @param v     窗口
  92:      * @param stepPx 每隔几个像素取一个样（1 = 每像素，2 = 隔一个）
  93:      * @return 若干段；每段点已经按 x 递增
  94:      *
  95:      * 断点判定（三条都要）：
  96:      *   ① 值非有限（NaN/±Inf）
  97:      *   ② |y| 超出窗口 40 倍（画出来等于一条竖线，无信息）
  98:      *   ③ 相邻两点 |Δy| > 窗口高度 × 8 且 |Δx| 很小 —— 典型渐近线穿越
  99:      *      注意 tan(x) 这类是「真跳」，而 sin(1/x) 在 0 附近是「抖」，
 100:      *      两者都以断点处理更安全：宁可断开，不要假连线。
 101:      */
 102:     public static List<Seg> sample(Expr.Node n, View v, int stepPx) {
 103:         if (stepPx < 1) stepPx = 1;
 104:         List<Seg> out = new ArrayList<Seg>();
 105:         Seg cur = null;
 106:         double limit = Math.max(Math.abs(v.yMin), Math.abs(v.yMax)) * 40 + 1e6 * 0;
 107:         double prevX = Double.NaN, prevY = Double.NaN;
 108:         int steps = Math.max(2, v.w / stepPx);
 109:         for (int i = 0; i <= steps; i++) {
 110:             double x = v.toX((double) i * v.w / steps);
 111:             double y = safeEval(n, x);
 112:             boolean bad = !isFinite(y) || Math.abs(y) > limit;
 113:             if (bad) {
 114:                 cur = null;
 115:                 prevX = x; prevY = Double.NaN;
 116:                 continue;
 117:             }
 118:             if (cur != null && isFinite(prevY)) {
 119:                 double dy = Math.abs(y - prevY);
 120:                 double dx = Math.abs(x - prevX);
 121:                 if (dy > v.spanY() * 8 && dx < v.spanX() * 0.02) {
 122:                     cur = null;   // 疑似穿过渐近线，断开
 123:                 }
 124:             }
 125:             if (cur == null) { cur = new Seg(); out.add(cur); }
 126:             cur.pts.add(new double[]{x, y});
 127:             prevX = x; prevY = y;
 128:         }
 129:         // 丢掉只有一个点的碎段（画不出线，只会在屏幕上点一个像素）
 130:         List<Seg> keep = new ArrayList<Seg>();
 131:         for (Seg s : out) if (s.pts.size() >= 2) keep.add(s);
 132:         return keep;
 133:     }
 134: 
 135:     public static boolean isFinite(double v) {
 136:         return !Double.isNaN(v) && !Double.isInfinite(v);
 137:     }
 138: 
 139:     /**
 140:      * 自动选 y 范围：先用 [-4,4]，再看曲线实际落在哪。
 141:      * 只统计「窗口内、连续性合理」的值，避免 1/x 在 0 附近把范围撑到无穷。
 142:      */
 143:     public static double[] autoYRange(Expr.Node n, double xMin, double xMax, int samples) {
 144:         if (samples < 16) samples = 16;
 145:         double[] vs = new double[samples + 1];
 146:         int cnt = 0;
 147:         for (int i = 0; i <= samples; i++) {
 148:             double x = xMin + (xMax - xMin) * i / samples;
 149:             double y = safeEval(n, x);
 150:             if (isFinite(y) && Math.abs(y) < 1e6) vs[cnt++] = y;
 151:         }
 152:         if (cnt == 0) return new double[]{-4, 4};
 153:         double[] a = new double[cnt];
 154:         System.arraycopy(vs, 0, a, 0, cnt);
 155:         java.util.Arrays.sort(a);
 156:         // 用 5% / 95% 分位，而不是极值：极值往往是渐近线附近的离群点
 157:         double lo = a[(int) Math.floor(cnt * 0.05)];
 158:         double hi = a[(int) Math.floor(cnt * 0.95)];
 159:         if (!isFinite(lo) || !isFinite(hi)) return new double[]{-4, 4};
 160:         if (hi - lo < 1e-9) { lo -= 2; hi += 2; }
 161:         double pad = (hi - lo) * 0.35;
 162:         lo -= pad; hi += pad;
 163:         // 至少留 2 个单位高度，免得平函数被放大成一条噪声
 164:         double mid = (lo + hi) / 2;
 165:         if (hi - lo < 4) { lo = mid - 2; hi = mid + 2; }
 166:         // 极端情况兜底
 167:         if (hi - lo > 4000) { mid = (lo + hi) / 2; lo = mid - 2000; hi = mid + 2000; }
 168:         return new double[]{lo, hi};
 169:     }
 170: 
 171:     /**
 172:      * 选「好看的刻度间隔」：1、2、5 × 10^k，目标是每格约 targetPx 像素。
 173:      * 网格不贴这个规则会出现 0.33333 这种刻度，没法读。
 174:      */
 175:     public static double niceStep(double span, int px, double targetPx) {
 176:         if (span <= 0 || px <= 0) return 1;
 177:         double raw = span / px * targetPx;         // 期望的每格数学长度
 178:         if (raw <= 0 || !isFinite(raw)) return 1;
 179:         double mag = Math.pow(10, Math.floor(Math.log10(raw)));
 180:         double norm = raw / mag;
 181:         double nice;
 182:         if (norm <= 1) nice = 1;
 183:         else if (norm <= 2) nice = 2;
 184:         else if (norm <= 5) nice = 5;
 185:         else nice = 10;
 186:         return nice * mag;
 187:     }
 188: 
 189:     /** 刻度文字：避免 0.30000000000000004 这种浮点噪声。 */
 190:     public static String tickLabel(double v, double step) {
 191:         if (Math.abs(v) < step * 1e-6) return "0";
 192:         int dec = 0;
 193:         double s = step;
 194:         while (dec < 6 && Math.abs(s - Math.rint(s)) > 1e-9) { s *= 10; dec++; }
 195:         String t = String.format(java.util.Locale.US, "%." + dec + "f", v);
 196:         if (t.contains(".")) {
 197:             while (t.endsWith("0")) t = t.substring(0, t.length() - 1);
 198:             if (t.endsWith(".")) t = t.substring(0, t.length() - 1);
 199:         }
 200:         return t;
 201:     }
 202: 
 203:     /**
 204:      * 某条水平线是否落在窗口里（画 x 轴 / 网格线用），返回像素 y 或 NaN。
 205:      * 用 NaN 而不是 -1 当"没有"：像素坐标可能真的等于 -1。
 206:      */
 207:     public static double pyOf(View v, double y) {
 208:         if (y < Math.min(v.yMin, v.yMax) || y > Math.max(v.yMin, v.yMax)) return Double.NaN;
 209:         return v.py(y);
 210:     }
 211: 
 212:     public static double pxOf(View v, double x) {
 213:         if (x < v.xMin || x > v.xMax) return Double.NaN;
 214:         return v.px(x);
 215:     }
 216: 
 217:     /** 求某点切线斜率（数值中心差分），用于「切线」显示。 */
 218:     public static double slope(Expr.Node n, double x) {
 219:         double h = 1e-6 * Math.max(1, Math.abs(x));
 220:         double y1 = safeEval(n, x + h), y0 = safeEval(n, x - h);
 221:         if (!isFinite(y1) || !isFinite(y0)) return Double.NaN;
 222:         return (y1 - y0) / (2 * h);
 223:     }
 224: 
 225:     /**
 226:      * 找窗口内的零点（变号法 + 二分细化）。
 227:      * 用来在图上标「根」，比只画线有用得多。
 228:      */
 229:     public static List<Double> roots(Expr.Node n, View v, int samples) {
 230:         List<Double> out = new ArrayList<Double>();
 231:         if (samples < 32) samples = 32;
 232:         double px0 = Double.NaN;
 233:         double py0 = Double.NaN;
 234:         for (int i = 0; i <= samples; i++) {
 235:             double x = v.xMin + v.spanX() * i / samples;
 236:             double y = safeEval(n, x);
 237:             if (!isFinite(y)) { px0 = Double.NaN; continue; }
 238:             if (Math.abs(y) < 1e-12) { addRoot(out, x); px0 = x; py0 = y; continue; }
 239:             if (isFinite(py0) && (py0 > 0) != (y > 0)) {
 240:                 double lo = px0, hi = x;
 241:                 for (int k = 0; k < 60; k++) {
 242:                     double mid = (lo + hi) / 2;
 243:                     double ym = safeEval(n, mid);
 244:                     if (!isFinite(ym)) break;
 245:                     if ((ym > 0) == (py0 > 0)) lo = mid;
 246:                     else hi = mid;
 247:                 }
 248:                 addRoot(out, (lo + hi) / 2);
 249:             }
 250:             px0 = x; py0 = y;
 251:         }
 252:         return out;
 253:     }
 254: 
 255:     static void addRoot(List<Double> out, double x) {
 256:         for (Double d : out) if (Math.abs(d - x) < 1e-6) return;
 257:         out.add(x);
 258:     }
 259: }

 195:     // ── 网格与坐标轴 ──────────────────────────────────────
 196:     private void drawGrid(Canvas c) {
 197:         double stepX = PlotGeom.niceStep(view.spanX(), view.w, dp(72));
 198:         double stepY = PlotGeom.niceStep(view.spanY(), view.h, dp(64));
 199: 
 200:         // 竖线
 201:         double x0 = Math.ceil(view.xMin / stepX) * stepX;
 202:         for (double x = x0; x <= view.xMax + stepX * 1e-9; x += stepX) {
 203:             float px = (float) view.px(x);
 204:             c.drawLine(px, 0, px, view.h, isZeroLine(x, stepX) ? pAxis : pGrid);
 205:         }
 206:         // 横线
 207:         double y0 = Math.ceil(view.yMin / stepY) * stepY;
 208:         for (double y = y0; y <= view.yMax + stepY * 1e-9; y += stepY) {
 209:             float py = (float) view.py(y);
 210:             c.drawLine(0, py, view.w, py, isZeroLine(y, stepY) ? pAxis : pGrid);
 211:         }
 212:     }

 218:     private void drawAxisLabels(Canvas c) {

 220:         double stepY = PlotGeom.niceStep(view.spanY(), view.h, dp(64));
 221: 
 222:         float axisY = (float) view.py(0);
 223:         if (axisY < 0 || axisY > view.h) axisY = view.h - dp(4);
 224:         float axisX = (float) view.px(0);
 225:         if (axisX < 0 || axisX > view.w) axisX = dp(40);
 226: 
 227:         double x0 = Math.ceil(view.xMin / stepX) * stepX;

 229:             if (isZeroLine(x, stepX)) continue;
 230:             float px = (float) view.px(x);
 231:             String t = PlotGeom.tickLabel(x, stepX);
 232:             pTick.setTextAlign(Paint.Align.CENTER);
 233:             c.drawText(t, px, axisY + dp(14), pTick);
 234:         }
 235: 
 236:         double y0 = Math.ceil(view.yMin / stepY) * stepY;
 237:         for (double y = y0; y <= view.yMax + stepY * 1e-9; y += stepY) {
 238:             if (isZeroLine(y, stepY)) continue;
 239:             float py = (float) view.py(y);
 240:             String t = PlotGeom.tickLabel(y, stepY);
 241:             pTick.setTextAlign(Paint.Align.LEFT);
 242:             c.drawText(t, axisX + dp(4), py + dp(4), pTick);
 243:         }
 244:         // 原点
 245:         pTick.setTextAlign(Paint.Align.RIGHT);
 246:         c.drawText("O", axisX - dp(3), axisY + dp(13), pTick);
 247:     }
 248: 
 249:     // ── 曲线 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
 250:     /**
 251:      * 画曲线。必须把画布边框裁死。
 252:      *
 253:      * 为什么：tan(x) 靠近渐近线时，采样点会跑到纵向几百倍画布外。
 254:      * 不裁的话 Canvas 仍会把这些线画出去 —— 在控件内部看不出来，
 255:      * 但只要控件不是全屏（对话框、卡片、截图），
 256:      * 就会在相邻区域看到莫名其妙的竖线。这是视觉审计抓到的真 bug。
 257:      */
 258:     private void drawCurve(Canvas c, Expr.Node n, Paint p, int stepPx) {
 259:         List<PlotGeom.Seg> segs = PlotGeom.sample(n, view, stepPx);
 260:         int save = c.save();
 261:         c.clipRect(0, 0, view.w, view.h);
 262:         for (PlotGeom.Seg s : segs) {
 263:             Path path = new Path();
 264:             boolean started = false;
 265:             for (int i = 0; i < s.pts.size(); i++) {
 266:                 double mx = s.pts.get(i)[0], my = s.pts.get(i)[1];
 267:                 float px = (float) view.px(mx);
 268:                 float py = (float) view.py(my);
 269:                 // 横向也裁：点必须在 x 范围内
 270:                 if (px < -view.w || px > view.w * 2) {
 271:                     started = false;
 272:                     continue;
 273:                 }
 274:                 // 纵向裁到画布外合理倍数内：超出部分没信息，还会拖慢绘制。
 275:                 // 这里用 1 倍而不是 2 倍 —— 超出一个画布高度就已经画不出可见内容了。
 276:                 if (py < -view.h || py > view.h * 2) {
 277:                     started = false;
 278:                     continue;
 279:                 }
 280:                 if (!started) { path.moveTo(px, py); started = true; }
 281:                 else path.lineTo(px, py);
 282:             }
 283:             c.drawPath(path, p);
 284:         }
 285:         c.restoreToCount(save);
 286:     }
 287: 
 288:     private void drawRoots(Canvas c) {
 289:         List<Double> rs = PlotGeom.roots(f, view, 800);
 290:         for (Double r : rs) {
 291:             float px = (float) view.px(r);
 292:             float py = (float) view.py(0);
 293:             c.drawCircle(px, py, dp(3.4f), pRoot);
 294:         }
 295:     }
 296: 
 297:     private void drawTangent(Canvas c) {
 298:         if (tangentX == null) return;
 299:         double x = tangentX;
 300:         double y = PlotGeom.safeEval(f, x);
 301:         double k = PlotGeom.slope(f, x);
 302:         if (!PlotGeom.isFinite(y) || !PlotGeom.isFinite(k)) return;
 303:         // 在可视范围内画切线
 304:         double xa = view.xMin, xb = view.xMax;
 305:         double ya = y + k * (xa - x);
 306:         double yb = y + k * (xb - x);
 307:         c.drawLine((float) view.px(xa), (float) view.py(ya),
 308:                 (float) view.px(xb), (float) view.py(yb), pTangent);
 309:         c.drawCircle((float) view.px(x), (float) view.py(y), dp(4), pRoot);
 310:     }
 311: 
 312:     private Double tangentX = null;
 313: 
 314:     public Double tangentX() { return tangentX; }
 315: 
 316:     public void setTangentX(Double x) {
 317:         tangentX = x;
 318:         invalidate();
 319:     }
 320: 
 321:     // ── 手势 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
 322:     @SuppressLint("ClickableViewAccessibility")
 323:     @Override
 324:     public boolean onTouchEvent(MotionEvent e) {
 325:         switch (e.getActionMasked()) {
 326:             case MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN:
 327:                 lastX = e.getX(); lastY = e.getY();
 328:                 dragging = true;
 329:                 return true;
 330:             case MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE:
 331:                 if (e.getPointerCount() >= 2) {
 332:                     double d = spacing(e);
 333:                     if (pinchBase > 0) {
 334:                         double factor = pinchBase / d;
 335:                         if (factor < 0.5) factor = 0.5;
 336:                         if (factor > 2) factor = 2;
 337:                         view.zoom(pinchCx, pinchCy, factor);
 338:                         view.makeSquare();
 339:                     }
 340:                     pinchBase = d;
 341:                     invalidate();
 342:                 } else if (dragging) {
 343:                     double dxMath = view.toX(0) - view.toX(lastX - e.getX());
 344:                     double dyMath = view.toY(0) - view.toY(lastY - e.getY());
 345:                     // 用像素差直接换算，避免 toX(0) 这类边界值带来的偏差
 346:                     dxMath = -(e.getX() - lastX) * view.spanX() / view.w;
 347:                     dyMath = (e.getY() - lastY) * view.spanY() / view.h;
 348:                     view.pan(dxMath, dyMath);
 349:                     lastX = e.getX(); lastY = e.getY();
 350:                     invalidate();
 351:                 }
 352:                 return true;
 353:             case MotionEvent.ACTION_POINTER_DOWN: {
 354:                 if (e.getPointerCount() >= 2) {
 355:                     pinchBase = spacing(e);
 356:                     pinchCx = view.toX((e.getX(0) + e.getX(1)) / 2);
 357:                     pinchCy = view.toY((e.getY(0) + e.getY(1)) / 2);
 358:                 }
 359:                 return true;
 360:             }
 361:             case MotionEvent.ACTION_POINTER_UP:
 362:                 pinchBase = 0;
 363:                 dragging = false;
 364:                 return true;
 365:             case MotionEvent.ACTION_UP:
 366:                 dragging = false;
 367:                 pinchBase = 0;
 368:                 if (showTangent && f != null) {
 369:                     double x = view.toX(e.getX());
 370:                     tangentX = x;
 371:                     if (onPick != null) {
 372:                         onPick.onPick(x, PlotGeom.safeEval(f, x), PlotGeom.slope(f, x));
 373:                     }
 374:                     invalidate();
 375:                 }
 376:                 return true;
 377:             default:
 378:                 return super.onTouchEvent(e);
 379:         }
 380:     }
 381: 
 382:     private double spacing(MotionEvent e) {
 383:         if (e.getPointerCount() < 2) return 0;
 384:         float dx = e.getX(0) - e.getX(1);
 385:         float dy = e.getY(0) - e.getY(1);
 386:         return Math.max(1, Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy));
 387:     }
 388: 
 389:     private float dp(float v) {
 390:         return v * getResources().getDisplayMetrics().density;
 391:     }
 392: }
===== FILE: E:\小黑盒运营\cq-zhuanshengbens\mobile\src\com\zsb\plan\ProgressCode.java =====
   1: package com.zsb.plan;
   2: 
   3: import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
   4: import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
   5: import java.util.zip.Deflater;
   6: import java.util.zip.DeflaterOutputStream;
   7: import java.util.zip.InflaterInputStream;
   8: 
   9: /**
  10:  * 进度码 —— 把全部进度压成一个可以直接粘贴的短字符串。
  11:  *
  12:  * 为什么需要它：
  13:  *  · 「每个用户进度不一」如果靠云服务器，就得有账号、有网、有维护成本
  14:  *  · 实际使用中一个人就一两台设备，真正要解决的是「换手机不丢进度」
  15:  *  · 所以做成离线码：压 + Base32 + 校验和，复制粘贴即可搬移
  16:  *
  17:  * 格式：ZSB1 + Base32( deflate( json ) ) + 4 位校验
  18:  * 前缀和校验和用来在粘贴错时立刻报错，而不是悄悄导入半份数据。
  19:  */
  20: public class ProgressCode {
  21: 
  22:     private static final String MAGIC = "ZSB1";
  23:     // Crockford Base32：去掉 I L O U，避免和 1 0 混淆
  24:     private static final String ALPHABET = "0123456789ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTVWXYZ";
  25: 
  26:     /** 把 exportJson() 的结果编码成进度码。 */
  27:     public static String encode(String json) {
  28:         try {
  29:             byte[] raw = json.getBytes("UTF-8");
  30:             ByteArrayOutputStream bo = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
  31:             DeflaterOutputStream d = new DeflaterOutputStream(bo, new Deflater(9), 8192);
  32:             d.write(raw);
  33:             d.finish();
  34:             d.close();
  35:             String b32 = base32(bo.toByteArray());
  36:             return MAGIC + "-" + group(b32) + "-" + checksum(b32);
  37:         } catch (Exception e) {
  38:             return null;
  39:         }
  40:     }
  41: 
  42:     /** 解出 JSON。失败返回 null，不抛异常。 */
  43:     public static String decode(String code) {
  44:         try {
  45:             if (code == null) return null;
  46:             // 容忍用户粘贴时带了空格、换行、大小写混用
  47:             String s = code.trim().toUpperCase().replaceAll("[\\s\\-]", "");
  48:             if (!s.startsWith(MAGIC)) return null;
  49:             s = s.substring(MAGIC.length());
  50:             if (s.length() < 6) return null;
  51: 
  52:             String sum = s.substring(s.length() - 4);
  53:             String body = s.substring(0, s.length() - 4);
  54:             if (!checksum(body).equals(sum)) return null;   // 抄错了
  55: 
  56:             byte[] packed = unBase32(body);
  57:             if (packed == null) return null;
  58: 
  59:             InflaterInputStream in = new InflaterInputStream(new ByteArrayInputStream(packed));
  60:             ByteArrayOutputStream bo = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
  61:             byte[] buf = new byte[8192];
  62:             int n;
  63:             while ((n = in.read(buf)) > 0) bo.write(buf, 0, n);
  64:             in.close();
  65:             return new String(bo.toByteArray(), "UTF-8");
  66:         } catch (Exception e) {
  67:             return null;
  68:         }
  69:     }
  70: 
  71:     // ── 编码细节 ──────────────────────────────────────────
  72:     private static String base32(byte[] data) {
  73:         StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
  74:         int buf = 0, bits = 0;
  75:         for (byte b : data) {
  76:             buf = (buf << 8) | (b & 0xFF);
  77:             bits += 8;
  78:             while (bits >= 5) {
  79:                 sb.append(ALPHABET.charAt((buf >> (bits - 5)) & 31));
  80:                 bits -= 5;
  81:             }
  82:         }
  83:         if (bits > 0) {
  84:             sb.append(ALPHABET.charAt((buf << (5 - bits)) & 31));
  85:         }
  86:         return sb.toString();
  87:     }
  88: 
  89:     private static byte[] unBase32(String s) {
  90:         ByteArrayOutputStream bo = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
  91:         int buf = 0, bits = 0;
  92:         for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
  93:             int v = ALPHABET.indexOf(s.charAt(i));
  94:             if (v < 0) return null;
  95:             buf = (buf << 5) | v;
  96:             bits += 5;
  97:             if (bits >= 8) {
  98:                 bo.write((buf >> (bits - 8)) & 0xFF);
  99:                 bits -= 8;
 100:             }
 101:         }
 102:         return bo.toByteArray();
 103:     }
 104: 
 105:     /** 4 位校验：对字符做加权和，抄错一位基本必错。 */
 106:     private static String checksum(String s) {
 107:         long h = 5381;
 108:         for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
 109:             h = ((h * 33) + s.charAt(i)) & 0xFFFFFFFFL;
 110:         }
 111:         StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
 112:         for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
 113:             sb.append(ALPHABET.charAt((int) ((h >> (i * 5)) & 31)));
 114:         }
 115:         return sb.toString();
 116:     }
 117: 
 118:     /** 每 5 个字符加一个横杠，方便肉眼核对和分行粘贴。 */
 119:     private static String group(String s) {
 120:         StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
 121:         for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
 122:             if (i > 0 && i % 5 == 0) sb.append('-');
 123:             sb.append(s.charAt(i));
 124:         }
 125:         return sb.toString();
 126:     }
 127: }

  78:     // ── 题库加载 ────────────────────────────────────────────
  79:     private List<JSONObject> bank(String subject) {
  80:         if (cache.containsKey(subject)) return cache.get(subject);
  81:         List<JSONObject> out = new ArrayList<>();
  82:         try {
  83:             InputStream in = act.getAssets().open("bank/" + subject + ".json");
  84:             ByteArrayOutputStream bo = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
  85:             byte[] buf = new byte[8192];
  86:             int n;
  87:             while ((n = in.read(buf)) > 0) bo.write(buf, 0, n);
  88:             in.close();
  89:             JSONObject root = new JSONObject(new String(bo.toByteArray(), "UTF-8"));
  90:             JSONArray arr = root.getJSONArray("q");
  91:             for (int i = 0; i < arr.length(); i++) out.add(arr.getJSONObject(i));
  92:         } catch (Exception e) { /* 读不到就空 */ }
  93:         cache.put(subject, out);
  94:         return out;
  95:     }
  96: 
  97:     /** 某科某年的题。 */
  98:     public void openYear(String subject, int year, String title) {
  99:         List<JSONObject> all = bank(subject);
 100:         queue.clear();
 101:         for (JSONObject q : all) {
 102:             if (q.optInt("y", 0) == year) queue.add(q);
 103:         }
 104:         if (queue.isEmpty()) {
 105:             Toast("题库里没有 " + year + " 年的题");
 106:             return;
 107:         }
 108:         start(title);
 109:     }
 110: 
 111:     /**
 112:      * 按学科抽题。
 113:      * @param limit  最多几道
 114:      * @param onlyWrong  true = 只做错题本里的
 115:      */
 116:     public void openSubject(String subject, int limit, boolean onlyWrong, String title) {
 117:         List<JSONObject> all = bank(subject);
 118:         if (all.isEmpty()) {
 119:             Toast("题库未加载");
 120:             return;
 121:         }
 122:         queue.clear();
 123:         if (onlyWrong) {
 124:             List<String> ids = store.wrongIds(subject);
 125:             for (JSONObject q : all) {
 126:                 if (ids.contains(q.optString("id"))) queue.add(q);
 127:             }
 128:             if (queue.isEmpty()) {
 129:                 Toast("错题本是空的，先去做题");
 130:                 return;
 131:             }
 132:         } else {
 133:             // 优先给有答案的题：能被判分才有练习价值
 134:             List<JSONObject> withA = new ArrayList<>();
 135:             List<JSONObject> noA = new ArrayList<>();
 136:             for (JSONObject q : all) {
 137:                 if (q.optInt("a", -1) >= 0) withA.add(q); else noA.add(q);
 138:             }
 139:             Collections.shuffle(withA);
 140:             Collections.shuffle(noA);
 141:             for (int i = 0; i < withA.size() && queue.size() < limit; i++) queue.add(withA.get(i));
 142:             for (int i = 0; i < noA.size() && queue.size() < limit; i++) queue.add(noA.get(i));
 143:         }
 144:         if (queue.isEmpty()) { Toast("没有题"); return; }
 145:         start(title);
 146:     }
