 236:     private void render() {
 237:         if (idx >= queue.size()) { finish(); return; }
 238:         answered = false;
 239:         JSONObject q = queue.get(idx);
 240: 
 241:         tvProgress.setText((idx + 1) + " / " + queue.size() + "   ✓" + right);
 242:         tvStem.setText((idx + 1) + ". " + q.optString("q", ""));
 243:         tvSol.setVisibility(View.GONE);
 244:         tvSol.setText("");
 245: 
 246:         optBox.removeAllViews();
 247:         navBar.removeAllViews();
 248:         scroller.scrollTo(0, 0);
 249: 
 250:         JSONArray opts = q.optJSONArray("o");
 251:         int n = opts == null ? 0 : opts.length();
 252:         for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
 253:             final int fi = i;
 254:             final String text = opts.optString(i, "");
 255:             final TextView row = new TextView(act);
 256:             row.setText("ABCD".charAt(Math.min(i, 3)) + ".  " + text);
 257:             row.setTextSize(16);
 258:             row.setTextColor(C_FG);
 259:             row.setLineSpacing(dp(4), 1f);
 260:             row.setPadding(dp(15), dp(14), dp(15), dp(14));
 261:             GradientDrawable g = new GradientDrawable();
 262:             g.setColor(C_BG2);
 263:             g.setCornerRadius(dp(12));
 264:             g.setStroke(dp(1), C_BG3);
 265:             row.setBackground(g);
 266:             LinearLayout.LayoutParams lp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 267:                     ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT);
 268:             lp.setMargins(0, dp(5), 0, dp(5));
 269:             row.setOnClickListener(new View.OnClickListener() {
 270:                 @Override public void onClick(View v) {
 271:                     if (answered) return;
 272:                     choose(fi, row);
 273:                 }
 274:             });
 275:             optBox.addView(row, lp);
 276:         }
 277:     }
 278: 
 279:     private void choose(int pick, TextView row) {
 280:         if (idx >= queue.size()) return;
 281:         answered = true;
 282:         JSONObject q = queue.get(idx);
 283:         int ans = q.optInt("a", -1);
 284:         JSONArray opts = q.optJSONArray("o");
 285:         int n = opts == null ? 0 : opts.length();
 286: 
 287:         // 解析可信度护栏，见 trustedSol()
 288:         String sol = trustedSol(q);
 289: 
 290:         if (ans < 0) {
 291:             // 没答案：只标记"已选"，不判对错 —— 不编造答案
 292:             paint(row, C_AMBER);
 293:             tvSol.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
 294:             tvSol.setText("这道题暂未收录答案，先自己判断。"
 295:                     + (sol.isEmpty() ? "" : "\n\n" + sol));
 296:         } else {
 297:             // 把所有选项该上的色都上齐
 298:             for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
 299:                 View cv = optBox.getChildAt(i);
 300:                 if (!(cv instanceof TextView)) continue;
 301:                 if (i == ans) paint((TextView) cv, C_TEAL);
 302:                 else if (i == pick) paint((TextView) cv, C_RED);
 303:             }
 304:             if (pick == ans) {
 305:                 right++;
 306:             } else {
 307:                 wrong.add(q);
 308:                 store.addWrong(q.optString("id"), q.optString("subjectSafe", ""), q.optString("q"), "");
 309:             }
 310:             tvSol.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
 311:             tvSol.setText(pick == ans
 312:                     ? ("答对了。" + (sol.isEmpty() ? "" : "\n\n" + sol))
 313:                     : ("答错了，正确答案是 " + "ABCD".charAt(ans) + "。"
 314:                        + (sol.isEmpty() ? "" : "\n\n" + sol)));
 315:         }
 316: 
 317:         // 下一题按钮
 318:         navBar.removeAllViews();
 319:         TextView next = new TextView(act);
 320:         boolean last = idx + 1 >= queue.size();
 321:         next.setText(last ? "看结果" : "下一题");
 322:         next.setTextSize(16);
 323:         next.setTextColor(C_FG);
 324:         next.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 325:         next.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
 326:         next.setPadding(0, dp(15), 0, dp(15));
 327:         GradientDrawable g = new GradientDrawable();
 328:         g.setColor(C_BG3);
 329:         g.setCornerRadius(dp(13));
 330:         next.setBackground(g);
 331:         next.setOnClickListener(new View.OnClickListener() {
 332:             @Override public void onClick(View v) {
 333:                 idx++;
 334:                 if (idx >= queue.size()) finish(); else render();
 335:             }
 336:         });
 337:         navBar.addView(next, new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 338:                 ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT));
 339:     }
 340: 
 341:     private void paint(TextView v, int color) {
 342:         GradientDrawable g = new GradientDrawable();
 343:         g.setColor(C_BG3);
 344:         g.setCornerRadius(dp(12));
 345:         g.setStroke(dp(2), color);
 346:         v.setBackground(g);
 347:         v.setTextColor(color);
 348:     }

 366:

 368:         sub.setText("答对 " + right + " 题 · 正确率 " + pct + "%"
 369:                 + (wrong.isEmpty() ? "" : "\n错的 " + wrong.size() + " 题已进错题本"));
 370:         sub.setTextSize(15);
 371:         sub.setTextColor(C_FG2);
 372:         sub.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
 373:         sub.setLineSpacing(dp(6), 1f);
 374:         sub.setPadding(dp(20), dp(14), dp(20), dp(28));
 375:         done.addView(sub);
 376: 
 377:         TextView close = new TextView(act);
 378:         close.setText("完成");
 379:         close.setTextSize(16);
 380:         close.setTextColor(C_FG);
 381:         close.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
 382:         close.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
 383:         close.setPadding(0, dp(15), 0, dp(15));

 392:

 396:                 ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT));
 397:         dlg.setContentView(root);

 400:     private void Toast(String s) {
 401:         android.widget.Toast.makeText(act, s, android.widget.Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
 402:     }
 403: 
 404:     private void dismiss() {
 405:         if (dlg != null && dlg.isShowing()) dlg.dismiss();
 406:         dlg = null;
 407:     }
 408: 
 409:     public boolean isShowing() { return dlg != null && dlg.isShowing(); }
 410: 
 411:     /**
 412:      * 解析可信度护栏 —— 2026-10-28 事故的直接防线。
 413:      *
 414:      * 当时的现象：题干问"500 个 32*32 点阵需要多少容量"，下面挂的解析却在讲
 415:      * "中英文切换的快捷键是 Ctrl+Space"。根因是 PDF 转题库时题号与解析块错位，
 416:      * 解析被成段挂到了别的题上（comp.json 里 2007 年的解析有 89% 实际是 2013 年的）。
 417:      * 离线已清洗掉 129 条错配，这里再加一道运行时护栏：
 418:      * 解析必须和本题有实质重合才显示，否则宁可不显示 ——
 419:      * 空解析只是少点信息，错解析直接教错。
 420:      *
 421:      * 判定（任一成立才算可信）：
 422:      *   A. 解析含题干 >=8 字的连续原文（是连续片段，不是零散词）
 423:      *   B. 解析含正确选项的完整文本（>=4 字）
 424:      */
 425:     private static String trustedSol(JSONObject q) {
 426:         String sol = q.optString("sol", "");
 427:         if (sol.trim().isEmpty()) return "";
 428:         String stem = normText(q.optString("q", ""));
 429:         String solN = normText(sol);
 430:         if (stem.isEmpty() || solN.isEmpty()) return sol;
 431: 
 432:         if (longestRun(stem, solN) >= 8) return sol;
 433: 
 434:         int ai = q.optInt("a", -1);
 435:         JSONArray os = q.optJSONArray("o");
 436:         if (os != null && ai >= 0 && ai < os.length()) {
 437:             String on = normText(os.optString(ai, ""));
 438:             if (on.length() >= 4 && solN.contains(on)) return sol;
 439:         }
 440: 
 441:         // C. 弱条件：题干里有 >=6 字与解析重合（实际数据里只有两条靠这条放行，
 442:         //    都是"下图所示"这类题干不含答案线索的正确解析）。门槛刻意留高，
 443:         //    错配样本的这条分数都 <0.10，拦得住。
 444:         if (longestRun(solN, stem) >= 6 && solN.length() >= 15) {
 445:             int hit = 0;
 446:             for (int i = 0; i + 6 <= stem.length(); i++) {
 447:                 if (solN.contains(stem.substring(i, i + 6))) { hit++; }
 448:             }
 449:             if (hit * 100 / Math.max(1, stem.length()) >= 12) return sol;
 450:         }
 451:         return "";
 452:     }
 453: 
 454:     /** 去掉空白与标点，只留实义字符，用于比对。 */
 455:     private static String normText(String s) {
 456:         if (s == null) return "";
 457:         StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder(s.length());
 458:         for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
 459:             char c = s.charAt(i);
 460:             if (Character.isWhitespace(c)) continue;
 461:             if ("\uFF0C\u3002\u3001\uFF1B\uFF1A\uFF1F\uFF01\uFF08\uFF09\u201C\u201D\u2018\u2019\u00B7\u2026\u2014,.;:?!()[]\"'*=_".indexOf(c) >= 0) continue;
 462:             b.append(c);
 463:         }
 464:         return b.toString();
 465:     }
 466: 
 467:     /** a 的最长连续子串有多长出现在 b 里。 */
 468:     private static int longestRun(String a, String b) {
 469:         int best = 0;
 470:         for (int i = 0; i < a.length(); i++) {
 471:             if (a.length() - i <= best) break;
 472:             for (int L = a.length() - i; L > best; L--) {
 473:                 if (b.contains(a.substring(i, i + L))) { best = L; break; }
 474:             }
 475:         }
 476:         return best;
 477:     }
 478: }

  14: /**
  15:  * 纯逻辑状态容器。**不依赖任何 Android 类**，所以能在电脑上直接跑测试。
  16:  *
  17:  * 分工：
  18:  *   StateLogic —— 只管数据在内存里怎么变、怎么序列化成 JSON 字符串
  19:  *   Store      —— 只管这份字符串怎么落到 SharedPreferences（Android 专属）
  20:  *
  21:  * 这样拆开的好处是真实存在的：所有业务规则（勾选、推进、剩余天数、计时累计、
  22:  * 导出导入）都能在桌面上用真实数据验证，不必依赖真机或模拟器。
  23:  */
  24: public class StateLogic {
  25: 
  26:     protected final Set<String> done = new HashSet<>();
  27:     protected final Set<String> completed = new HashSet<>();
  28:     protected final Map<String, String> notes = new HashMap<>();
  29:     protected final Map<String, Long> timing = new HashMap<>();
  30:     protected final Set<String> star = new HashSet<>();
  31:     protected final Map<String, Integer> hard = new HashMap<>();
  32: 
  33:     public static String key(String date, String id) {
  34:         return date + "|" + id;
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     // ── 勾选 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  38:     public boolean isDone(String date, String id) { return done.contains(key(date, id)); }
  39: 
  40:     public void setDone(String date, String id, boolean v) {
  41:         String k = key(date, id);
  42:         if (v) done.add(k); else done.remove(k);
  43:     }
  44: 
  45:     public boolean toggleDone(String date, String id) {
  46:         boolean nv = !isDone(date, id);
  47:         setDone(date, id, nv);
  48:         return nv;
  49:     }
  50: 
  51:     public int doneCount() { return done.size(); }
  52: 
  53:     // ── 推进 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  54:     public int currentIndex(Plan plan) {
  55:         for (int i = 0; i < plan.days.size(); i++) {
  56:             if (!completed.contains(plan.days.get(i).date)) return i;
  57:         }
  58:         return Math.max(0, plan.days.size() - 1);
  59:     }
  60: 
  61:     /**
  62:      * 按真实日历定位「今天是第几天」。
  63:      *
  64:      * 之前只有 currentIndex()（第一个未确认的天），与日历脱钩：
  65:      * 三天不打开 App，第四天仍显示第一天，剩余天数也不动。
  66:      * 这里改成以 plan.days 里的真实日期定位今天；
  67:      * 若那天已确认（进度超前），往后找最近一个未确认的。
  68:      */
  69:     public int todayIndex(Plan plan) {
  70:         if (plan == null || plan.days.isEmpty()) return 0;
  71: 
  72:         String today = todayIso();
  73:         int pos = -1;
  74: 
  75:         // ① 计划里正好有今天
  76:         for (int i = 0; i < plan.days.size(); i++) {
  77:             if (today.equals(plan.days.get(i).date)) { pos = i; break; }
  78:         }
  79: 
  80:         // ② 今天不在计划内（开学前 / 计划结束 / 中间断档）→ 用日期比较定位
  81:         if (pos < 0) {
  82:             pos = 0;
  83:             for (int i = 0; i < plan.days.size(); i++) {
  84:                 if (plan.days.get(i).date.compareTo(today) <= 0) pos = i;
  85:                 else break;
  86:             }
  87:         }
  88: 
  89:         // ③ 该天已确认 → 往后找最近未确认的
  90:         for (int i = Math.max(0, pos); i < plan.days.size(); i++) {
  91:             if (!completed.contains(plan.days.get(i).date)) return i;
  92:         }
  93:         return Math.max(0, plan.days.size() - 1);
  94:     }
  95: 
  96:     /** 超前为正、落后为负：已确认的前置天数 - 应该完成的天数。 */
  97:     public int aheadBehind(Plan plan, int todayIdx) {
  98:         int doneBefore = 0;
  99:         for (int i = 0; i < todayIdx && i < plan.days.size(); i++) {
 100:             if (completed.contains(plan.days.get(i).date)) doneBefore++;
 101:         }
 102:         return doneBefore - todayIdx;
 103:     }
 104: 
 105:     /** 距考试还有几天（按真实日历）。 */
 106:     public int realDaysToExam(Plan plan) {
 107:         if (plan == null || plan.meta == null) return 0;
 108:         String ex = plan.meta.examDate;
 109:         if (ex == null || ex.length() < 10) return 0;
 110:         try {
 111:             java.text.SimpleDateFormat f =
 112:                     new java.text.SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd", java.util.Locale.CHINA);
 113:             f.setLenient(false);
 114:             java.util.Date d0 = f.parse(todayIso());
 115:             java.util.Date d1 = f.parse(ex.substring(0, 10));
 116:             if (d0 == null || d1 == null) return 0;
 117:             long days = (d1.getTime() - d0.getTime()) / (24L * 3600L * 1000L);
 118:             return (int) Math.max(0, days);
 119:         } catch (Exception e) {
 120:             return 0;
 121:         }
 122:     }
 123: 
 124:     public int daysLeft(Plan plan) {
 125:         int n = 0;
 126:         for (Plan.Day d : plan.days) if (!completed.contains(d.date)) n++;
 127:         return n;
 128:     }

 175:     // ── 序列化 ────────────────────────────────────────────
 176:     /** 序列化成 JSON 字符串。返回 null 表示没变化（理论上不会）。 */
 177:     public String serialize() {
 178:         try {
 179:             JSONObject o = new JSONObject();
 180:             o.put("done", toObj(done));
 181:             o.put("completed", toObj(completed));
 182:             o.put("star", toObj(star));
 183:             o.put("notes", new JSONObject(notes));
 184:             o.put("hard", new JSONObject(hard));
 185:             JSONObject tm = new JSONObject();
 186:             for (Map.Entry<String, Long> e : timing.entrySet()) tm.put(e.getKey(), e.getValue());
 187:             o.put("timing", tm);
 188:             o.put("updatedAt", nowIso());
 189:             return o.toString();
 190:         } catch (Exception e) {
 191:             return null;
 192:         }
 193:     }
 194: 
 195:     /** 从 JSON 字符串恢复。损坏内容一律当空进度，绝不抛到调用方。 */
 196:     public void deserialize(String raw) {
 197:         if (raw == null || raw.isEmpty()) return;
 198:         try {
 199:             JSONObject o = new JSONObject(raw);
 200:             readSet(o.optJSONObject("done"), done);
 201:             readSet(o.optJSONObject("completed"), completed);
 202:             readSet(o.optJSONObject("star"), star);
 203:             readMap(o.optJSONObject("notes"), notes);
 204:             readIntMap(o.optJSONObject("hard"), hard);
 205:             JSONObject tm = o.optJSONObject("timing");
 206:             if (tm != null) {
 207:                 for (Iterator<String> it = tm.keys(); it.hasNext(); ) {
 208:                     String k = it.next();
 209:                     timing.put(k, tm.optLong(k));
 210:                 }
 211:             }
 212:         } catch (Exception ignored) {
 213:             // 存档坏了就当空进度，不让 App 起不来
 214:         }
 215:     }
 216: 
 217:     private static void readSet(JSONObject o, Set<String> out) {
 218:         if (o == null) return;
 219:         for (Iterator<String> it = o.keys(); it.hasNext(); ) {
 220:             String k = it.next();
 221:             if (o.optBoolean(k, false)) out.add(k);
 222:         }
 223:     }
 224: 
 225:     private static void readMap(JSONObject o, Map<String, String> out) {
 226:         if (o == null) return;
 227:         for (Iterator<String> it = o.keys(); it.hasNext(); ) {
 228:             String k = it.next();
 229:             out.put(k, o.optString(k, ""));
 230:         }
 231:     }
 232: 
 233:     private static void readIntMap(JSONObject o, Map<String, Integer> out) {
 234:         if (o == null) return;
 235:         for (Iterator<String> it = o.keys(); it.hasNext(); ) {
 236:             String k = it.next();
 237:             out.put(k, o.optInt(k, 1));
 238:         }
 239:     }
 240: 
 241:     private static JSONObject toObj(Set<String> s) throws Exception {
 242:         JSONObject o = new JSONObject();
 243:         for (String k : s) o.put(k, true);
 244:         return o;
 245:     }
 246: 
 247:     // ── 导出 / 导入 ────────────────────────────────────────
 248:     public String exportJson() {
 249:         try {
 250:             JSONObject o = new JSONObject();
 251:             o.put("_app", "zsb-plan");
 252:             o.put("_version", 1);
 253:             o.put("_exportedAt", nowIso());
 254:             o.put("state", new JSONObject(serialize()));
 255:             return o.toString(2);
 256:         } catch (Exception e) {
 257:             return "{}";
 258:         }
 259:     }
 260: 
 261:     /** 返回导入后的勾选条数。内容不合法时抛异常，且不改动已有数据。 */
 262:     public int importJson(String raw) throws Exception {
 263:         JSONObject root = new JSONObject(raw);
 264:         JSONObject st = root.optJSONObject("state");
 265:         if (st == null) st = root;
 266: 
 267:         // 先解析到临时容器，全部成功后再替换——避免半截导入把数据清掉
 268:         Set<String> nd = new HashSet<>(), nc = new HashSet<>(), ns = new HashSet<>();
 269:         Map<String, String> nn = new HashMap<>();
 270:         Map<String, Integer> nh = new HashMap<>();
 271:         Map<String, Long> nt = new HashMap<>();
 272:         readSet(st.optJSONObject("done"), nd);
 273:         readSet(st.optJSONObject("completed"), nc);
 274:         readSet(st.optJSONObject("star"), ns);
 275:         readMap(st.optJSONObject("notes"), nn);
 276:         readIntMap(st.optJSONObject("hard"), nh);
 277:         JSONObject tm = st.optJSONObject("timing");
 278:         if (tm != null) for (Iterator<String> it = tm.keys(); it.hasNext(); ) {
 279:             String k = it.next(); nt.put(k, tm.optLong(k));
 280:         }
 281: 
 282:         done.clear(); done.addAll(nd);
 283:         completed.clear(); completed.addAll(nc);
 284:         star.clear(); star.addAll(ns);
 285:         notes.clear(); notes.putAll(nn);
 286:         hard.clear(); hard.putAll(nh);
 287:         timing.clear(); timing.putAll(nt);
 288:         return done.size();
 289:     }
 290: 
 291:     public void resetAll() {
 292:         done.clear(); completed.clear(); star.clear();
 293:         notes.clear(); timing.clear(); hard.clear();
 294:     }
 295: 
 296:     // ── 日期 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
 297:     public static String todayIso() {
 298:         return new SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd", Locale.CHINA).format(new Date());
 299:     }
 300: 
 301:     static String nowIso() {
 302:         return new SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss", Locale.CHINA).format(new Date());
 303:     }
 304: }
===== FILE: E:\小黑盒运营\cq-zhuanshengbens\mobile\src\com\zsb\plan\Store.java =====
   1: package com.zsb.plan;
   2: 
   3: import android.content.ContentValues;
   4: import android.content.Context;
   5: import android.content.SharedPreferences;
   6: import android.database.Cursor;
   7: import android.database.sqlite.SQLiteDatabase;
   8: import android.database.sqlite.SQLiteOpenHelper;
   9: import android.os.Handler;
  10: import android.os.Looper;
  11: 
  12: import java.util.ArrayList;
  13: import java.util.List;
  14: 
  15: /**
  16:  * 持久化层 —— 数据库版。
  17:  *
  18:  * 为什么从 SharedPreferences 换成 SQLite：
  19:  *  · 原来把整个进度序列化成一个 JSON 字符串，每次勾一个框都要重写全量
  20:  *  · 一天勾一二十个任务、每个任务还有计时和默写，很快就是几十 KB 反复整写
  21:  *  · 单条记录更新改成一行 UPDATE，代价与总进度无关
  22:  *
  23:  * 业务规则仍然全在 StateLogic（纯 Java，可在电脑上测）。
  24:  * 这里只负责「读库 → 填进 StateLogic」和「StateLogic 变了 → 写回库」。
  25:  *
  26:  * 兼容老数据：首次打开时如果发现旧的 zsb_state 里还有进度，
  27:  * 自动迁移过来，之后不再读它（但不清空，留作后悔药）。
  28:  */
  29: public class Store extends StateLogic {
  30: 
  31:     private static final String DB = "zsb.db";
  32:     private static final int DB_VER = 3;
  33: 
  34:     private final Db db;
  35:     private final SharedPreferences legacy;
  36: 
  37:     private final Handler saveHandler = new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper());
  38:     private final Runnable saveTask = new Runnable() {
  39:         @Override public void run() {
  40:             savePending = false;
  41:             writeNow();
  42:         }
  43:     };
  44:     private boolean savePending;
  45: 
  46:     public Store(Context ctx) {
  47:         this.legacy = ctx.getSharedPreferences("zsb_state", Context.MODE_PRIVATE);
  48:         this.db = new Db(ctx.getApplicationContext());
  49:         loadAll();
  50:         migrateLegacy();
  51:     }
  52: 
  53:     // ── 建表 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  54:     static class Db extends SQLiteOpenHelper {
  55: 
  56:         Db(Context c) {
  57:             super(c, DB, null, DB_VER);
  58:         }
  59: 
  60:         @Override
  61:         public void onCreate(SQLiteDatabase d) {
  62:             // 勾选：一天一个任务一行
  63:             d.execSQL("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS done ("
  64:                     + "d TEXT NOT NULL, i TEXT NOT NULL, v INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,"
  65:                     + " PRIMARY KEY (d, i))");
  66: 
  67:             // 推进：哪天已确认
  68:             d.execSQL("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS committed ("
  69:                     + "d TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY)");
  70: 
  71:             // 计时：累积毫秒
  72:             d.execSQL("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS timing ("
  73:                     + "d TEXT NOT NULL, i TEXT NOT NULL, ms INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,"
  74:                     + " PRIMARY KEY (d, i))");
  75: 
  76:             // 默写/笔记
  77:             d.execSQL("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS notes ("
  78:                     + "t TEXT, d TEXT NOT NULL, i TEXT NOT NULL,"
  79:                     + " PRIMARY KEY (d, i))");
  80: 
  81:             // 错题星级 0..3
  82:             d.execSQL("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS hard ("
  83:                     + "i TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, lv INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0)");
  84: 
  85:             // 错题本：做题做错的题（v2 新增）
  86:             // q 存题干前 80 字做摘要，避免依赖题库版本
  87:             d.execSQL("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS wrong ("
  88:                     + "i TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, s TEXT, q TEXT,"
  89:                     + " ts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, n INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1)");
  90: 
  91:             // 关键值（schema 版本、最后同步时间等）
  92:             d.execSQL("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS kv ("
  93:                     + "k TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, v TEXT)");
  94: 
  95:             // 英语闯关成绩。之前 right/wrong 只是 EngView 里的两个内存变量，
  96:             // 退出界面就归零，一行都没落过盘 —— 排行榜要用的数据以前根本不存在。
  97:             // client_rev 是幂等键（uid+startedAt+mode 的哈希），防止重复上报。
  98:             d.execSQL("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS eng_round ("
  99:                     + "id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,"
 100:                     + "d TEXT NOT NULL, mode INTEGER NOT NULL,"
 101:                     + " r INTEGER NOT NULL, w INTEGER NOT NULL, ms INTEGER NOT NULL,"
 102:                     + " started INTEGER NOT NULL, rev TEXT UNIQUE, up INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0)");
 103: 
 104:             d.execSQL("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_done_d ON done(d)");
 105:             d.execSQL("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_timing_d ON timing(d)");
 106:             d.execSQL("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_wrong_s ON wrong(s)");
 107:         }
 108: 
 109:         @Override
 110:         public void onUpgrade(SQLiteDatabase d, int o, int n) {
 111:             // v1 → v2：加错题本表。只加表，不动已有进度。
 112:             if (o < 2) {
 113:                 d.execSQL("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS wrong ("
 114:                         + "i TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, s TEXT, q TEXT,"
 115:                         + " ts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, n INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1)");
 116:                 d.execSQL("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_wrong_s ON wrong(s)");
 117:             }
 118:             // v2 → v3：加英语闯关成绩表。只加表，不动已有进度。
 119:             if (o < 3) {
 120:                 d.execSQL("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS eng_round ("
 121:                         + "id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,"
 122:                         + "d TEXT NOT NULL, mode INTEGER NOT NULL,"
 123:                         + " r INTEGER NOT NULL, w INTEGER NOT NULL, ms INTEGER NOT NULL,"
 124:                         + " started INTEGER NOT NULL, rev TEXT UNIQUE, up INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0)");
 125:             }
 126:         }
 127:     }
 128: 
 129:     // ── 载入 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
 130:     private void loadAll() {
 131:         SQLiteDatabase r = db.getReadableDatabase();
 132: 
 133:         Cursor c = r.rawQuery("SELECT d, i FROM done WHERE v=1", null);
 134:         while (c.moveToNext()) done.add(key(c.getString(0), c.getString(1)));
 135:         c.close();
 136: 
 137:         c = r.rawQuery("SELECT d FROM committed", null);
 138:         while (c.moveToNext()) completed.add(c.getString(0));
 139:         c.close();
 140: 
 141:         c = r.rawQuery("SELECT d, i, ms FROM timing", null);
 142:         while (c.moveToNext()) timing.put(key(c.getString(0), c.getString(1)), c.getLong(2));
 143:         c.close();
 144: 
 145:         c = r.rawQuery("SELECT d, i, t FROM notes", null);
 146:         while (c.moveToNext()) {
 147:             String t = c.getString(2);
 148:             notes.put(key(c.getString(0), c.getString(1)), t == null ? "" : t);
 149:         }
 150:         c.close();
 151: 
 152:         c = r.rawQuery("SELECT i, lv FROM hard WHERE lv>0", null);
 153:         while (c.moveToNext()) hard.put(c.getString(0), c.getInt(1));
 154:         c.close();
 155: 
 156:         r.close();
 157:     }
 158: 
 159:     /** 老版本把整份 JSON 存在 SharedPreferences；有就迁进来。 */
 160:     private void migrateLegacy() {
 161:         try {
 162:             if (db.getReadableDatabase().rawQuery(
 163:                     "SELECT v FROM kv WHERE k='migrated'", null).getCount() > 0) {
 164:                 return;   // 已经迁过
 165:             }
 166:             String old = legacy.getString("state_json", null);
 167:             if (old == null || old.length() < 10) return;
 168:             deserialize(old);        // 填进内存
 169:             writeNow();              // 立刻落库
 170:             db.getWritableDatabase().execSQL(
 171:                     "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO kv(k,v) VALUES('migrated',?)",
 172:                     new Object[]{nowIso()});
 173:         } catch (Exception ignored) {
 174:             // 迁移失败就当新用户，不让 App 起不来
 175:         }
 176:     }
 177: 
 178:     // ── 写库（关键动作立即写）──────────────────────────────
 179:     @Override
 180:     public void setDone(String date, String id, boolean v) {
 181:         super.setDone(date, id, v);
 182:         SQLiteDatabase w = db.getWritableDatabase();
 183:         if (v) {
 184:             ContentValues cv = new ContentValues();
 185:             cv.put("d", date);
 186:             cv.put("i", id);
 187:             cv.put("v", 1);
 188:             w.insertWithOnConflict("done", null, cv, SQLiteDatabase.CONFLICT_REPLACE);
 189:         } else {
 190:             w.delete("done", "d=? AND i=?", new String[]{date, id});
 191:         }
 192:     }
 193: 
 194:     @Override
 195:     public void commitDay(String date) {
 196:         super.commitDay(date);
 197:         ContentValues cv = new ContentValues();
 198:         cv.put("d", date);
 199:         db.getWritableDatabase().insertWithOnConflict(
 200:                 "committed", null, cv, SQLiteDatabase.CONFLICT_REPLACE);
 201:     }
 202:

 204:     public void uncommitDay(String date) {
 205:         super.uncommitDay(date);
 206:         db.getWritableDatabase().delete("committed", "d=?", new String[]{date});
 207:     }

 210:     public void addTiming(String date, String id, long ms) {

 212:         ContentValues cv = new ContentValues();
 213:         cv.put("d", date);
 214:         cv.put("i", id);
 215:         cv.put("ms", timingOf(date, id));
 216:         db.getWritableDatabase().insertWithOnConflict(
 217:                 "timing", null, cv, SQLiteDatabase.CONFLICT_REPLACE);
 218:     }

 220:     @Override
 221:     public void bumpHard(String id) {
 222:         super.bumpHard(id);
 223:         saveHard(id);
 224:     }
 225: 
 226:     @Override
 227:     public void clearHard(String id) {
 228:         super.clearHard(id);
 229:         saveHard(id);
 230:     }
 231: 
 232:     private void saveHard(String id) {
 233:         SQLiteDatabase w = db.getWritableDatabase();
 234:         int lv = hardLevel(id);
 235:         if (lv <= 0) {
 236:             w.delete("hard", "i=?", new String[]{id});
 237:         } else {
 238:             ContentValues cv = new ContentValues();
 239:             cv.put("i", id);
 240:             cv.put("lv", lv);
 241:             w.insertWithOnConflict("hard", null, cv, SQLiteDatabase.CONFLICT_REPLACE);
 242:         }
 243:     }
 244: 
 245:     // ── 错题本 ────────────────────────────────────────────
 246:     /**
 247:      * 记录一道做错的题。同一题重复做错只累加次数，不重复占行。
 248:      * 只存题号 + 学科 + 题干摘要，不存答案 —— 题库更新后仍能对上。
 249:      */
 250:     public void addWrong(String qid, String subject, String stem, String extra) {
 251:         if (qid == null || qid.isEmpty()) return;
 252:         String s = stem == null ? "" : stem;
 253:         if (s.length() > 80) s = s.substring(0, 80);
 254:         SQLiteDatabase w = db.getWritableDatabase();
 255:         // 先查是否已存在，存在就 n+1
 256:         Cursor c = w.rawQuery("SELECT n FROM wrong WHERE i=?", new String[]{qid});
 257:         int n = 0;
 258:         while (c.moveToNext()) n = c.getInt(0);
 259:         c.close();
 260:         ContentValues cv = new ContentValues();
 261:         cv.put("i", qid);
 262:         cv.put("s", subject == null ? "" : subject);
 263:         cv.put("q", s);
 264:         cv.put("ts", System.currentTimeMillis());
 265:         cv.put("n", n + 1);
 266:         w.insertWithOnConflict("wrong", null, cv, SQLiteDatabase.CONFLICT_REPLACE);
 267:     }
 268: 
 269:     /** 取某科错题 id 列表；subject 传 null 或空串则取全部。 */
 270:     public List<String> wrongIds(String subject) {
 271:         List<String> out = new ArrayList<>();
 272:         SQLiteDatabase r = db.getReadableDatabase();
 273:         Cursor c;
 274:         if (subject == null || subject.isEmpty()) {
 275:             c = r.rawQuery("SELECT i FROM wrong ORDER BY ts DESC", null);
 276:         } else {
 277:             c = r.rawQuery("SELECT i FROM wrong WHERE s=? ORDER BY ts DESC",
 278:                     new String[]{subject});
 279:         }
 280:         while (c.moveToNext()) out.add(c.getString(0));
 281:         c.close();
 282:         return out;
 283:     }
 284: 
 285:     /** 错题本总数。 */
 286:     public int wrongCount() {
 287:         SQLiteDatabase r = db.getReadableDatabase();
 288:         Cursor c = r.rawQuery("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM wrong", null);
 289:         int n = 0;
 290:         while (c.moveToNext()) n = c.getInt(0);
 291:         c.close();
 292:         return n;
 293:     }
 294: 
 295:     /** 答对后从错题本移除。 */
 296:     public void removeWrong(String qid) {
 297:         db.getWritableDatabase().delete("wrong", "i=?", new String[]{qid});
 298:     }

 300:     @Override
 301:     public void resetAll() {
 302:         super.resetAll();
 303:         SQLiteDatabase w = db.getWritableDatabase();
 304:         w.delete("done", null, null);
 305:         w.delete("committed", null, null);
 306:         w.delete("timing", null, null);
 307:         w.delete("notes", null, null);
 308:         w.delete("hard", null, null);
 309:     }
 310: 
 311:     /** 默写框每敲一个字都走这里 —— 700ms 合并一次写盘。 */
 312:     @Override
 313:     public void setNote(String date, String id, String text) {
 314:         super.setNote(date, id, text);
 315:         saveSoon();
 316:     }
 317: 
 318:     private void saveSoon() {
 319:         if (savePending) return;
 320:         savePending = true;
 321:         saveHandler.postDelayed(saveTask, 700);
 322:     }
 323: 
 324:     /** 立即写盘。切后台 / 退出前必须调，否则最后 700ms 的输入会丢。 */
 325:     public void flush() {
 326:         if (savePending) {
 327:             saveHandler.removeCallbacks(saveTask);
 328:             savePending = false;
 329:         }
 330:         writeNow();
 331:     }
 332: 
 333:     public void save() {
 334:         if (savePending) return;
 335:         writeNow();
 336:     }
 337: 
 338:     /** 把内存里的全部状态整体刷进库（导入/迁移后用）。 */
 339:     private void writeNow() {
 340:         SQLiteDatabase w = db.getWritableDatabase();
 341:         w.beginTransaction();
 342:         try {
 343:             w.delete("done", null, null);
 344:             w.delete("committed", null, null);
 345:             w.delete("timing", null, null);
 346:             w.delete("notes", null, null);
 347:             w.delete("hard", null, null);
 348: 
 349:             for (String k : done) {
 350:                 int p = k.indexOf('|');
 351:                 if (p <= 0) continue;
 352:                 ContentValues cv = new ContentValues();
 353:                 cv.put("d", k.substring(0, p));
 354:                 cv.put("i", k.substring(p + 1));
 355:                 cv.put("v", 1);
 356:                 w.insertWithOnConflict("done", null, cv, SQLiteDatabase.CONFLICT_REPLACE);
 357:             }
 358:             for (String d : completed) {
 359:                 ContentValues cv = new ContentValues();
 360:                 cv.put("d", d);
 361:                 w.insertWithOnConflict("committed", null, cv, SQLiteDatabase.CONFLICT_REPLACE);
 362:             }
 363:             for (java.util.Map.Entry<String, Long> e : timing.entrySet()) {
 364:                 int p = e.getKey().indexOf('|');
 365:                 if (p <= 0) continue;
 366:                 ContentValues cv = new ContentValues();
 367:                 cv.put("d", e.getKey().substring(0, p));
 368:                 cv.put("i", e.getKey().substring(p + 1));
 369:                 cv.put("ms", e.getValue());
 370:                 w.insertWithOnConflict("timing", null, cv, SQLiteDatabase.CONFLICT_REPLACE);
 371:             }
 372:             for (java.util.Map.Entry<String, String> e : notes.entrySet()) {
 373:                 int p = e.getKey().indexOf('|');
 374:                 if (p <= 0) continue;
 375:                 ContentValues cv = new ContentValues();
 376:                 cv.put("d", e.getKey().substring(0, p));
 377:                 cv.put("i", e.getKey().substring(p + 1));
 378:                 cv.put("t", e.getValue());
 379:                 w.insertWithOnConflict("notes", null, cv, SQLiteDatabase.CONFLICT_REPLACE);
 380:             }
 381:             for (java.util.Map.Entry<String, Integer> e : hard.entrySet()) {
 382:                 if (e.getValue() == null || e.getValue() <= 0) continue;
 383:                 ContentValues cv = new ContentValues();
 384:                 cv.put("i", e.getKey());
 385:                 cv.put("lv", e.getValue());
 386:                 w.insertWithOnConflict("hard", null, cv, SQLiteDatabase.CONFLICT_REPLACE);
 387:             }
 388:             w.setTransactionSuccessful();
 389:         } finally {
 390:             w.endTransaction();
 391:         }
 392:     }
 393: 
 394:     /** 导出 / 生成进度码前需要一份最新快照。 */
 395:     public String exportSnapshot() {
 396:         flush();
 397:         return exportJson();
 398:     }
 399: 
 400:     // ── 英语闯关成绩 ──────────────────────────────────────
 401: 
 402:     /** 一轮成绩。对应 eng_round 一行。 */
 403:     public static class EngRound {
 404:         public long id;
 405:         public String day = "";
 406:         public int mode;
 407:         public int right;
 408:         public int wrong;
 409:         public long ms;
 410:         public long started;
 411:         public String rev = "";
 412:         public boolean uploaded;
 413:         public int total() { return right + wrong; }
 414:         public int rate() { return total() == 0 ? 0 : right * 100 / total(); }
 415:     }
 416: 
 417:     /**
 418:      * 记一轮成绩。
 419:      *
 420:      * 幂等：rev 有 UNIQUE 约束，同一个 rev 重复调用不会写第二条。
 421:      * rev 由调用方用 uid+started+mode 算，这样「上报成功了但没收到响应、
 422:      * 用户又重进了一次」不会在榜上算两次。
 423:      */
 424:     public void addEngRound(EngRound r) {
 425:         if (r == null) return;
 426:         ContentValues cv = new ContentValues();
 427:         cv.put("d", r.day);
 428:         cv.put("mode", r.mode);
 429:         cv.put("r", r.right);
 430:         cv.put("w", r.wrong);
 431:         cv.put("ms", r.ms);
 432:         cv.put("started", r.started);
 433:         cv.put("rev", r.rev);
 434:         cv.put("up", r.uploaded ? 1 : 0);
 435:         db.getWritableDatabase().insertWithOnConflict(
 436:                 "eng_round", null, cv, SQLiteDatabase.CONFLICT_IGNORE);
 437:     }
 438: 
 439:     /** 读成绩。sinceDay 非空时只取那天及以后（按字符串比较，ISO 日期正好可比）。 */
 440:     public List<EngRound> engRounds(String sinceDay) {
 441:         List<EngRound> out = new ArrayList<>();
 442:         Cursor c = sinceDay == null || sinceDay.isEmpty()
 443:                 ? db.getReadableDatabase().rawQuery(
 444:                         "SELECT id,d,mode,r,w,ms,started,rev,up FROM eng_round ORDER BY started DESC", null)
 445:                 : db.getReadableDatabase().rawQuery(
 446:                         "SELECT id,d,mode,r,w,ms,started,rev,up FROM eng_round WHERE d>=? ORDER BY started DESC",
 447:                         new String[]{sinceDay});
 448:         while (c.moveToNext()) {
 449:             EngRound r = new EngRound();
 450:             r.id = c.getLong(0);
 451:             r.day = c.getString(1);
 452:             r.mode = c.getInt(2);
 453:             r.right = c.getInt(3);
 454:             r.wrong = c.getInt(4);
 455:             r.ms = c.getLong(5);
 456:             r.started = c.getLong(6);
 457:             r.rev = c.getString(7) == null ? "" : c.getString(7);
 458:             r.uploaded = c.getInt(8) != 0;
 459:             out.add(r);
 460:         }
 461:         c.close();
 462:         return out;
 463:     }
 464: 
 465:     /** 还没传上去的成绩。 */
 466:     public List<EngRound> pendingEngRounds() {
 467:         List<EngRound> out = new ArrayList<>();
 468:         for (EngRound r : engRounds(null)) if (!r.uploaded) out.add(r);
 469:         return out;
 470:     }
 471: 
 472:     /** 标记成已上传。按 rev 定位，不依赖自增 id。 */
 473:     public void markEngUploaded(String rev) {
 474:         if (rev == null || rev.isEmpty()) return;
 475:         ContentValues cv = new ContentValues();
 476:         cv.put("up", 1);
 477:         db.getWritableDatabase().update("eng_round", cv, "rev=?", new String[]{rev});
 478:     }
 479: 
 480:     /** 今日（或指定日）汇总，给「我的正确率」用。 */
 481:     public int[] engDaily(String day) {
 482:         // [轮数, 对题, 错题, 毫秒]
 483:         int[] out = new int[4];
 484:         Cursor c = db.getReadableDatabase().rawQuery(
 485:                 "SELECT COUNT(*), SUM(r), SUM(w), SUM(ms) FROM eng_round WHERE d=?", new String[]{day});
 486:         if (c.moveToNext()) {
 487:             out[0] = c.getInt(0);
 488:             out[1] = c.getInt(1);
 489:             out[2] = c.getInt(2);
 490:             out[3] = (int) Math.min(Integer.MAX_VALUE, c.getLong(3));
 491:         }
 492:         c.close();
 493:         return out;
 494:     }
 495: }

  23: /**
  24:  * 云同步界面 —— 一个全屏 Dialog：填 token、填 gist id、两个按钮、一行状态。
  25:  *
  26:  * 为什么不直接吃 Store：
  27:  *   同步界面只需要「要一份本地 JSON」和「把一份 JSON 写回去」这两件事。
  28:  *   定义 LocalStore 两个方法让主界面注入，SyncView 就完全不认识 Store 的内部结构；
  29:  *   以后进度表结构再改，这里一行都不用动。
  30:  *
  31:  * 用法（主界面里加这几行即可）：
  32:  *   new SyncView(this, new SyncView.LocalStore() {
  33:  *       @Override public String exportJson() { return store.exportSnapshot(); }
  34:  *       @Override public void importJson(String json) {
  35:  *           try { store.importJson(json); } catch (Exception ignored) { }
  36:  *           store.flush();
  37:  *           render();
  38:  *       }
  39:  *   }).show();
  40:  *
  41:  * 安全约定：
  42:  *   · token 只存 SharedPreferences("zsb_sync")，不进 SQLite、不进日志、不上报
  43:  *   · 输入框是密码型，屏幕上不回显
  44:  */
  45: public class SyncView {
  46: 
  47:     // ── 本地进度的两个注入点 ──────────────────────────────
  48:     public interface LocalStore {
  49:         /** 导出当前本地进度为 JSON 字符串（与 StateLogic.exportJson 同构）。 */
  50:         String exportJson();
  51:         /** 用给定 JSON 覆盖本地进度。实现方负责异常兜底。 */
  52:         void importJson(String json);
  53:     }
  54: 
  55:     /** 同步结束后的可选回调，主界面要刷新时可以接。 */
  56:     public interface OnDone {
  57:         void onDone(boolean changedLocally);
  58:     }

 242:         body.addView(btnClose, clp);

 245:         root.addView(scroller, new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
 246:                 ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, 0, 1f));
 247:

 249:     }

 283: 
 284:     // ── 偏好存取 ──────────────────────────────────────────
 285:     private void loadPrefs() {
 286:         etToken.setText(sp.getString(K_TOKEN, ""));
 287:         etGist.setText(sp.getString(K_GIST, ""));
 288:     }
 289: 
 290:     private void savePrefs() {
 291:         sp.edit()
 292:                 .putString(K_TOKEN, text(etToken).trim())
 293:                 .putString(K_GIST, text(etGist).trim())
 294:                 .apply();
 295:     }
 296: 
 297:     private static String text(EditText e) {
 298:         return e == null || e.getText() == null ? "" : e.getText().toString();
 299:     }
 300: 
 301:     // ── 上传：拉 → 合并 → 推 → 写回本地 ───────────────────
 302:     private void doPush() {
 303:         if (busy) return;
 304:         final String token = text(etToken).trim();
 305:         final String gistId = text(etGist).trim();
 306:         if (token.isEmpty()) {
 307:             status(C_RED, "先填 GitHub Token");
 308:             etToken.requestFocus();
 309:             return;
 310:         }
 311:         if (store == null) {
 312:             status(C_RED, "本地进度不可用，无法导出");
 313:             return;
 314:         }
 315: 
 316:         String localJson;
 317:         try {
 318:             localJson = store.exportJson();
 319:         } catch (Throwable t) {
 320:             status(C_RED, "导出本地进度失败：" + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
 321:             return;
 322:         }
 323:         if (localJson == null || localJson.trim().isEmpty()) localJson = "{}";
 324: 
 325:         savePrefs();
 326:         start("正在读取云端…");
 327: 
 328:         final GistSync gs = new GistSync(token, gistId);
 329:         final String local = localJson;
 330: 
 331:         // 先拉云端：否则上传会直接盖掉另一台设备刚记的进度
 332:         gs.pullAsync(new GistSync.Cb() {
 333:             @Override public void ok(String remote) {
 334:                 if (remote != null && !remote.trim().isEmpty()) {
 335:                     String merged = GistSync.merge(local, remote);
 336:                     applyLocal(merged);
 337:                     status(C_FG2, "云端已有进度，已合并后上传…");
 338:                     pushMerged(gs, merged);
 339:                 } else {
 340:                     status(C_FG2, gistId.isEmpty()
 341:                             ? "首次同步，正在创建 Gist…" : "云端还没有进度文件，直接上传…");
 342:                     pushMerged(gs, local);
 343:                 }
 344:             }
 345: 
 346:             @Override public void fail(String msg) {
 347:                 // 读不到云端（比如 gist 被删 / 首次创建）时，退化成直接推本地
 348:                 status(C_AMBER, msg + "；改为直接上传本机进度…");
 349:                 pushMerged(gs, local);
 350:             }
 351:         });
 352:     }
 353: 
 354:     private void pushMerged(GistSync gs, final String json) {
 355:         gs.pushAsync(json, new GistSync.Cb() {
 356:             @Override public void ok(String id) {
 357:                 if (id != null && !id.isEmpty()) {
 358:                     etGist.setText(id);
 359:                     sp.edit().putString(K_GIST, id).apply();
 360:                 }
 361:                 done(C_TEAL, "已上传到云端。Gist ID：" + (id == null ? "" : id));
 362:             }
 363: 
 364:             @Override public void fail(String msg) {
 365:                 done(C_RED, "上传失败：" + msg);
 366:             }
 367:         });
 368:     }
 369: 
 370:     // ── 恢复：拉 → 合并 → 写回本地 → 再推一次 ──────────────
 371:     private void doPull() {
 372:         if (busy) return;
 373:         final String token = text(etToken).trim();
 374:         final String gistId = text(etGist).trim();
 375:         if (token.isEmpty()) {
 376:             status(C_RED, "先填 GitHub Token");
 377:             etToken.requestFocus();
 378:             return;
 379:         }
 380:         if (gistId.isEmpty()) {
 381:             status(C_RED, "先填 Gist ID，或用「上传到云端」自动创建一个");
 382:             etGist.requestFocus();
 383:             return;
 384:         }
 385: 
 386:         savePrefs();
 387:         start("正在从云端读取…");
 388: 
 389:         final GistSync gs = new GistSync(token, gistId);
 390:         gs.pullAsync(new GistSync.Cb() {
 391:             @Override public void ok(String remote) {
 392:                 if (remote == null || remote.trim().isEmpty()) {
 393:                     done(C_AMBER, "云端还没有这个文件，先点「上传到云端」");
 394:                     return;
 395:                 }
 396:                 String localJson;
 397:                 try {
 398:                     localJson = store == null ? "{}" : store.exportJson();
 399:                 } catch (Throwable t) {
 400:                     localJson = "{}";
 401:                 }
 402:                 // 恢复不是覆盖：两边的完成记录都保留，避免恢复把本机进度冲掉
 403:                 String merged = GistSync.merge(localJson, remote);
 404:                 boolean changed = applyLocal(merged);
 405:                 String sum = summary(merged);
 406:                 if (changed) {
 407:                     status(C_FG2, "已写入本机，正在把合并结果回传云端…");
 408:                     gs.pushAsync(merged, new GistSync.Cb() {
 409:                         @Override public void ok(String id) {
 410:                             if (id != null && !id.isEmpty()) {
 411:                                 etGist.setText(id);
 412:                                 sp.edit().putString(K_GIST, id).apply();
 413:                             }
 414:                             done(C_TEAL, "恢复完成。" + sum);
 415:                         }
 416: 
 417:                         @Override public void fail(String msg) {
 418:                             done(C_AMBER, "本机已恢复，但回传云端失败：" + msg);
 419:                         }
 420:                     });
 421:                 } else {
 422:                     done(C_TEAL, "已恢复。" + sum);
 423:                 }
 424:             }
 425: 
 426:             @Override public void fail(String msg) {
 427:                 done(C_RED, "读取失败：" + msg);
 428:             }
 429:         });
 430:     }
 431: 
 432:     /**
 433:      * 把 JSON 写回本地。返回 true 表示确实动了本地数据。
 434:      * 实现方（主模型那侧）负责 flush 和刷新界面。
 435:      */
 436:     private boolean applyLocal(String json) {
 437:         if (store == null || json == null || json.isEmpty()) return false;
 438:         try {
 439:             store.importJson(json);
 440:             localChanged = true;
 441:             return true;
 442:         } catch (Throwable t) {
 443:             status(C_RED, "写入本机失败：" + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
 444:             return false;
 445:         }
 446:     }
 447: 
 448:     /** 合并结果的人话摘要：完成几天、勾了几项。 */
 449:     private static String summary(String json) {
 450:         try {
 451:             JSONObject root = new JSONObject(json);
 452:             JSONObject st = root.optJSONObject("state");
 453:             if (st == null) st = root;
 454:             int done = sizeOf(st.optJSONObject("done"));
 455:             int days = sizeOf(st.optJSONObject("completed"));
 456:             return "已勾选 " + done + " 项，已推进 " + days + " 天。";
 457:         } catch (Throwable t) {
 458:             return "";
 459:         }
 460:     }
 461: 
 462:     private static int sizeOf(JSONObject o) {
 463:         if (o == null) return 0;
 464:         int n = 0;
 465:         for (Iterator<String> it = o.keys(); it.hasNext(); ) {
 466:             it.next();
 467:             n++;
 468:         }
 469:         return n;
 470:     }
 471: 
 472:     // ── 状态与忙闲 ────────────────────────────────────────
 473:     private void start(String msg) {
 474:         busy = true;
 475:         localChanged = false;
 476:         setButtons(false);
 477:         status(C_FG2, msg);
 478:     }
 479: 
 480:     private void done(int color, String msg) {
 481:         busy = false;
 482:         setButtons(true);
 483:         status(color, msg);
 484:         if (onDone != null) {
 485:             boolean changed = localChanged;
 486:             try {
 487:                 onDone.onDone(changed);
 488:             } catch (Throwable ignored) {
 489:                 // 主界面刷新失败不影响同步结果
 490:             }
 491:         }
 492:     }
 493: 
 494:     private void setButtons(boolean enabled) {
 495:         if (btnPush != null) {
 496:             btnPush.setEnabled(enabled);
 497:             btnPush.setAlpha(enabled ? 1f : 0.45f);
 498:         }
 499:         if (btnPull != null) {
 500:             btnPull.setEnabled(enabled);
 501:             btnPull.setAlpha(enabled ? 1f : 0.45f);
 502:         }
 503:     }
 504: 
 505:     private void status(int color, String msg) {
 506:         if (tvStatus == null) return;
 507:         tvStatus.setTextColor(color);
 508:         tvStatus.setText(msg == null ? "" : msg);
 509:     }
 510: 
 511:     private void dismiss() {
 512:         savePrefs();
 513:         if (dlg != null && dlg.isShowing()) dlg.dismiss();
 514:         dlg = null;
 515:     }
 516: }

  24: /**
  25:  * App 内更新。
  26:  *
  27:  * 流程：启动后静默查 GitHub Release 最新版 → 有新版才弹窗 →
  28:  * 下载 APK 到 cache → 拉起系统安装器覆盖安装。
  29:  *
  30:  * 版本比较用 versionName（1.1.0 这种）换算成同单位整数，
  31:  * 不用 versionCode —— 因为 versionCode 是构建计数（10、11、12），
  32:  * 和 tag 里的 1.1.0 不是一套单位。混着比会永远判定「有新版」，
  33:  * 每次启动都弹框，那比没有更新功能更烦。
  34:  *
  35:  * 签名必须始终用同一把钥匙（指纹 05:24:6E:...），否则系统拒绝覆盖，
  36:  * 只能卸载重装 —— 那样进度就没了。
  37:  */
  38: public class Update {
  39: 
  40:     /**
  41:      * 更新**主通道**：用户自有域名（Cloudflare Pages 托管，已绑自定义域名）。
  42:      *
  43:      * 2026-10-27 直连实测（不带代理，模拟手机）：
  44:      *   app.xxjcloudns.kdns.fr/update.json   HTTP 200
  45:      *   app.xxjcloudns.kdns.fr/*.apk          HTTP 206，可断点续传，速度 288KB/s~4.9MB/s
  46:      *   zsb-update.pages.dev                 HTTP 200 / 810ms（兜底）
  47:      *   github.com                           HTTP 000 / 超时 12s   ← 被阻断
  48:      *   api.github.com                       HTTP 200 / 321ms     ← 通，二级兜底
  49:      *
  50:      * ── 2026-10-28 更正（之前的「账号级 1101 故障」是误判）─────────
  51:      *
  52:      * 实测结果：
  53:      *   *.workers.dev                        → DNS 污染，每次解析出不同 IP
  54:      *                                          （202.160.129.6 / 103.73.161.52
  55:      *                                            / 74.86.142.55），TCP 443 全部超时
  56:      *   cloudflare.com / pages.dev / 自定义域名 → 全部正常
  57:      *   github.com                           → 仍被阻断
  58:      *
  59:      * 也就是说 Worker 运行时一直是好的，坏的是 workers.dev 这个后缀的路由。
  60:      * 中途把 Worker 绑到自有域名 dl.xxjcloudns.kdns.fr 绕过这个问题，
  61:      * 但 2026-10-28 按用户决定把 Worker 整套撤掉了：Pages 与 Workers 的
  62:      * 单文件上限、免费额度完全一致，没必要同时维护两套部署。
  63:      * 现在书 PDF 与 APK 都在 Pages，一个域名管完。
  64:      */
  65:     private static final String PRIMARY_JSON =
  66:             "https://app.xxjcloudns.kdns.fr/update.json";
  67: 
  68:     // 2026-10-28：Worker 那条 dl.xxjcloudns.kdns.fr 兜底通道按用户决定整条撤掉。
  69:     // Worker 运行时本身没毛病，坏的是 workers.dev 后缀的路由（DNS 污染），
  70:     // 后来绑自有域名 dl 绕过 —— 但既然书 PDF 和 APK 都已经统一放在 Pages，
  71:     // 再维持第二套部署只是多一个会失联的源。现在只剩三个通道：
  72:     //   app.xxjcloudns.kdns.fr（自有域名，主）→ zsb-update.pages.dev（兜底）
  73:     //   → api.github.com（本地 Pages 全挂时才试）
  74:     // 老机器（v1.5.7 及更早）读 dl 会 404，但 fetchLatestAny 是「取最高版本、
  75:     // 单源失败即忽略」，不是「读到即返回」，所以缺这一条不影响它们升级 ——
  76:     // 它们读自己的 dl 清单拿到的是旧版，而主通道给的是新版，取最大值仍然是新版。
  77: 
  78:     /**
  79:      * 教材 PDF 的下载基地址。
  80:      *
  81:      * 三本教材共 11.7 MB，占安装包 60%，而 Cloudflare Pages 和
  82:      * Workers Static Assets 的**单文件上限都是 25 MiB**（2026-10-28 查文档确认），
  83:      * APK 20.13 MB 看着还够，但再塞两本练习册就顶死了。
  84:      * 所以 PDF 从安装包里拿掉，改成第一次看原图时按需拉这一本。
  85:      * 路径与 Worker 静态资源一致：/<bookId>.pdf
  86:      */
  87:     // 2026-10-28：Worker 已按用户决定撤掉（Pages 与 Workers 单文件上限、免费额度一致，
  88:     // 没必要跑两套）。书 PDF 与 APK 统一放在 Pages，这条路一个域名管完。
  89:     public static final String BOOK_BASE = "https://app.xxjcloudns.kdns.fr/books/";
  90: 
  91:     private static final String PAGES_JSON =
  92:             "https://zsb-update.pages.dev/update.json";
  93: 
  94:     /** 兜底：Pages 挂了时退到 GitHub API（注意是 api 域名，不是 github.com）。 */
  95:     private static final String API =
  96:             "https://api.github.com/repos/bianhongjun38/zhuansheng/releases/latest";
  97: 
  98:     /**
  99:      * GitHub 侧的兜底下载地址，由 findApk() 在探测版本时顺手记下。
 100:      *
 101:      * 2026-10-28 多轮实测（每条约 1MB，各连 5 次，间隔 1s）：
 102:      *   Pages 自有域名      成功 5/5   中位 536 KB/s  区间 504~550
 103:      *   GitHub release 资产 成功 1/5   中位 789 KB/s  四次 RemoteDisconnected
 104:      *   GitHub api 资产端点 成功 5/5   中位   7 KB/s  等于不通
 105:      *
 106:      * 结论：GitHub 在国内是「时通时断」，不能用单一实测的漂亮数字当结论
 107:      * （同一台机器 10 分钟内从 6.6 MB/s 掉到 1/5 成功率）。
 108:      * 所以顺序必须是 Pages 优先，GitHub 只在本机 Pages 全失败时才试。
 109:      */
 110:     private volatile String fallbackGithubUrl;
 111: 
 112:     private final Activity act;
 113:     private final Handler ui = new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper());
 114: 
 115:     public Update(Activity a) {
 116:         this.act = a;
 117:     }
 118: 
 119:     /** 启动时调用。silent=true 时只在有新版本才提示。 */
 120:     public void check(final boolean silent) {
 121:         new Thread(new Runnable() {
 122:             public void run() {
 123:                 try {
 124:                     final Latest lt = fetchLatestAny();
 125:                     if (lt == null) {
 126:                         if (!silent) toastOnUi("检查失败：网络不通");
 127:                         return;
 128:                     }
 129:                     final int remoteCode = lt.code;
 130:                     final int localCode = parseVer(localVersionName());
 131:                     if (remoteCode == 0) {
 132:                         if (!silent) toastOnUi("已是最新版本 v" + localVersionName());
 133:                         return;
 134:                     }
 135:                     if (remoteCode <= localCode) {
 136:                         if (!silent) toastOnUi("已是最新版本 v" + localVersionName());
 137:                         return;
 138:                     }
 139:                     if (lt.apkUrl == null) {
 140:                         if (!silent) toastOnUi("新版没有找到安装包");
 141:                         return;
 142:                     }
 143:                     ui.post(new Runnable() {
 144:                         public void run() { askUpdate(lt.ver, lt.notes, buildSources(lt)); }
 145:                     });
 146:                 } catch (Exception e) {
 147:                     if (!silent) toastOnUi("检查失败：" + e.getClass().getSimpleName());
 148:                 }
 149:             }
 150:         }).start();
 151:     }
 152: 
 153:     /** 统一后的「最新版」信息，两个来源都归一到这个结构。 */
 154:     static class Latest {
 155:         String ver;
 156:         int code;
 157:         String notes;
 158:         String apkUrl;
 159:     }
 160: 
 161:     /**
 162:      * 三个来源都查一遍，取**版本号最高**的那个，而不是"第一个能读到就用"。
 163:      *
 164:      * 为什么必须改成取最大值：
 165:      * 前两条通道读的是我们自己域名上的 update.json —— 一份要手动跑
 166:      * 发版pages.py 才会更新的静态文件。忘了跑，它就永远停在旧版本。
 167:      * 旧实现里它排在第一位且"读到即返回"，于是：
 168:      *   update.json 停在 1.2.3，GitHub 上已经是 1.4.1，
 169:      *   本机 1.4.1 → 10203 <= 10401 → 判定「已是最新版本」。
 170:      * 明明线上有新版、明明 GitHub 通道完全正常，却被一份过期静态文件
 171:      * 整个挡住。这不是"某个源挂了"，是取值策略错了。
 172:      *
 173:      * 现在任何一份过期都只是被忽略，不会压住更高版本。
 174:      *
 175:      * 同版本时优先保留带下载地址的那份：update.json 可能在 APK 超过
 176:      * Pages 单文件上限时把 apk 置为 null，那时要能补上 GitHub 的地址。
 177:      */
 178:     private Latest fetchLatestAny() {
 179:         // 探测顺序保持"自有域名优先"——只是不再提前返回，
 180:         // 三份都要拿到手才能比出最高的那个。
 181:         Latest[] all = {
 182:                 fetchManifest(PRIMARY_JSON),        // app.xxjcloudns.kdns.fr（Pages，主通道）
 183:                 fetchManifest(PAGES_JSON),          // zsb-update.pages.dev（Pages 兜底）
 184:                 fetchFromGithub(),                  // api.github.com
 185:         };
 186: 
 187:         Latest best = null;
 188:         for (Latest lt : all) {
 189:             if (lt == null || lt.code == 0) continue;
 190:             if (best == null) { best = lt; continue; }
 191:             if (lt.code > best.code) {
 192:                 best = lt;
 193:             } else if (lt.code == best.code
 194:                     && best.apkUrl == null && lt.apkUrl != null) {
 195:                 // 版本一样但这份能下载，补上地址
 196:                 best.apkUrl = lt.apkUrl;
 197:             }
 198:         }
 199:         return best;
 200:     }
 201: 
 202:     /** 读一份 update.json 清单。 */
 203:     private Latest fetchManifest(String src) {
 204:         HttpURLConnection c = null;
 205:         try {
 206:             c = (HttpURLConnection) new URL(src).openConnection();
 207:             c.setConnectTimeout(8000);
 208:             c.setReadTimeout(10000);
 209:             c.setRequestProperty("User-Agent", "zsb-plan");
 210:             c.setRequestProperty("Cache-Control", "no-cache");
 211:             if (c.getResponseCode() != 200) return null;
 212:             String s = readAll(c.getInputStream());
 213:             JSONObject o = new JSONObject(s);
 214:             if (!o.optBoolean("ok", false)) return null;
 215:             String v = o.optString("versionName", "");
 216:             int code = o.optInt("versionInt", parseVer(v));
 217:             if (code == 0) return null;
 218:             Latest lt = new Latest();
 219:             lt.ver = v;
 220:             lt.code = code;
 221:             lt.notes = o.optString("notes", "");
 222:             JSONObject apk = o.optJSONObject("apk");
 223:             if (apk != null) lt.apkUrl = apk.optString("url", null);
 224:             return lt;
 225:         } catch (Exception e) {
 226:             return null;
 227:         } finally {
 228:             if (c != null) c.disconnect();
 229:         }
 230:     }
 231: 
 232:     /** 兜底通道：GitHub Release。 */
 233:     private Latest fetchFromGithub() {
 234:         JSONObject rel = fetchLatest();
 235:         if (rel == null) return null;
 236:         String tag = rel.optString("tag_name", "");
 237:         int code = parseVer(tag);
 238:         if (code == 0) return null;
 239:         Latest lt = new Latest();
 240:         lt.ver = tag.replaceFirst("^[vV]", "");
 241:         lt.code = code;
 242:         lt.notes = rel.optString("body", "");
 243:         lt.apkUrl = findApk(rel);
 244:         return lt;
 245:     }
 246: 
 247:     private static String readAll(InputStream in) throws Exception {
 248:         BufferedReader r = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(in, "UTF-8"));
 249:         StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
 250:         String ln;
 251:         while ((ln = r.readLine()) != null) sb.append(ln);
 252:         r.close();
 253:         return sb.toString();
 254:     }
 255: 
 256:     private JSONObject fetchLatest() {
 257:         HttpURLConnection c = null;
 258:         try {
 259:             c = (HttpURLConnection) new URL(API).openConnection();
 260:             c.setConnectTimeout(8000);
 261:             c.setReadTimeout(10000);
 262:             c.setRequestProperty("Accept", "application/vnd.github+json");
 263:             c.setRequestProperty("User-Agent", "zsb-plan");
 264:             if (c.getResponseCode() != 200) return null;
 265:             BufferedReader r = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(c.getInputStream(), "UTF-8"));
 266:             StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
 267:             String ln;
 268:             while ((ln = r.readLine()) != null) sb.append(ln);
 269:             r.close();
 270:             return new JSONObject(sb.toString());
 271:         } catch (Exception e) {
 272:             return null;
 273:         } finally {
 274:             if (c != null) c.disconnect();
 275:         }
 276:     }
 277: 
 278:     /** "1.1.0" / "v1.1.0" → 10100。解析不了返回 0。 */
 279:     static int parseVer(String s) {
 280:         if (s == null) return 0;
 281:         try {
 282:             String t = s.trim();
 283:             if (t.startsWith("v") || t.startsWith("V")) t = t.substring(1);
 284:             // 去掉可能的后缀，如 1.1.0-beta
 285:             int cut = t.length();
 286:             for (int i = 0; i < t.length(); i++) {
 287:                 char ch = t.charAt(i);
 288:                 if (ch != '.' && (ch < '0' || ch > '9')) { cut = i; break; }
 289:             }
 290:             t = t.substring(0, cut);
 291:             if (t.isEmpty()) return 0;
 292:             String[] p = t.split("\\.");
 293:             int maj = p.length > 0 ? Integer.parseInt(p[0]) : 0;
 294:             int min = p.length > 1 ? Integer.parseInt(p[1]) : 0;
 295:             int pat = p.length > 2 ? Integer.parseInt(p[2]) : 0;
 296:             return maj * 10000 + min * 100 + pat;
 297:         } catch (Exception e) {
 298:             return 0;
 299:         }
 300:     }
 301: 
 302:     private String localVersionName() {
 303:         try {
 304:             String n = act.getPackageManager()
 305:                     .getPackageInfo(act.getPackageName(), 0).versionName;
 306:             return n == null ? "0.0.0" : n;
 307:         } catch (Exception e) {
 308:             return "0.0.0";
 309:         }
 310:     }
 311: 
 312:     /** 给设置页显示用：本机版本号。 */
 313:     public String currentVersion() {
 314:         return localVersionName();
 315:     }
 316: 
 317:     /**
 318:      * 查询线上最新版本号，回调在主线程。
 319:      * 失败时给出 null，调用方负责显示「查询失败」。
 320:      *
 321:      * 与 check() 的区别：这个**只查不弹窗**，用于设置页按钮文案，
 322:      * 让用户一眼看到「本机 1.2.0 / 线上 1.2.1」。
 323:      */
 324:     public void queryLatest(final boolean silent, final OnLatest cb) {
 325:         new Thread(new Runnable() {
 326:             public void run() {
 327:                 final Latest lt = fetchLatestAny();
 328:                 final String tag = lt == null ? null : lt.ver;
 329:                 final int code = lt == null ? 0 : lt.code;
 330:                 ui.post(new Runnable() {
 331:                     public void run() {
 332:                         if (cb != null) cb.onLatest(tag, code);
 333:                         else if (!silent) {
 334:                             toastOnUi(tag == null ? "网络不通，稍后再试"
 335:                                     : "线上最新 v" + tag);
 336:                         }
 337:                     }
 338:                 });
 339:             }
 340:         }).start();
 341:     }
 342: 
 343:     /** queryLatest 的回调。tag 为 null 表示网络失败。 */
 344:     public interface OnLatest {
 345:         void onLatest(String tag, int code);
 346:     }
 347: 
 348:     /**
 349:      * 取安装包的下载地址。
 350:      *
 351:      * **必须用 api.github.com 的资产端点，不能用 browser_download_url。**
 352:      *
 353:      * 2026-10-27 实测（不带代理，模拟手机处境）：
 354:      *   api.github.com                       HTTP 200   321ms
 355:      *   github.com                           HTTP 000   超时 12s   ← 卡死在这里
 356:      *   release-assets.githubusercontent.com HTTP 404   388ms（域名可达）
 357:      *
 358:      * browser_download_url 是 https://github.com/.../releases/download/...，
 359:      * 也就是走被阻断的那个域名 —— 版本能查到、但一按下载就永远转圈。
 360:      *
 361:      * api.github.com/repos/.../releases/assets/{id} 带 Accept: octet-stream
 362:      * 会 302 到 release-assets.githubusercontent.com（那个域名直连是通的），
 363:      * 实测完整下载 745 KB/s、1.4 秒拿 1 MB。
 364:      */
 365:     private String findApk(JSONObject rel) {
 366:         try {
 367:             JSONArray arr = rel.getJSONArray("assets");
 368:             for (int i = 0; i < arr.length(); i++) {
 369:                 JSONObject a = arr.getJSONObject(i);
 370:                 String name = a.optString("name", "");
 371:                 if (!name.endsWith(".apk")) continue;
 372:                 String api = a.optString("url", "");
 373:                 if (api.startsWith("https://api.github.com/")) {
 374:                     fallbackGithubUrl = api;
 375:                     return api;
 376:                 }
 377:                 fallbackGithubUrl = a.optString("browser_download_url", null);
 378:                 // 兜底：万一副本接口没给 url，退回老地址（可能很慢但不至于不可用）
 379:                 return a.optString("browser_download_url", null);
 380:             }
 381:         } catch (Exception ignored) {
 382:         }
 383:         return null;
 384:     }
 385: 
 386:     /**
 387:      * 组装下载源列表，**顺序即优先级**。
 388:      *
 389:      * 第一档：本次探测到的清单里的 apk.url —— 也就是 Pages 自有域名，
 390:      *         实测 5/5 成功、504~550 KB/s、波动极小。
 391:      * 后两档：GitHub 的两个端点，只在前者失败时才会走到。
 392:      */
 393:     private java.util.List<String> buildSources(Latest lt) {
 394:         java.util.List<String> out = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 395:         if (lt != null && lt.apkUrl != null) out.add(lt.apkUrl);
 396:         if (fallbackGithubUrl != null) out.add(fallbackGithubUrl);
 397:         return out;
 398:     }
 399: 
 400:     private void askUpdate(String ver, String notes, final java.util.List<String> urls) {
 401:         final java.util.List<String> url = urls;
 402:         String body = "当前 v" + localVersionName() + "，最新 v" + ver + "。";
 403:         if (notes != null && notes.trim().length() > 0) {
 404:             String n = notes.trim();
 405:             body += "\n\n" + (n.length() > 500 ? n.substring(0, 500) + "…" : n);
 406:         }
 407:         body += "\n\n覆盖安装不会丢失学习进度。"
 408:                 + "\n如果提示「不允许安装」，在系统设置里允许本应用安装未知应用即可。";
 409: 
 410:         new AlertDialog.Builder(act)
 411:                 .setTitle("发现新版本 v" + ver)
 412:                 .setMessage(body)
 413:                 .setPositiveButton("立即更新", new DialogInterface.OnClickListener() {
 414:                     public void onClick(DialogInterface d, int w) { download(url); }
 415:                 })
 416:                 .setNegativeButton("以后再说", null)
 417:                 .show();
 418:     }
 419: 
 420:     /**
 421:      * 按顺序尝试多个下载源，任一成功即返回。
 422:      *
 423:      * 为什么要多源：本机 2026-10-28 实测——
 424:      *   github.com（release 资产，302 到 release-assets）完整 20.12 MB / 3.1s / 6.6 MB/s
 425:      *   app.xxjcloudns.kdns.fr（Pages，APK 3.65 MB / 2.9s / 1.3 MB/s）
 426:      * 两条都通，但国内线路质量会变（同一天不同时段、不同运营商差别很大）。
 427:      * 单一源一旦抽风，用户就只能干等或失败；多源按序退避最稳。
 428:      *
 429:      * 每次断线后不重下整个包：用 Range 从已下载的字节处续传。
 430:      * 20 MB 的包在国内掉一次线就全废，这个成本太高。
 431:      */
 432:     private void download(final java.util.List<String> urls) {
 433:         final java.util.List<String> sources = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 434:         for (String u : urls) if (u != null && !sources.contains(u)) sources.add(u);
 435: 
 436:         final ProgressDialog pd = new ProgressDialog(act);
 437:         pd.setTitle("正在下载更新");
 438:         pd.setMessage("0%");
 439:         pd.setCancelable(false);
 440:         pd.show();
 441: 
 442:         new Thread(new Runnable() {
 443:             public void run() {
 444:                 final File out = new File(act.getCacheDir(), "update.apk");
 445: 
 446:                 // 关键：每次下载前必须清掉上一次的残留。
 447:                 //
 448:                 // 以前这里不删。download() 成功路径从不 delete(out)，只有
 449:                 // "某个源失败"才删。于是用户装过一次后 /cache/update.apk
 450:                 // 永久保留旧安装包，下一次更新会拿它做 Range 续传：
 451:                 // 服务器回 416 → 旧代码把 416 当成功 → 安装旧包。
 452:                 // 现象就是"能收到更新提示，但装出来还是旧版本"，且永久复现。
 453:                 if (out.exists()) out.delete();
 454: 
 455:                 Exception last = null;
 456: 
 457:                 outer:
 458:                 for (String src : sources) {
 459:                     HttpURLConnection c = null;
 460:                     try {
 461:                         long got = fetchResumable(src, out, pd);
 462:                         if (got < 1024 * 1024) {
 463:                             throw new Exception("文件不完整（" + got + " 字节）");
 464:                         }
 465:                         // 必须是真正的 APK（zip 头 PK\x03\x04）。
 466:                         // 只有长度检查不够：断流时长度可能过线但内容残缺，
 467:                         // 那种文件系统装到一半会报"解析包出现问题"。
 468:                         if (!looksLikeApk(out)) {
 469:                             throw new Exception("下载内容不是有效安装包（" + got + " 字节）");
 470:                         }
 471:                         // 第三道校验：把包里真正的 versionName 读出来，
 472:                         // 必须**严格大于**本机版本，否则绝不给安装器。
 473:                         //
 474:                         // 2026-10-28 线上事故：Pages 的 _routes.json 是 SPA 回退，
 475:                         // 请求一个已下架的 apk 会返回 200 + index.html；
 476:                         // 更隐蔽的是换包/发错包 —— 下载链路全绿、zip 头正常，
 477:                         // 但里面是**旧版本**，系统安装器于是弹
 478:                         // "已安装相同版本"，用户永远升不上去。
 479:                         // 这个检查让那种情况变成一句明确的错误，而不是装完才发现。
 480:                         String apkVer = apkVersionName(out);
 481:                         if (apkVer == null) {
 482:                             throw new Exception("安装包读取失败（可能不是完整 APK）");
 483:                         }
 484:                         int apkCode = parseVer(apkVer);
 485:                         int myCode = parseVer(localVersionName());
 486:                         if (apkCode <= myCode) {
 487:                             throw new Exception("下载到的版本 v" + apkVer
 488:                                     + " 不高于本机 v" + localVersionName()
 489:                                     + "，已拒绝安装");
 490:                         }
 491:                         ui.post(new Runnable() {
 492:                             public void run() { pd.dismiss(); install(out); }
 493:                         });
 494:                         return;   // 成功，收工
 495:                     } catch (Exception e) {
 496:                         last = e;
 497:                         // 这个源不行，清掉半截文件换下一个 —— 不同源的包可能不同版本，
 498:                         // 混着续传会拼出一个既不是 A 也不是 B 的坏文件。
 499:                         out.delete();
 500:                     } finally {
 501:                         if (c != null) c.disconnect();
 502:                     }
 503:                 }
 504: 
 505:                 final String msg = last == null ? "没有可用下载源"
 506:                         : ("下载失败：" + last.getMessage());
 507:                 ui.post(new Runnable() {
 508:                     public void run() {
 509:                         pd.dismiss();
 510:                         toastOnUi(msg + "\n可在浏览器打开 app.xxjcloudns.kdns.fr 手动下载。");
 511:                     }
 512:                 });
 513:             }
 514:         }).start();
 515:     }
 516: 
 517:     /**
 518:      * 从一个源把 APK 拉到 out，支持断点续传，返回最终字节数。
 519:      *
 520:      * 关键点：
 521:      *   ① 手动跟 302（api.github.com 的资产端点必须这样，否则拿到的是 JSON 描述）
 522:      *   ② api.github.com 必须带 Accept: octet-stream，同上
 523:      *   ③ 若 out 已有部分内容且源支持 Range，就带 Range 续传，不让用户白等
 524:      */
 525:     private long fetchResumable(String start, File out, ProgressDialog pd) throws Exception {
 526:         long have = out.exists() ? out.length() : 0;
 527:         String target = start;
 528:         HttpURLConnection c = null;
 529: 
 530:         for (int hop = 0; hop < 6; hop++) {
 531:             c = (HttpURLConnection) new URL(target).openConnection();
 532:             c.setConnectTimeout(15000);
 533:             c.setReadTimeout(60000);
 534:             c.setInstanceFollowRedirects(false);
 535:             c.setRequestProperty("User-Agent", "zsb-plan");
 536:             if (target.contains("api.github.com")) {
 537:                 c.setRequestProperty("Accept", "application/octet-stream");
 538:                 c.setRequestProperty("X-GitHub-Api-Version", "2022-11-28");
 539:             }
 540:             if (have > 0) {
 541:                 c.setRequestProperty("Range", "bytes=" + have + "-");
 542:             }
 543:             int sc = c.getResponseCode();
 544:             if (sc == 301 || sc == 302 || sc == 303 || sc == 307 || sc == 308) {
 545:                 String loc = c.getHeaderField("Location");
 546:                 c.disconnect();
 547:                 if (loc == null) throw new Exception("重定向无地址");
 548:                 target = loc;
 549:                 continue;
 550:             }
 551:             break;
 552:         }
 553:         if (c == null) throw new Exception("连接失败");
 554: 
 555:         int sc = c.getResponseCode();
 556:         if (sc == 416) {
 557:             // 416 = 请求的区间超出文件总长度。
 558:             //
 559:             // 以前这里直接 return out.length()，把它当成"已经下完了"。
 560:             // 但真实场景是：本地留着**旧版本**的 update.apk，它的长度
 561:             // 大于服务端这个新文件的长度，于是永远 416，永远返回旧文件。
 562:             // 这是"更新后版本没变"的根因，必须重下而不是当成功。
 563:             out.delete();
 564:             throw new Exception("续传区间失效，已清除重试");
 565:         }
 566:         if (sc != 200 && sc != 206) throw new Exception("HTTP " + sc);
 567: 
 568:         // 206 = 续传成功；200 = 服务端不支持 Range，得从头写
 569:         boolean append = (sc == 206);
 570:         if (!append) have = 0;
 571: 
 572:         long total = have + c.getContentLength();
 573:         InputStream in = c.getInputStream();
 574:         FileOutputStream fo = new FileOutputStream(out, append);
 575:         byte[] buf = new byte[64 * 1024];
 576:         int n;
 577:         long done = have;
 578:         int lastPct = -1;
 579:         try {
 580:             while ((n = in.read(buf)) > 0) {
 581:                 fo.write(buf, 0, n);
 582:                 done += n;
 583:                 if (total > 0) {
 584:                     final int pct = (int) (done * 100L / total);
 585:                     if (pct != lastPct) {
 586:                         lastPct = pct;
 587:                         ui.post(new Runnable() {
 588:                             public void run() { pd.setMessage(pct + "%"); }
 589:                         });
 590:                     }
 591:                 }
 592:             }
 593:             fo.flush();
 594:         } finally {
 595:             try { fo.close(); } catch (Exception ignored) { }
 596:             try { in.close(); } catch (Exception ignored) { }
 597:             c.disconnect();
 598:         }
 599:         return out.length();
 600:     }
 601: 
 602:     private void install(File apk) {
 603:         try {
 604:             Intent i = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW);
 605:             Uri u;
 606:             if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 24) {
 607:                 // 7.0+ 禁止 file:// 跨应用，用自家 ApkProvider 提供 content://
 608:                 u = Uri.parse("content://" + ApkProvider.AUTHORITY + "/" + ApkProvider.NAME);
 609:                 i.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION);
 610:             } else {
 611:                 u = Uri.fromFile(apk);
 612:             }
 613:             i.setDataAndType(u, "application/vnd.android.package-archive");
 614:             i.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
 615:             act.startActivity(i);
 616:         } catch (Exception e) {
 617:             toastOnUi("打开安装器失败：" + e.getClass().getSimpleName()
 618:                     + "\n可在浏览器打开仓库手动下载。");
 619:         }
 620:     }
 621: 
 622:     private void toastOnUi(final String s) {
 623:         ui.post(new Runnable() {
 624:             public void run() {
 625:                 android.widget.Toast.makeText(act, s, android.widget.Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
 626:             }
 627:         });
 628:     }
 629: 
 630:     /**
 631:      * 头 4 字节是不是 zip 魔数 PK\x03\x04。
 632:      *
 633:      * APK 就是 zip。Cloudflare 出错时会返回 HTML 错误页（200 + 文本），
 634:      * 断流会留下半截文件 —— 两者长度都可能过 1MB 门槛，
 635:      * 但只有真正的 zip 开头才能被系统安装器接受。
 636:      */
 637:     /**
 638:      * 读 APK 里真实的 versionName。读不到返回 null。
 639:      *
 640:      * 用 PackageManager.getPackageArchiveInfo —— 这是系统自己解析
 641:      * AndroidManifest 的结果，跟安装器看到的是同一份数据，
 642:      * 不会被文件名或清单里的字段骗过去。
 643:      */
 644:     private String apkVersionName(File f) {
 645:         try {
 646:             android.content.pm.PackageManager pm = act.getPackageManager();
 647:             android.content.pm.PackageInfo pi = pm.getPackageArchiveInfo(
 648:                     f.getAbsolutePath(), 0);
 649:             if (pi == null || pi.versionName == null) return null;
 650:             return pi.versionName;
 651:         } catch (Exception e) {
 652:             return null;
 653:         }
 654:     }
 655: 
 656:     private boolean looksLikeApk(File f) {
 657:         if (f == null || !f.exists() || f.length() < 4) return false;
 658:         java.io.FileInputStream in = null;
 659:         try {
 660:             in = new java.io.FileInputStream(f);
 661:             byte[] h = new byte[4];
 662:             int n = 0;
 663:             while (n < 4) {
 664:                 int r = in.read(h, n, 4 - n);
 665:                 if (r <= 0) break;
 666:                 n += r;
 667:             }
 668:             return n == 4 && h[0] == 0x50 && h[1] == 0x4B
 669:                     && h[2] == 0x03 && h[3] == 0x04;
 670:         } catch (Exception e) {
 671:             return false;
 672:         } finally {
 673:             try { if (in != null) in.close(); } catch (Exception ignored) { }
 674:         }
 675:     }
 676: }
